### [1] [World In-Context Learning for Agentic Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12885)
*Heng Fang,Zehui Guo,Yuehan Zhang,Mingyang Du,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 World In-Context Learning（WICL）范式与 RoboScript 框架：冻结模型从单次演示出发，在仿真世界中排练任务，将世界反馈写入上下文而非网络权重，形成可冻结、可部署的 Task Memory，从而在新场景中实现无需梯度更新的机器人操作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练得到的动作策略会把操作技能固化到网络权重中，使所学内容与训练分布强耦合；而现有上下文学习（ICL）虽避免这种耦合，却因演示来自其他场景、缺乏机器人当前世界反馈而难以匹敌训练策略。作者观察到智能体在上下文中学习到的是语义信息（任务步骤与物体角色）而非视觉外观，因此一个世界中的经验可迁移到另一个世界。

Method: 提出 WICL：冻结模型以演示为起点，在仿真器等世界中排练任务，并将反馈写入上下文而非权重。构建模块化基础设施 RoboScript 实现面向智能体操作（agentic manipulation）的 WICL：从单个演示出发，通过仿真排练学习每个任务，将学习到的知识冻结为 Task Memory，并以数据隔离方式在新场景部署冻结记忆，确保每次部署输入在真实机器人上均可用。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 全部 50 个任务上，RoboScript 在干净与随机化设置下取得 83.4% 平均成功率；每任务仅用一次演示且无梯度更新，超过最强训练动作策略 20 个百分点以上。方法可跨视觉分布泛化、通过反思改进、随基础模型扩展，并可通过跨具身记忆适配迁移到真实双臂机器人。

Conclusion: 将“世界”纳入循环后，机器人可以在上下文中学习操作任务，并达到与在权重中学习相当的效果；代码已公开。

Abstract: Trained action policies write manipulation skill into network weights, tying what is learned to the distribution it is learned from. In-context learning (ICL) avoids this coupling but has not matched trained policies, because a demonstration recorded in another scene carries no feedback from the world the robot currently faces. We observe that what an agent learns in context is semantic, recording task steps and object roles rather than visual appearance, so experience gathered in one world transfers to another. Based on this insight, we propose World In-Context Learning (WICL), a paradigm in which a frozen model starts from a demonstration, rehearses the task in a world such as a simulator, and writes the feedback into its context rather than its weights. We build RoboScript, a modular infrastructure that implements WICL for agentic manipulation. From a single demonstration, RoboScript learns each task through rehearsal in simulation, freezes the learned knowledge as a Task Memory, and deploys the frozen memory on new scenes with data isolation that ensures every deployment input is available on a real robot. On all 50 tasks of RoboTwin 2.0, RoboScript reaches 83.4% mean success across clean and randomized settings with one demonstration per task and no gradient updates, exceeding the strongest trained action policy by over 20 points. The method generalizes across visual distributions, improves through reflection, scales with the foundation model, and transfers to a physical dual-arm robot through cross-embodiment memory adaptation. With a world in the loop, a robot can learn a manipulation task in its context as well as it learns one in its weights. All code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [2] [SWAM: Scaling World Action Models with Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12870)
*Yu Zhou,Xinyuan Xia,Xuankun Yang,Yinghao Yang,Wendi Chen,Bowen Liu,Jianhan Ma,Weicheng Duan,Jun Lv,Cewu Lu,Yanwei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究世界动作模型（WAM）在引入动作学习时如何保持语言-视频对齐，基于约4.7万小时数据系统分析监督形式、数据混合、训练配方与模型架构，并给出在仿真和真实任务上泛化更强的训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM研究主要关注架构和训练机制，通常默认视频预训练获得的语言-视频对齐是稳固基础；但动作学习可能损害该对齐，且缺乏关于数据和监督设计以维持语言-视频对齐的原则性指导。

Method: 构建约47,000小时数据池，涵盖通用视频、第一视角视频、UMI演示和机器人演示；系统比较语言-视频监督、语言-视频-动作监督及二者联合使用对WAM性能的影响；分析语言标注质量对预训练语言-视频能力的影响，以及UMI和机器人演示中的动作监督如何将能力扩展到语言-视频-动作建模；进一步研究联合训练配方和模型架构，并在固定训练预算下混合第一视角视频与UMI演示、扩展预训练。

Result: 固定训练预算下，混合第一视角视频和UMI演示提升控制泛化；模型架构影响经验利用效率，扩展预训练带来进一步增益。所得配方仅在RoboTwin Clean演示上后训练，在Easy（Clean-to-Clean）和Hard（Clean-to-Random）条件下分别达到88.10%和61.56%成功率；在三个真实世界任务上平均成功率为40.00%，最强基线为23.33%，显示未见条件下更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 语言-视频监督、动作监督及数据混合方式对WAM维持语言-视频对齐和提升动作泛化至关重要；所提训练配方在仿真与真实场景均优于基线，并开源整理数据、训练代码和模型权重。

Abstract: World action models jointly predict video and actions conditioned on language. Existing studies focus primarily on architectures and training mechanisms, typically treating language-video alignment acquired through video pretraining as an established foundation. However, introducing action learning may impair this alignment. Principled guidance on data and supervision design for maintaining language-video alignment during action learning remains limited. To investigate this, we curate a pool of approximately 47,000 hours of general videos, egocentric videos, UMI demonstrations, and robot demonstrations. Building on this, we systematically study how language-video supervision, language-video-action supervision, and the joint use of these two forms affect WAM performance. In particular, we examine how language annotation quality affects pretrained language-video capabilities, and how action supervision from UMI and robot demonstrations extends these capabilities to language-video-action modeling. To better scale the model, we further conduct detailed studies on joint training recipe and model architecture. Under a fixed training budget, mixing egocentric videos and UMI demonstrations improves control generalization. Model architecture affects how effectively these experiences are used, while scaling pretraining under the selected configuration brings further gains. The resulting recipe, post-trained on RoboTwin Clean demonstrations only, achieves success rates of 88.10% and 61.56% under Easy (Clean-to-Clean) and Hard (Clean-to-Random) conditions, respectively. Our model achieves 40.00% SR across three real-world tasks, compared with 23.33% for the strongest baseline, indicating better generalization under unseen conditions. We release the curated data, training code, and model weights.

</details>


### [3] [EgoLabel: Zero-Shot Egocentric Auto-Labeling with Cross-Modal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12149)
*Xiaozhi Zhang,Changjia Xu,Yaoyuan Yan,Haoxiang Jie,Bingjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoLabel 是一个零样本、跨模态配准的自标注流水线，用现成模型完成几何、物体与手部估计并给出一致性诊断，在 H2O 上验证了手-深度锚定和相机轨迹精度，同时揭示绝对精度与内部一致性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角自动标注需要深度、相机位姿、物体和人体姿态估计在独立开发的模型间保持一致；但单位、坐标约定和尺度差异会使单模型看似合理的预测在几何上不一致。

Method: 提出 EgoLabel 零样本流水线，无需目标域标注训练，集成 MonST3R 做几何、LocateAnything 结合 GMC-BoT-SORT 与 SAM 2 做物体、V-HPOT 做手部，并用 FisheyeViT 做全身兼容性演示；提供显式跨模态配准和一致性诊断。手部主评估使用 H2O 传感器深度，比较逐手深度锚定与单一全局尺度。

Result: 在 20 个序列上，逐手深度锚定将每序列手-深度残差中位数的均值相对单一全局尺度降低 23%，汇总中位数为 84.6 mm，而真值参考为 20.5 mm；手部 PA-MPJPE 为 14.53±1.29 mm，绝对 MPJPE 为 119.1±31.3 mm；三个纯 RGB 序列经 Sim(3) 对齐后相机 ATE 为 1.40±0.29 cm。还发现滑动窗口实现问题会使传感器初始化深度在优化中改变；由于手部锚定与残差共享同一深度图，残差衡量的是内部一致性而非独立精度。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了该配准流水线的实用性与当前局限：跨模态配准和一致性诊断有效，但对齐精度与度量精度仍有显著差距，残差不能直接解释为独立准确度。

Abstract: Egocentric auto-labeling requires depth, camera pose, object, and human-pose estimates to agree across independently developed models. Differences in units, coordinate conventions, and scale can make individually plausible predictions geometrically inconsistent. We present \textbf{EgoLabel}, a zero-shot pipeline that combines off-the-shelf models without target-domain labeled training and provides explicit cross-modal registration and consistency diagnostics. The pipeline integrates MonST3R for geometry, LocateAnything with GMC-BoT-SORT and SAM~2 for objects, and V-HPOT for hands; FisheyeViT provides a separate whole-body compatibility demonstration. Our main hand evaluation uses H2O sensor depth. Across 20 sequences, per-hand depth anchoring reduces the mean of per-sequence median hand--depth residuals by $23\%$ relative to a single global scale, yielding a pooled median of $84.6\,\mathrm{mm}$ against a $20.5\,\mathrm{mm}$ ground-truth reference. Hand PA-MPJPE is $14.53\pm1.29\,\mathrm{mm}$, whereas absolute MPJPE is $119.1\pm31.3\,\mathrm{mm}$, revealing a substantial gap between aligned and metric accuracy. On three RGB-only sequences, camera ATE is $1.40\pm0.29\,\mathrm{cm}$ after $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ alignment. We also identify a sliding-window implementation issue that allows sensor-initialized depth to change during optimization. Because hand anchoring and its residual share the same depth map, the residual measures internal consistency rather than independent accuracy. These results characterize both the utility and the current limits of the registration pipeline.

</details>


### [4] [Compass 1: Towards Open-World and Persistent Embodied Navigation from Monocular RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12087)
*Hai Zhang,Siqi Liang,Yanjun Guo,Shoufeng Zhang,Yunhao Wang,Hangyu Du,Haoguang Mai,Hongchen Li,Jiazhi Yang,Jialong Zeng,Yu Hu,Chen Chen,Gangcheng Jiang,Zishun Zhuo,Lirui Zhao,Zhigang Li,Yu Qiao,Zhenjie Yang,Fu Zhang,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出具身导航基础模型 Compass 1，统一语言理解与视频生成，并通过历史表示与历史利用实现近2分钟视觉历史回溯和长时程稳定导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航架构难以同时维持持久空间感知与长时程执行稳定性，通常仅能有效利用最近少量帧，缺乏长期时空上下文与历史回溯能力。

Method: 在不改变底层架构的情况下引入 History Representation：融合稀疏视觉关键帧与显式 History Representation Map 以保留长期时空上下文；并提出 History Utilization，即结构化 VQA 监督协议，包含任务阶段 grounding、探索一致性和时空目标检索三类任务，以支持地图 grounded 推理和稳定长时程导航。同时建立单目 RGB 视频标注流水线，开源超过500小时真实交互与2000小时标注网络视频数据集。

Result: Compass 1 主要在1米高度相机分布上训练，可泛化至0.35米轮式机器人、0.5米四足机器人、0.85米人形机器人和1.5米无人机等不同具身与场景。开放世界评测总体成功率达79.4%，最强基线为17.8%；基础任务零样本执行超过90%，探索提升250%，困难持久历史回溯从0%提升至58%。

Conclusion: Compass 1 通过长期历史表示与结构化历史利用监督，实现了跨具身、跨场景的持久空间感知与长时程导航，显著优于现有基线，尤其在零样本执行、探索和历史回溯方面表现突出。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires agents to achieve persistent spatial awareness and long-horizon execution stability in open-world environments. However, existing navigation architectures struggle to sustain both capabilities, remaining effective only over a handful of recent frames. We present Compass 1, the first embodied navigation foundation model to unify language understanding and video generation while enabling memory backtracking through nearly 2mins of visual history. Without altering the underlying architecture, we introduce History Representation, which integrates sparse visual keyframes with an explicit History Representation Map to preserve long-term spatiotemporal context. To effectively leverage this history, we further introduce History Utilization, a structured VQA supervision protocol comprising three categories: task-stage grounding, exploration consistency, and spatiotemporal target retrieval, enabling map-grounded reasoning and stable persistent long-horizon navigation. To fuel this system, we establish a complete annotation pipeline for monocular RGB videos and open-source a massive dataset featuring over 500 hours of high-quality real-world interactions and over 2,000 hours of annotated web videos. Trained mainly on the distribution of 1m height camera, Compass 1 generalizes seamlessly across diverse embodiments and scenarios, ranging from indoor environments to pedestrian-dense streets, spanning wheeled robot at 0.35m, quadruped dog at 0.5m, humanoid at 0.85m, and aerial drone at 1.5m. Compass 1achieves a dominant overall success rate of 79.4% across open-world evaluations, compared to 17.8% for the strongest baseline. It demonstrates exceptional zero-shot execution exceeding 90% on foundational tasks, a 250% boost in exploration, and an unparalleled leap from 0% to 58% in challenging persistent history backtracking.

</details>


### [5] [Scale-Consistent Monocular Fisheye Visual Odometry via Spherical Bundle Adjustment-Guided Unsupervised Depth Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11934)
*Wanhui Luo,Hongbo Zhao,Yi Zhou,Qijun Chen,Yiming Nie,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S²D-FVO：一种融合球面光束法平差（SBA）与无监督深度学习的单目鱼眼视觉里程计框架，用以缓解长轨迹尺度漂移并提升深度估计与轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目鱼眼VO因大视场和简单配置适用于机器人定位与建图，但长轨迹尺度漂移严重；无监督深度学习可缓解该问题，然而鱼眼图像强畸变使深度估计困难，且像素重投影残差不能均匀反映视线角偏差，标量角残差的一阶线性化对某一切向扰动不敏感。

Method: SBA采用由球面对数映射导出的切空间角重投影残差，使其在两个切向均保持敏感性且范数等于对应标量角残差；引入基于中值深度的尺度正则项约束重建尺度。无监督深度学习中，SBA替代位姿估计网络为视图合成提供更可靠相对位姿；训练后的深度网络反过来为SBA提供尺度参考，提升尺度一致性并细化三维路标位置。

Result: 在多个公开数据集及两个新采集的大规模数据集上实验表明，该框架在深度和轨迹估计精度上优于代表性现有方法，并适用于不同相机模型。

Conclusion: S²D-FVO通过SBA引导的无监督深度学习和尺度正则化，有效改善单目鱼眼VO的尺度一致性与深度估计精度，具有跨相机模型的适用性。

Abstract: The wide field of view of fisheye cameras and the simplicity of a monocular setup make monocular fisheye visual odometry (VO) attractive for robotic localization and mapping. Nevertheless, scale drift remains a major challenge over long trajectories. Unsupervised depth learning can help to mitigate this issue, but the pronounced distortion of fisheye images poses challenges for accurate depth estimation. This article presents S$^2$D-FVO, a monocular fisheye VO framework integrating spherical bundle adjustment (SBA)-guided unsupervised depth learning to improve scale consistency and depth estimation accuracy. Given that pixel reprojection residuals do not uniformly reflect angular deviations between viewing rays and that the first-order linearization of a scalar angular reprojection residual is insensitive to perturbations along one tangent direction, SBA employs tangent-space angular reprojection residuals derived from the spherical logarithmic map. These residuals retain sensitivity along both tangent directions, with their norms equal to the corresponding scalar angular residuals. A median depth-based scale regularization term is further integrated into SBA to constrain the reconstruction scale. Building on these formulations, SBA replaces the pose estimation network during unsupervised depth learning, providing more reliable relative poses for view synthesis. In turn, depth estimates from the well-trained depth estimation network provide scale references for SBA, improving scale consistency while allowing individual 3-D landmark positions to be refined. Extensive experiments conducted on multiple public datasets, as well as two newly collected large-scale datasets, demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms representative existing approaches in both depth and trajectory estimation accuracy and is applicable to different camera models.

</details>


### [6] [SurgTAP-3D: A Large-Scale Cross-Surgery Video Dataset with Contact-Aware 3D Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11697)
*Jiaxin Guo,Wenzhen Dong,Taoyu Wu,Haomin Kuang,Mengya Xu,Kejian Shi,Ziyi Wang,Tongfan Guan,Mingkai Jia,Yue Wang,Qi Dou,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模体内伪3D手术运动跟踪数据集与基准 SurgTAP-3D，并提出统一的4D标注框架，支持器械与组织的3D点跟踪、重建及手术动作理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数据集在真实临床场景中的规模与多样性有限，且标注多仅关注组织或器械，忽视工具-组织交互，难以支撑增强现实导航、VLA模型和世界模型所需的大规模高质量3D监督。

Method: 提出统一手术3D点跟踪标注框架，将未标定单目手术视频转换为结构化4D标注，包含接触区域提示、活动工具分割、动态光束法平差和全局4D refinement；构建包含520K帧、4,549个动作片段和1.4M条2D/3D点轨迹的SurgTAP-3D，覆盖机器人及手动手术并统一动作类别。

Result: 在SurgPose、SurgT和STIR上建立涵盖器械与组织跟踪的综合基准，验证器械位移估计、可变形表面重建和基础模型微调；数据涵盖遮挡、烟雾、出血和快速器械运动等真实手术挑战，实验证明数据集与标注框架有效。

Conclusion: SurgTAP-3D为3D手术点跟踪、重建和手术动作理解提供了可扩展的数据与基准支撑，有助于推动物理智能和自主手术系统发展。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scene dynamics in minimally invasive surgery (MIS) is a fundamental step toward physically intelligent and autonomous surgical systems. However, existing public datasets offer limited scalability and diversity in real-world clinical scenarios. Moreover, annotations typically focus on either tissue or instruments alone, overlooking tool-tissue interactions. This gap limits applications such as augmented reality guidance and autonomous surgery driven by vision-language-action (VLA) models or world models, which demand large-scale, high-quality 3D supervision. In this work, we construct SurgTAP-3D, a large-scale in-vivo pseudo-3D surgical motion tracking dataset and benchmark, comprising 520K frames, 4,549 action clips, and 1.4M 2D/3D point trajectories from diverse robotic and manual procedures under unified action categories. To make annotation scalable, we design a unified framework for surgical 3D point tracking that converts uncalibrated monocular surgical videos into structured 4D annotations through contact-region prompting, active-tool segmentation, dynamic bundle adjustment, and global 4D refinement. This pipeline adapts foundation priors to surgical videos and produces temporally consistent trajectories, pseudo-scale geometry, camera poses, and instrument masks. The dataset captures realistic challenges inherent to surgical environments, including occlusions, smoke, bleeding, and rapid instrument motion. We establish a comprehensive benchmark on SurgPose, SurgT, and STIR, covering both instrument and tissue tracking. We demonstrate instrument displacement estimation, deformable surface reconstruction, and foundation-model fine-tuning. Extensive experiments and analysis demonstrate the effectiveness of our dataset and annotation framework for 3D surgical point tracking, reconstruction, and surgical action understanding. Homepage: this https URL

</details>


### [7] [ForeAct: Geometry-Guided Action Learning from World Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11500)
*Wei Lu,Jiaxi Zhang,Yunheng Wang,Taowen Wang,Yuetong Fang,Zhenhui Zhang,Shuning Zhang,Zizhao Yuan,Yixiao Feng,Zecui Zeng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeAct 将预训练视频世界模型重用为单次前向传播的表示骨干，并通过下一帧相对深度监督的可学习 token 注入未来几何感知，实现低延迟、几何感知的机器人操作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时建模场景几何及其在交互中的演化：world action models 依赖迭代扩散/rectified-flow 推理，计算成本高且表示缺乏显式空间结构；显式几何建模常需辅助模块，增加参数与计算；几何中心策略多只恢复观测空间结构，缺乏对未来几何变化的显式监督。

Method: ForeAct 直接对当前观测的干净 latent 做一次前向传播，免去未来帧合成与迭代去噪，同时保留视频世界模型的时空先验。它不引入独立几何骨干，而是用由下一帧相对深度监督的可学习 token，通过双向自注意力塑造共享视频表示，将骨干中已编码的空间知识转化为面向动作生成的未来感知表示。轻量几何读出仅在训练时使用，部署时移除，因此无需深度输入或外部几何骨干即可实现低延迟、几何感知控制。

Result: 无需额外大规模预训练，ForeAct 在 LIBERO 上取得 96.6% 平均成功率，在 SimplerEnv-Bridge 上取得 63.5%，并在 90 次真实世界 rollout 中取得 74.4% 成功率。

Conclusion: ForeAct 通过重用视频世界模型并以未来相对深度监督可学习 token，在较小参数开销下统一了几何感知与未来动态建模，实现了低延迟、无需深度输入和外部几何骨干的有效机器人操作控制。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires representations that capture both scene geometry and its evolution during interaction. Existing approaches have largely treated these two aspects separately. World action models capture such dynamics by coupling action prediction with future video generation, but their reliance on iterative diffusion or rectified-flow inference leads to high computational cost, while their representations lack explicit spatial structure. Explicitly modeling scene geometry typically requires auxiliary modules that increase both parameter count and computational cost. Conversely, geometry-centric policies primarily recover observed spatial structure and often lack explicit supervision for anticipating future geometric changes during interaction. To address these limitations, we introduce ForeAct, which repurposes a pretrained video world model as a single-pass representation backbone for low-latency control. ForeAct directly processes the clean latent of the current observation in one forward pass, eliminating future-frame synthesis and iterative denoising while retaining the backbone's pretrained spatiotemporal priors. Rather than introducing a separate geometry backbone, ForeAct uses learnable tokens supervised by next-frame relative depth to shape the shared video representation through bidirectional self-attention. This compact interface turns the spatial knowledge already encoded in the backbone into a future-aware representation for action generation, with little parameter overhead. The lightweight geometry readout is used only during training and removed at deployment, enabling low-latency, geometry-aware control without depth input or an external geometry backbone. Without additional large-scale pretraining, ForeAct achieves 96.6% average success on LIBERO, 63.5% on SimplerEnv-Bridge, and a 74.4% success rate across 90 real-world rollouts.

</details>


### [8] [CrossLiDAR: Controllable Multi-Sensor LiDAR Sequence Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11131)
*Khanh Nguyen,Thomas Walker,Hichem Telli,Carlos de la Torre-Ortiz,Stefano Fiorini,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Wassim Swaileh,Tinghuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossLiDAR 是一个基于 Transformer 的统一框架，可在紧凑距离图像潜空间中生成可控且时空一致的多传感器 LiDAR 序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真 LiDAR 仿真是自动驾驶系统规模化开发与验证的瓶颈；现有生成器通常仅支持单一传感器配置，对结构与几何的控制有限，且长时程 4D 生成易出现漂移。

Method: 采用 rectified flow 目标与 Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformer（MM-DiT）骨干，在距离图像潜空间中建模；引入 Inter-sensor Module 实现跨传感器交互，并用 ControlNet 风格分支提供 BEV 先验或 3D 边界框等结构条件；通过生成短 N 帧序列并自回归扩展提升长时程一致性。

Result: 在 nuScenes 和 Waymo 数据集上显著优于现有基线，JSD 平均提升 29.56%，MMD 平均提升 15.36%。

Conclusion: CrossLiDAR 支持标定一致的多传感器 LiDAR 长时程推演，服务于可控的 scenario-based 测试，缩小了与先进自动驾驶仿真之间的差距。

Abstract: High-fidelity LiDAR simulation remains a bottleneck for scalable development and validation of autonomous systems. Existing generators can synthesize realistic scans, but they are typically constrained to a single sensor configuration, offer limited control over structure and geometry, and drift in long-horizon 4D generation. We present CrossLiDAR, a unified transformer-based framework capable of generating controllable and spatio-temporally consistent multi-sensor LiDAR sequences. Our approach leverages a rectified flow objective with a Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformer (MM-DiT) backbone, within a compact latent space of range images. To support multiple LiDAR sensors, an Inter-sensor Module enables interaction across sensors, while a ControlNet-style branch provides structural conditioning (e.g., BEV priors or 3D bounding boxes). For modeling the temporal consistency, CrossLiDAR generates short $N$-frame sequences and extends them autoregressively to improve long-horizon coherence. Our model significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an average improvement of 29.56\% in JSD and 15.36\% in MMD evaluated on nuScenes and the Waymo Dataset. CrossLiDAR bridges the gap to modern autonomous-driving simulation by enabling calibration-consistent multi-sensor LiDAR rollouts over long horizons for controllable, scenario-based testing.

</details>


### [9] [SoftPAT3D: Physics-Augmented Text-to-3D Scene Generation with Deformables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11087)
*Lei Shu,Alberta Longhini,Leonidas Guibas,Gordon Wetzstein,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SoftPAT3D 是一个物理增强的文本到3D场景生成框架，可将布料、毛绒玩具、绳索等可变形物体生成为物理有效、可直接仿真的刚/柔体，并与支撑家具、其他物体及自身保持无穿透接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理增强流程仅支持刚体，无法处理文本到3D生成的可变形物体网格：这些网格几何无效、可能自交，且缺少静息形状、材料模型和可接受的初始状态，而这些正是可变形仿真的必要输入。

Method: 该方法从制造方式而非生成的不一致变形构型构造静息形状；用材料库为每个可变形物体赋予初始弹性参数并支持优化；重建被遮挡支撑并转为水密碰撞网格；将物体从可优化放置位置释放到提示指定的支撑部件上方；在统一接触模型下驱动整个场景到静态平衡，并用几何检查和视觉语言模型验证完成度。失败时用放置网格搜索修正；利用同一接触能量可微仿真的伴随方法实现网格搜索达不到的目标、联合放置多个物体并恢复优化材料参数。

Result: 在多体场景中可单次仿真达到平衡，也可仅由文本提示运行；在全部65个提示上，SoftPAT3D均未产生任何非预期互穿，而其他系统均出现该问题；生成场景可直接支持场景编辑和机器人操作。

Conclusion: SoftPAT3D使文本到3D生成的可变形物体场景具备物理有效性、仿真就绪性和无穿透接触，并可扩展到多体、仅文本输入、编辑与机器人操作等应用。

Abstract: We present SoftPAT3D, a physics-augmented framework for text-to-3D scene generation in which deformable objects, such as cloth, stuffed toys and ropes, are generated as physically valid, simulation-ready bodies and placed in intersection-free contact with the furniture that supports them, with one another and with themselves. Existing physics-augmented pipelines simulate rigid bodies only and cannot accommodate a generated deformable object, which text-to-3D generation returns as a geometrically invalid mesh, sometimes self-intersecting, that has no rest shape, no material model and no admissible initial state. These are the inputs a deformable simulation requires, and we construct each from information already in the pipeline. The rest shape comes from how the object is manufactured rather than from the generated physically inconsistent deformed configuration, and a material library assigns each deformable object its initial elastic parameters, which can then be optimized. The occluded support is regenerated and converted into a watertight collision mesh, each object is released from an optimizable placement above the supporting part that the prompt specifies, and the complete scene is driven to static equilibrium under one unified contact model, with completion verified by geometric checks and a vision-language model. When a scene fails the prompt, a grid search over placements corrects it, and the adjoint of a differentiable simulation of the same contact energy reaches the targets the grid does not, places several bodies jointly and recovers and optimizes material parameters. Multi-body scenes equilibrate in a single simulation, and the pipeline also runs from the text prompt alone. On all sixty-five prompts, SoftPAT3D produces no undesired interpenetration of any kind, whereas every other system does, and its scenes directly support scene editing and robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [10] [Locating and Repairing Domain Shift in VLM Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10720)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Michael Alexander Riegler,Guandong Xu,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出VLM端到端驾驶规划器的跨城市域偏移在模态与层维度上具有结构化规律，提出先定位后修复的MoLaRx框架，在多种VLM和跨城市基准上以更少可训练参数和更低推理延迟取得最小轨迹误差差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨城市域偏移方法通常将其视为单一整体，通过统一对齐或单点编辑处理；作者认为这种观点不足，因为VLM轨迹规划器中的域偏移在图像/文本模态和网络层上呈结构化分布，且与结构匹配的定位-修复策略应优于统一目标基线。

Method: 作者通过activation patching分析发现：图像与文本token的偏移模式不同，层间偏移划分依赖架构但在数据集上稳定，从而将域偏移建模为模态-层二维张量。基于此提出MoLaRx：用Causal Importance Score估计模态-层域偏移张量并将层划分为功能区域，再由CIS-Guided Selective Repair为各区域匹配MMD、LoRA或冻结等修复算子，组合可随架构自动适配。

Result: 在LFM2-VL-1.6B、Qwen2-VL-2B和PaliGemma2-3B三个架构不同的VLM上，于nuScenes和Argoverse 2两个跨城市基准的六个主表设置中，MoLaRx均取得最低跨城市轨迹误差差距，同时可训练参数减少50-60%，推理延迟降低8.4-10.9 ms；而统一MMD-only适配在六个设置中均崩溃。

Conclusion: 跨城市域偏移应被视为模态-层结构化张量并进行选择性修复；结构引导的定位-修复优于统一对齐，验证了MoLaRx的有效性与效率，并强调结构化修复对VLM驾驶规划器跨域适配的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly adopted as end-to-end driving planners, yet they suffer from cross-city domain shift. Existing methods treat this shift as a single quantity addressed by uniform alignment or single-site editing. We challenge this view on two counts: (i) domain shift in VLM trajectory planners is inherently structured across modality and layer, and (ii) a locate-then-repair recipe matched to this structure outperforms every uniform-objective baseline. We analyse domain shift via activation patching and uncover two regularities. (F1) Modality axis. Image and text tokens exhibit distinct shift patterns in each VLM. (F2) Layer axis. The layer-wise shift partition is architecture-dependent and dataset-stable. These findings recast domain shift as a two-dimensional tensor indexed by modality (image vs. text tokens) and layer, motivating a simple principle: locate where shift occurs, and repair only there. We instantiate this principle in MoLaRx (Modality-Layer Repair), a locate-then-repair framework. The Causal Importance Score (CIS) estimates the domain shift as a modality-layer tensor and partitions the layers into functional zones. CIS-Guided Selective Repair (CISR) then applies a matched operator (Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) / low-rank adaptation (LoRA) / freezing) to repair each zone, with the composition adapting automatically to each architecture. We evaluate MoLaRx on three architecturally distinct VLMs (LFM2-VL-1.6B, Qwen2-VL-2B, and PaliGemma2-3B) across two cross-city benchmarks (nuScenes and Argoverse 2). MoLaRx attains the lowest cross-city trajectory-error gap among adaptation methods in all six main-table settings, while using 50-60% fewer trainable parameters and 8.4-10.9 ms lower inference latency. In contrast, uniform MMD-only adaptation collapses in all six settings, highlighting the importance of structure-guided repair.

</details>


### [11] [PLS-GS: Probabilistic Lidar Scaffolds for Compact, Surface-Aligned Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10699)
*Krishnan Harikumar,Alejandro Fontan,Thierry Peynot,Emili Hernández,Niko Sünderhauf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLS-GS 利用相机与高精度 LiDAR 数据构建概率化 LiDAR 脚手架，引导 3DGS 生成更紧凑且贴合测量表面的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 3DGS 仅从图像优化，而机器人与建图应用常同时提供相机和 LiDAR 数据，可结合丰富外观与准确几何结构；目标是利用这种互补感知构建紧凑、表面对齐的 3DGS。

Method: 提出 PLS-GS，对位置和颜色联合拟合分层高斯混合模型（GMM），形成连续自适应脚手架，避免体素或八叉树离散划分；每个渲染高斯关联一个 GMM 分量，并在其协方差诱导的局部表面坐标系中约束；使用 Mahalanobis support loss 限制渲染高斯相对 LiDAR 表面的偏差。

Result: 在五个大型室外场景中，PLS-GS 相比图像初始化和 LiDAR 初始化基线，使图元显著更接近 LiDAR 测量，同时使用更少图元，模型更紧凑且渲染更快；在相同容量下，图元更贴近 LiDAR 测量且渲染质量相当。

Conclusion: PLS-GS 能有效融合 LiDAR 几何先验与图像信息，构建紧凑、表面贴合且高效的 3DGS 表示。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is typically optimized from images only, many robotics and mapping applications provide both camera and high-definition LiDAR data, combining rich visual appearance with accurate geometric structure. We introduce PLS-GS, a probabilistic LiDAR scaffold for Gaussian Splatting that exploits this complementary sensing setting to construct a compact 3DGS representation aligned to the measured surface. PLS-GS fits a Hierarchical Gaussian Mixture Model jointly over position and color, yielding a continuous and adaptive scaffold that eliminates the need for discrete scene partitioning such as voxel grids or octrees. The fitted mixture then guides the renderable 3DGS Gaussians: each rendering Gaussian is associated with a GMM component and constrained in the local surface frame induced by that component's covariance. A Mahalanobis support loss bounds the deviation of the rendering Gaussians from the corresponding LiDAR-derived surface. Across five large outdoor scenes, PLS-GS places primitives markedly closer to the LiDAR measurements than both image- and LiDAR-initialized baselines, while employing substantially fewer primitives, yielding a considerably more compact model and faster rendering. At matched capacity, PLS-GS places primitives closer to the LiDAR measurements, with comparable rendering quality. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [12] [Dynamics-Informed Action Learning for Goal-Conditioned Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10660)
*Yinuo Jiang,Guangzhi Wang,Yiran Wang,Junfu Pu,Cheng Cheng,Honghao Chen,Cheng Cheng,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DIAL通过训练时的rollout对齐与多步逆动力学监督，并在测试时用逆/前向动力学自回归生成动作序列来初始化CEM，从而提升潜在世界模型在视觉控制中的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型存在两个差距：教师强制训练未让动力学模型接触规划时递归预测的潜在状态；基于采样的规划器通常从与目标无关的提议初始化动作搜索，未利用世界模型学到的动作条件动力学。

Method: DIAL在训练中引入rollout对齐，使前向模型暴露于自身自回归潜在预测；用多步逆监督训练轻量逆动力学头从潜在状态转移推断动作；并将固定参数的逆头应用于模型生成转移，促使预测rollout保留动作相关信息。测试时，DIAL自回归耦合逆动力学与前向动力学，构造面向目标的动力学知情动作序列，用于初始化CEM提议分布的均值，再由标准CEM采样与更新细化。

Result: 在四个视觉控制基准上，DIAL在标准与长时程场景中均优于现有潜在世界模型和规划基线。

Conclusion: DIAL弥合了潜在动力学学习与规划使用之间的差距，通过动力学知情动作初始化与CEM细化实现了更强的视觉控制规划表现。

Abstract: Latent world models enable visual control by planning action sequences in compact representation spaces, but a gap remains between how latent dynamics are learned and how they are used for planning. First, teacher-forced training does not expose the dynamics model to the recursively predicted latent states encountered during planning. Second, sampling-based planners typically initialize action search from goal-agnostic proposals, without exploiting the action-conditioned dynamics learned by the world model. We introduce Dynamics-Informed Action Learning (DIAL) to bridge these two gaps. During training, rollout alignment exposes the forward model to its own autoregressive latent predictions, while multi-step inverse supervision trains a lightweight inverse dynamics head to infer actions from latent-state transitions. We further apply the inverse head to model-generated transitions with its parameters held fixed, encouraging predicted rollouts to retain action-relevant information. At test time, DIAL autoregressively couples the inverse and forward dynamics to construct a dynamics-informed action sequence toward the goal, which initializes the mean of the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) proposal distribution. The subsequent CEM search then refines this initialization using the standard sampling and updating procedure. Experiments across four visual control benchmarks demonstrate that DIAL outperforms existing latent world models and planning baselines in both standard and long-horizon scenarios.

</details>


### [13] [How Many Demonstrations Does a VLA Need? On the Sample Efficiency of VLA Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09964)
*Rahul Chand,Miguel Villa Floran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统评估六种预训练VLA模型在LIBERO四个套件上的适应效率，揭示全数据微调所掩盖的样本效率差异，并分析冻结VLM对微调的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA比较多基于零样本或全数据微调成功率，难以反映实际部署前适应目标场景所需的数据量与计算量。

Method: 在四个LIBERO套件上，对π0、π0.5及GR00T N1-N1.7变化演示数量与训练步数进行微调评估；并通过冻结VLM分析预训练方式对适应效率的影响。

Result: 全数据微调下模型差异被掩盖：LIBERO-Spatial上50条演示时成功率差距可达3倍；π0.5在各预算下样本效率最高，比π0少需2–5倍演示，长时程套件差距最大；GR00T中N1.5在四个套件中的三个上比N1.6/N1.7适应更高效，尽管后者零样本更强。冻结VLM严重降低π模型性能，但几乎不影响GR00T。验证损失在同套件内与闭环成功率相关，却低估π0.5相对π0的样本效率优势。

Conclusion: VLA适应效率需作为独立维度评估；样本效率差异与预训练中VLM是否联合训练等因素相关，验证损失不能完全替代闭环成功率与数据效率分析。

Abstract: Pretrained vision language action (VLA) models show strong zero-shot performance, but are rarely deployed directly and are often first post-trained on demonstrations from the target setting. Yet VLAs are typically compared by zero-shot or full-data fine-tuned success, revealing little about the data and compute required for adaptation. We study adaptation efficiency for six VLAs ($\pi_{0}$, $\pi_{0.5}$, and GR00T N1-N1.7) on four LIBERO suites, varying both demonstrations and training steps. This reveals differences hidden by full-data fine-tuning: models within two points on the full LIBERO-Spatial dataset differ by up to $3\times$ in success with 50 demonstrations. $\pi_{0.5}$ needs $2$--$5\times$ fewer demonstrations than $\pi_{0}$ and is the most sample-efficient model at every budget, with the largest gap on the long-horizon suite. Within GR00T, N1.5 adapts more efficiently than N1.6 and N1.7 on three of four suites, despite their stronger zero-shot performance. We study one pretraining factor behind these differences by freezing the VLM during finetuning: this severely degrades the $\pi$ models, whose VLM is jointly trained with the action expert during pretraining, but barely affects GR00T models. Finally, validation loss on held-out demonstrations tracks closed-loop success within a suite, but understates how much more sample-efficient $\pi_{0.5}$ is than $\pi_{0}$.

</details>

### [14] [Same Physics, Different Gradients: A State-of-the-Art Report on Differentiable Physics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12162)
*Lei Shu,Zizhou Huang,Yunuo Chen,Yifei Li,Tao Du,Minchen Li,Tuur Stuyck,Miles Macklin,Kui Wu,Bo Zhu,Yin Yang,Ming C. Lin,Alberta Longhini,Rika Antonova,Leonidas Guibas,Gordon Wetzstein,Jeannette Bohg,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该摘要提出用“指涉对象（referent）”与“实现方式（realization）”两轴区分可微物理仿真中的导数方法，并补充接触非光滑性的正交分类；进而讨论精确梯度是否有用、零阶方法何时优于仿真梯度，并给出报告清单与诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微仿真将动画、系统辨识、控制和计算设计转化为基于梯度的优化，但现有引擎和方法在“对哪个数学对象求导”以及“反向传播如何组织”上被文献混为一谈，导致精确性、内存成本和适用性主张难以比较。

Method: 作者分离两个问题：指涉对象包括实际执行的有限计算、所近似的收敛方程、连续动力学；实现方式包括自动微分、方程级伴随求解和自定义伴随。成本还取决于反向传播所需状态的保留或恢复方式。方法按两轴归类，并用第二个正交分类处理接触导致的非光滑映射；随后归纳四类“有用梯度丢失”机制，在统一测试平台上测量，并综述零阶方法与仿真梯度的证据。

Result: 交叉两轴可将已分类的方法和引擎放入同一框架，区分文献中合并的主张，并表明精确性与内存之间的表面权衡来自方法构建方式而非本质约束。测试平台显示，精确导数相对方程级伴随的优势取决于拟合目标来源和求解器预算；在四类机制起作用的情形下，零阶方法可能优于仿真梯度。

Conclusion: 论文通过该视角综述模型类别、系统技术与应用，并提出报告检查清单、已发布的诊断工具以及后续研究议程，以规范可微仿真的导数语义、精确性评估和成本报告。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation propagates derivatives through a physics simulator, turning animation, system identification, control, and computational design into gradient-based optimization. Engines differ in the physics they model and, less visibly, in which mathematical object they differentiate. We separate two questions the literature conflates. The referent is which object the returned derivative belongs to: the finite computation that executed, the converged equation it approximates, or the continuous dynamics. The realization describes the organization of the backward pass: automatically generated differentiation, an equation-level adjoint solve, or a custom adjoint. The referent defines the target against which exactness is assessed; the implementation determines how accurately that target is approximated. Cost depends on the realization together with how the states needed by the backward pass are retained or recovered. Crossing the axes places every method and engine we classify in a cell, separates claims the literature merges, and shows that the apparent trade-off between exactness and memory follows from how methods have been built rather than from a constraint. A second, orthogonal taxonomy covers contact, where the map is non-smooth. We then ask whether a correct gradient helps. Four mechanisms by which useful gradients are lost are cataloged and measured on one testbed, where the advantage of the exact derivative over the equation-level adjoint depends on where the fitting target comes from as much as on the solver budget, and we review the evidence that zeroth-order methods can outperform simulator gradients in the regimes where those mechanisms act. The model classes, systems techniques and applications are surveyed through this lens, and we close with a reporting checklist, a released diagnostic that computes it, and a research agenda.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [15] [Preference-Augmented Distributional Reinforcement Learning for Risk-Sensitive Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09932)
*Zhaofan Zhang,Yue Chang,Rufeng Chen,Hechang Chen,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于分位数权重分布偏好建模的分布强化学习导航方法，通过偏好引导探索训练并在部署时针对最坏偏好分布优化，以提升不确定环境下的安全性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于IQN的分布强化学习导航通常训练时使用固定或随机采样的CVaR水平，部署时依赖手工超参数或外部传感器调整风险水平；标量CVaR限制了尾风险偏好学习范围，并造成训练与部署风险偏好不匹配。

Method: 将风险偏好变化表示为分位数权重分布的偏移，并通过偏好引导探索让智能体接触多样风险偏好；部署阶段针对最坏情况偏好分布进行优化，得到考虑多种风险剖面的保守策略，从而减少超参数调优和对有限传感器信号的依赖。

Result: 在仿真环境和车辆平台上的实验表明，该方法降低了碰撞率并提高了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通过分布级风险偏好建模与最坏偏好优化，可改善部分可观测和不确定条件下的安全导航性能，并缓解训练-部署风险偏好不匹配问题。

Abstract: Ensuring safe and reliable navigation under diverse sources of uncertainty remains a central challenge for autonomous systems. Within distributional reinforcement learning (DistRL), Implicit Quantile Networks (IQN) have attracted interest for navigation under uncertainty because they enable risk adaptation through flexible quantile sampling. Existing approaches, however, typically use a fixed or randomly sampled Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) level during training and adjust the risk level during deployment using hand-tuned hyperparameters or external sensor signals. This scalar-CVaR formulation confines learning to a narrow family of tail-risk preferences, creating a mismatch between training-time preference exposure and deployment-time risk adjustment. We instead represent variations in risk preference as shifts in quantile-weight distributions and expose the agent to diverse preferences through preference-guided exploration. At deployment, we optimize against the worst-case preference distribution to derive a conservative policy that accounts for multiple risk profiles. This reduces hyperparameter tuning and reliance on limited sensor signals under partial observability. Experiments across simulated environments and vehicle platforms show that our method reduces collisions while improving task success.

</details>


### [16] [Kinora: A Versioned Workflow for Text-to-Motion Generation, Robot Retargeting, and Interactive Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11601)
*Fangcheng Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kinora 将文本到动作生成、动作库、本地智能体工具、机器人重定向与轨迹细化通过版本化产物串联，支持可恢复执行、可追踪编辑、显式几何和模型特定验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作生成工具只给出候选轨迹，但交互式机器人创作还需要可恢复执行、可追踪编辑、显式几何表示以及针对具体模型的验证。

Method: 系统采用独立定义的52关节创作骨架和程序化骨骼预览，将人类几何与外部生成模型和机器人资产分离；人类动作编辑保留选中区间外帧，机器人编辑生成不可变候选并由报告决定接受与导出。评估包括服务端0.5.1与Gadget 0.15.2、156次核心回归测试、49次Gadget执行、4个CUDA生成/编辑案例、30帧G1重定向检查和3600帧合成G1夹具及暂停负对照。

Result: 4个CUDA案例均通过数值门限，但转向拼接仍保留14.3 cm支撑脚漂移代理警告；G1正例完成有界仿真回放，暂停负对照在回放前被隔离。

Conclusion: 结果支持部分集成与保留契约，但未证明语义质量提升、群体层面可行性或硬件安全性。

Abstract: Motion generation tools produce candidate trajectories, but interactive robot authoring also requires recoverable execution, traceable edits, explicit geometry, and model-specific validation. We present Kinora, a system that connects text-to-motion generation, a motion library, local agent tools, robot retargeting, and trajectory refinement through versioned artifacts. The current release uses an independently specified 52-joint authoring rig and procedural skeleton previews, separating the distributed human geometry from externally installed generation models and robot assets. Human-motion edits preserve frames outside a selected interval, while robot edits create immutable candidates whose reports govern acceptance and export. We evaluate server 0.5.1 and Gadget 0.15.2 with 156 core regression test executions, 49 Gadget executions, four CUDA generation/editing cases, a 30-frame G1 retargeting check, and a 3,600-frame synthetic G1 fixture paired with a suspended negative control. All four CUDA cases passed their numerical gates; the turning splice nevertheless retained a 14.3 cm support-foot drift proxy warning. The G1 positive fixture completed bounded simulation replay, whereas the suspended control was quarantined before replay. These results support selected integration and preservation contracts. They do not establish semantic-quality improvements, population-level feasibility, or hardware safety.

</details>


### [17] [Qubit-to-Motion: Traceable Action Provenance in Hybrid QRL for Robotic Bin-Packing Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11580)
*Hassen Nigatu,Howard Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Qubit-to-Motion 证据中心框架，用于支撑约束下的物体排序与仿真机器人放置，并建立从量子电路读出到机器人接触执行的可复现追溯链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子强化学习已用于机器人决策，但现有评估多关注任务成功、回报或收敛，尚未解决量子电路输出是否影响受约束动作，以及该影响能否沿机器人执行过程被追溯的问题。

Method: 框架仅以四个 Pauli-Z 读出作为 actor 和 critic 的唯一学习输入，并用支撑掩码约束可选物体；每个决策通过步索引和 SHA-256 指纹关联到封存路线、确定性笛卡尔与逆运动学路点、规定关节运动及 MuJoCo 接触回放；冻结检查点在五个确定性 30 物体场景变体上评估。

Result: 150 次放置均满足支撑、包容和稳定判据；固定状态零读出干预改变 90.0±0.0% 的决策，表明决策依赖联合电路表示；平均宏行程 34.537±0.206 m，每路线推理 8.60±1.27 ms，回放最终位置平均误差 8.764±0.220 μm，最大互穿 0.356 mm。

Conclusion: 结果证明了从决策到接触执行的可复现追溯性，并提供了量子读出影响受约束机器人行为的直接证据；实现已公开于匿名仓库。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum reinforcement learning has been explored for robotic decision-making, but evaluations often emphasize task success, return, or convergence, leaving unresolved whether quantum circuit outputs influence constrained actions and remain traceable through robot execution. We present \texttt{Qubit-to-Motion}, an evidence-centered framework for support-constrained sequencing and simulated robotic placement. Four Pauli-$Z$ readouts provide the sole learned input to the actor and critic, while a support mask enforces admissible object selection. Each decision is recorded and linked via step indices and SHA-256 fingerprints to a sealed route, deterministic Cartesian and inverse-kinematics waypoints, prescribed joint motion, and MuJoCo contact replay. A frozen checkpoint is evaluated across five deterministic 30-object scene variants. All 150 placements satisfy support, containment, and settling criteria. A fixed-state zero-readout intervention changes $90.0\pm0.0\%$ of decisions, demonstrating dependence on the joint circuit representation. Mean macro travel is $34.537\pm0.206$~m, inference requires $8.60\pm1.27$~ms per route, and replay achieves $8.764\pm0.220~\mu$m mean final position error with 0.356~mm maximum interpenetration. These results establish reproducible decision-to-contact traceability. The implementation is available at \href{ this https URL }{\textcolor{linkpink}{the anonymous repository}}.

</details>


### [18] [Kimodo-Policy: Learning Kinematic Action Models for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11554)
*Yunheng Wang,Jiaxi Zhang,Wei Lu,Yuetong Fang,Taowen Wang,Yixiao Feng,Shuning Zhang,Junzhe Xu,Yichi Wang,Zizhao Yuan,Kailun Huang,Lusong Li,Mingqian Zhou,Zecui Zeng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出运动学动作模型（KAMs），以全身运动生成为预训练目标，并用预训练运动生成器作为动作生成主干；实例 Kimodo-Policy 在仿真与真实人形机器人 loco-manipulation 任务上取得有竞争力的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 和世界动作模型能迁移语义与视觉动力学先验，但这些先验不一定刻画机器人运动结构；作者希望直接把全身运动作为预训练目标，以学习时间协调和跨身体部位协调先验。

Method: KAMs 通过任务条件、视觉观测和执行反馈对运动先验进行落地。Kimodo-Policy 将冻结的 Kimodo 文本到运动主干与轻量 ControlNet 视觉条件结合，采用 root-body-hand 层级协调移动与操作，并使用运动历史条件和重复动作块预测连接运动合成与闭环控制。

Result: 经大规模 loco-manipulation 预训练后，Kimodo-Policy 在多任务 HumanoidArena 上取得对比策略中最高的总体成功率 72.14%，在 SIMPLE 上表现有竞争力，在 8 个真实世界任务上平均成功率 60%；模型 678M 参数，其中 92M 可训练。消融实验支持预训练运动主干的贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明运动生成可作为人形机器人动作学习的互补基础，为 KAMs 范式提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires continuous coordination of high-dimensional whole-body motion to realize task objectives. While vision-language-action models and world action models transfer semantic and visual-dynamics priors to robot control, these priors do not necessarily capture the structure of robot motion. We introduce Kinematic Action Models (KAMs), a paradigm that treats whole-body motion as a direct pretraining target and uses a pretrained motion generator as the primary action-generation backbone. KAMs ground learned temporal and cross-body coordination priors through task conditioning, visual observations, and execution feedback. We present Kimodo-Policy as an initial instantiation, combining a frozen Kimodo text-to-motion backbone with lightweight ControlNet-based visual conditioning. A root-body-hand hierarchy coordinates locomotion and manipulation, while motion-history conditioning and repeated action-chunk prediction bridge motion synthesis and closed-loop control. Following large-scale loco-manipulation pretraining, the policy achieves the highest overall success among compared policies on multi-task HumanoidArena (72.14%), competitive performance on SIMPLE, and 60% mean success across eight real-world tasks, with 678M parameters (92M trainable). Ablations support the contribution of the pretrained motion backbone. Together, these results provide initial evidence for motion generation as a complementary foundation for humanoid action learning.

</details>


### [19] [K-PWM: Control-Oriented Structured Bilinear World Models under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11521)
*Santosh Mohan Rajkumar,Sriram Narayanan,Samuel E. Otto,Debdipta Goswami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出K-PWM：一种在部分观测下用于预测与控制的结构化概率世界模型，将Koopman双线性潜动态、非线性观测解码器与基于EKF/ES的概率潜状态推断结合，并用GECM训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限、含噪且部分观测的数据中学习世界模型仍很困难；许多物理系统提供的是仅揭示部分底层状态的向量测量，而非像素观测。

Method: K-PWM耦合Koopman双线性潜动态与非线性观测解码器，通过已学习模型进行扩展卡尔曼滤波和平滑来推断潜状态，无需单独训练推断网络；双线性结构可精确传播高斯置信并支持不确定性感知MPPI控制的高效rollout。训练采用广义期望条件最大化GECM，对生成器、噪声协方差和潜先验进行闭式更新，并用梯度学习解码器；Koopman一致初始化从数据选择潜维并初始化动作依赖动态。

Result: 在部分观测Gymnasium MuJoCo系统且训练数据有限时，K-PWM的长期预测误差低于所评估基线，同时训练时间显著短于最强预测基线；在高维MuJoCo Menagerie机器人上取得有竞争力的跟踪精度，且每步计算时间低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结构化概率动态是在部分观测下进行数据高效、计算高效世界建模与控制的有效途径，并具备实时部署潜力。

Abstract: World models learn internal simulators of environment dynamics for prediction, planning, and control, yet learning them from limited, noisy, and partial observations remains challenging. While much progress has focused on pixel-based observations, many physical systems instead provide vector-valued measurements that reveal only part of the underlying state. We introduce K-PWM, a probabilistic world model for prediction and control under partial observation. K-PWM couples Koopman-bilinear latent dynamics with a nonlinear observation decoder and performs probabilistic latent-state inference through the learned model via extended Kalman filtering and smoothing, without a separately trained inference network. Its bilinear structure enables exact propagation of the maintained Gaussian belief through the latent transition and efficient rollouts for uncertainty-aware model predictive path integral (MPPI) control. For training K-PWM, we present a generalized expectation conditional maximization (GECM) procedure with closed-form updates for the generators, noise covariances, and latent prior, complemented by gradient-based decoder learning. A Koopman-consistent initialization selects the latent dimension from data and initializes the action-dependent dynamics. With limited training data, K-PWM achieves lower long-horizon prediction error than the evaluated baselines on partially observed Gymnasium MuJoCo systems, while substantially reducing training time relative to the strongest prediction baseline. On high-dimensional MuJoCo Menagerie robots, it achieves competitive tracking accuracy with low per-step computation times, demonstrating potential for real-time deployment. These results support structured probabilistic dynamics as a data- and compute-efficient approach to world modeling and control under partial observation.

</details>


### [20] [Breaking survivorship bias in driving world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11516)
*Zihao Sheng,Zilin Huang,Yuhao Luo,Zhengyang Wan,Boyue Wang,Soyoung Ahn,David A. Noyce,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出驾驶世界模型存在幸存者偏差，即训练数据中的未来几乎都安全结束，导致模型在碰撞 imminent 时仍想象良性结果；提出中性世界模型NWM，混合常规驾驶与真实事故视频并用边界框引导目标学习，从而更可靠地预测安全关键未来并用于决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型的可靠性受训练数据中未来分布影响。由于主流驾驶数据集记录的未来几乎总是安全结束，模型存在幸存者偏差，难以在碰撞即将发生时准确想象危险后果，限制了其在安全关键决策中的应用。

Method: 提出中性世界模型（NWM），从常规驾驶数据与真实事故视频的混合数据中学习常规和安全关键未来，并采用边界框引导的训练目标。进一步将NWM作为想象引擎：给定多条自车候选轨迹，预测每条轨迹对应的未来，再用奖励函数评估想象结果并选择动作。

Result: NWM能更忠实地预判即将发生的碰撞，同时保持较低误报碰撞率和有竞争力的常规驾驶生成质量。在挑战性NAVSIM场景中，该决策流程比基线世界模型更可靠地选择更优轨迹；使用自动驾驶研究车辆采集的真实行人假人横穿数据进行零样本实验，证明可迁移到未见传感器观测。

Conclusion: 纠正幸存者偏差可使世界模型更忠实地推理未来，并支持自动驾驶中的安全关键决策。

Abstract: World models allow autonomous vehicles to imagine the consequences of candidate actions before execution, but their reliability depends on the futures represented in their training data. We identify a survivorship bias in driving world models: because the futures recorded in prevailing driving datasets almost always end safely, existing models often imagine benign outcomes even when a collision is imminent. We introduce a neutral world model (NWM) that learns both routine and safety-critical futures from a mixture of routine driving and real accident videos with a bounding-box-guided objective. The NWM more faithfully anticipates imminent collisions, while maintaining a low false collision rate and competitive generation quality in routine driving. We further use the model as an imagination engine for decision-making. Given multiple ego trajectories, the NWM predicts the future associated with each candidate, and a reward function evaluates these imagined outcomes before selecting an action. On challenging NAVSIM scenarios, this procedure selects the better trajectory more reliably than the baseline world model. Zero-shot experiments with real-world pedestrian dummy crossing data collected using an autonomous research vehicle further demonstrate transfer to unseen sensor observations. These results show that correcting survivorship bias enables world models to more faithfully reason about futures and support safety-critical decision-making in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [21] [Review the Episodes, Refine the Lessons: Simple Memory Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11509)
*Yifei Yao,Yude Zou,Xing Gao,Guanbo Jiao,Yukai Wang,Jianming Ma,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimplEvo 探索不更新权重、不依赖后训练策略或特权信息时，MLLM 通过自写文本记忆在具身控制中能达到的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体常围绕 MLLM 增加复杂 harness（如后训练策略、递归自我改进），但其增益是否超过 MLLM 原生能力尚不清楚。

Method: 提出 SimplEvo：MLLM 从单次演示写一次无坐标任务记忆；仅在训练回合构建有界工作记忆，并在每回合后按过程、完成与恢复经验进行精炼；测试时两类记忆固定，不进行权重更新或使用特权信息。

Result: 在 EBench 上，GPT-6-Astra 直接控制成功率从 46.7% 提升至 81.7%；RoboDojo 排行榜达 69.63% SR、75.32 分。仅用文本记忆即可在更少 token 下达到与存视觉帧记忆相近 SR，并可泛化到未见布局与随机场景配置；工作记忆可由 GPT-6-Astra 写入后被较弱 MLLM 直接复用并提升性能。

Conclusion: SimplEvo 表明，无需复杂 harness 或权重更新，MLLM 借助自写文本记忆即可显著提升具身任务表现，且记忆具有跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Recent studies of embodied agents based on frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) develop harness engineering around the MLLM, such as harnesses with post-trained policies and recursive self-improvement loops. Yet whether these additions exceed the native capability of the MLLM remains unexplored. We introduce SimplEvo to explore how far an MLLM can improve under direct control when it retains its execution experience in self-written text memories. It requires no weight updates, post-trained policies, or privileged information. The MLLM writes a coordinate-free task memory once from a single demonstration. Under a strict train-test protocol, it then creates a bounded working memory on training episodes only and refines it after each episode with lessons on procedure, completion, and recovery. Both memories stay fixed at test time. On EBench, SimplEvo lifts the success rate (SR) of GPT-6-Astra under direct control from 46.7% to 81.7%, and tops the RoboDojo leaderboard with 69.63% SR and a score of 75.32. Compared with memories that also store past visual frames, SimplEvo reaches a similar SR with text memory alone while using fewer tokens. With its memories fixed, SimplEvo also generalizes to unseen layouts and randomized scene configurations. Moreover, the working memory transfers across models: written by GPT-6-Astra, it can be reused as is by weaker MLLMs and improve their performance without any adaptation of their own.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond Memory Limits: A Hierarchical Spatio-Temporal Map Representation for Large-Scale 3D Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11489)
*Yizhe Zhang,Jianping Li,Yilun Gao,Liangliang Yin,Pengfei Wan,Bisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLAVOR 提出一种分层时空框架，将稠密度量几何限制在机器人中心局部地图内，同时用轻量全局观测与任务上下文支持长时程自主 UAV 探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 UAV 探索系统多维护全局可查询几何地图，内存与空间查询成本随探索区域增长；机器人中心局部地图虽可限制开销，却丢失长时程任务所需的全局上下文。

Method: FLAVOR 在观测层用轻量 LiDAR 关键帧可见性记录保留逐射线前沿证据；在任务层将局部地图范围外未解决前沿保留为与里程计锚点关联的紧凑待处理任务；用稀疏拓扑图连接锚点及保留路径。返回任务区域时，从观测重建前沿与探索视点，并在执行前基于当前局部地图实例化与验证拓扑路径。

Result: 在完成全部三个仿真场景的最佳基线对比中，探索时间缩短 31.98–41.88%，地图内存占用降低 94–99%；真实部署中，相对显式点云 ikd-tree，森林环境内存降低 92.67%，建筑工地降低 62.42%，并在受限机载资源下保持长期任务连续性。

Conclusion: FLAVOR 使稠密地图容量在探索扩展时保持固定，将探索相关内存增长限制在紧凑观测与任务表示中，在受限资源下实现高效且具长时程连续性的 UAV 自主探索。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV exploration is increasingly used for large-scale surveying and inspection in inaccessible or disaster-affected environments, where onboard memory and computational resources are often severely constrained. Most existing UAV exploration systems maintain globally queryable geometric map representations for motion planning and exploration decision-making, causing memory consumption and spatial-query costs to increase with the explored area. Robot-centric local mapping bounds dense-map memory and computation but discards global task context needed for long-horizon this http URL present FLAVOR, a hierarchical spatio-temporal framework that confines dense metric geometry to a robot-centric local map while retaining compact global observation and task context. At the observation level, lightweight LiDAR-keyframe visibility records preserve ray-wise evidence for frontier reasoning. At the task level, unresolved frontiers beyond the local-map extent are retained as compact pending tasks associated with odometry anchors. A sparse topological graph connects these anchors through retained routes for future this http URL returning to a task region, FLAVOR reconstructs the frontier and exploration viewpoint from observations, while the topological route is instantiated and validated against the current local map before execution. Dense-map capacity remains fixed as exploration expands, with exploration-dependent memory growth confined to compact observation and task this http URL achieves 31.98--41.88% shorter exploration time and a 94--99% smaller map memory footprint than the best baseline that completes all three simulated scenes. In real-world deployments, it reduces this footprint by 92.67% in the forest and 62.42% at the construction site relative to an explicit point-cloud ikd-tree while preserving long-term task continuity under constrained onboard resources.

</details>


### [23] [Off-Policy RL with Online Inference Tuning: My Solution to the CoRL 2026 RoboEval Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11464)
*Ilia Larchenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文介绍 RoboEval 2026 双臂操作挑战的解决方案：以自价值 π0.5 策略做离策略强化学习，并结合生成示范与失败恢复数据，将官方评分 RDS 从 0.85 提升至 0.990（留出种子）和 0.987（官方种子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoboEval 不仅评估任务是否成功，还按任务各阶段的安全性、效率和双臂协调性，对照人类示范评分；因此策略需要直接优化官方细粒度评分，并弥补局部探索难以发现的数据。

Method: 方案分两部分。通用部分沿用 LeHome 方案：在基于 π0.5 且同时作为价值函数的策略上做离策略 RL，用 AWR 和 RECAP 基于精确官方分数的优势进行训练；每次 rollout 同时作为约 20 个推理参数的 Thompson 采样试验。竞赛特定部分加入局部探索难以获得的数据：可达到完整奖励的生成示范，以及从策略自身失败状态开始的恢复回合。

Result: 通用部分在两天内于留出种子达到 0.85 RDS，且远未收敛；加入竞赛特定数据后，提交策略在留出种子达到 0.990 RDS，在官方种子达到 0.987 RDS。

Conclusion: 直接以官方评分为优势信号的自价值离策略 RL 能高效提升策略表现，而生成完整奖励示范与失败恢复数据可进一步突破局部探索瓶颈，显著提高 RoboEval 综合评分。

Abstract: The CoRL 2026 RoboEval challenge has eight bimanual tasks, and it ranks a policy not only on whether it succeeds but on how: every stage of the task, done safely, efficiently and with both arms in coordination, scored against human demonstrations. My solution has two parts. The generic part, carried over from my LeHome challenge solution, is off-policy RL on a $\pi_{0.5}$-based policy that is also its own value function. It is trained with AWR and RECAP on advantages of the exact official score, in a loop where every rollout is at the same time a Thompson-sampling trial of about 20 inference parameters. Out of the box, it took the policy to 0.85 RDS on held-out seeds in two days (and it was still very far from convergence). The competition-specific part adds data that local exploration cannot find: generated demonstrations that reach the full reward, and recovery episodes that start from the policy's own failures. The submitted policy reaches 0.990 RDS on held-out seeds and 0.987 on the official seeds.

</details>

*Yiming Sun,Mi Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建 ArmedAgent 基准，覆盖 5 个场景、36 个任务，评估鲁棒性、恶意使用和间接注入，并在仿真中评测、在真实机械臂上验证；部分任务针对早期 VLA/WAM 范式无法执行的有害行为，并在常用智能体框架下测试多个前沿模型，包括 GPT-6-Astra。

Result: 测试发现当前系统存在显著安全风险；前沿模型在常用智能体框架下可执行此前 VLA 范式无法完成的有害行为，且间接提示注入可从数字资源传导至机器人物理控制。

Conclusion: 零样本机器人控制智能体的能力提升同时扩大了滥用与注入攻击面，亟需针对编码智能体物理控制场景的安全评测与防护机制。

Abstract: Advances in spatial understanding have enabled frontier models to power coding agents that control a real robot arm zero-shot and end-to-end, without task-specific demonstrations or retraining. This brings two new safety challenges. First, the extensive knowledge of frontier models lets these agents perform tasks that earlier VLA and WAM paradigms could not, opening the door to new forms of capability misuse. Second, coding agents can access digital resources such as web pages and emails. Threats in these resources, such as indirect prompt injection, can carry over into the physical world when the agent controls a robot. We therefore introduce ArmedAgent, the first systematic benchmark to evaluate both risks for coding agents performing zero-shot robot control. It covers robustness, malicious use, and indirect injection across 36 tasks in 5 scenarios, evaluated in simulation and validated on a physical robot arm. A distinctive portion of the tasks target harmful behaviors that earlier VLA paradigms could not perform. Testing several frontier models, including GPT-6-Astra, under widely used agent frameworks, we find substantial safety risks in current systems.

</details>


### [25] [Single-Radar Bilateral 3D Near-Field Imaging for Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11296)
*Yingqi Wang,Tingwei Chen,Zhongqin Wang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种运动辅助的近场毫米波MIMO重建框架，仅用单个前向安装雷达在机器人一次前向运动中获得轨迹两侧的三维散射结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭移动机器人需要可靠的近场三维感知，但单个前向毫米波雷达受近场波前曲率、视角相关的目标可见性以及俯仰多样性有限等因素影响，难以同时恢复路径两侧的家具和周边结构。

Method: 建立近场模型，用Tx到散射体再到Rx的球面路径关联场景反射率与跨频率、跨机器人位置的步进频率MIMO观测；采用双边Tx合并进行宽带方向加权；用滑动孔径反投影按近场传播距离相干聚焦；用Top-K功率融合不同可见性的局部图像；基于重建的横向-前向支撑进行多层高度提取，并利用原始MIMO通道进行带保护的 adaptive height refinement。

Result: 在受控环境、办公室/书房和走廊中，平均横向-前向定位误差分别为4.65、5.63和5.98 cm，峰值背景比（PBR）均超过13 dB。

Conclusion: 该框架仅依赖单个雷达的一次前向通过即可恢复双侧三维散射结构，且不依赖场景的学习表征。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating in homes need reliable 3D perception of nearby furniture and surrounding structures, yet recovering this information on both sides of the robot's path with a single forward-mounted millimeter-wave radar remains challenging because of near-field wavefront curvature, viewpoint-dependent target visibility, and limited elevation diversity. This paper presents a motion-assisted framework for reconstructing structures on both sides of a robot's trajectory. Our near-field model relates scene reflectivity to stepped-frequency multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) observations across frequencies and robot positions using spherical paths from transmitter (Tx) to scatterer to receiver (Rx). Bilateral Tx combining provides wideband directional weighting, while sliding-aperture back-projection coherently focuses the measurements using near-field propagation distances. Top-K power fusion combines local images with different visibility. The reconstruction provides lateral-forward support for multi-layer height extraction, followed by guarded adaptive height refinement using the original MIMO channels. Experiments in controlled, office/study, and corridor environments produce mean lateral-forward localization errors of 4.65, 5.63, and 5.98 cm, respectively, with peak-to-background ratios (PBRs) exceeding 13 dB. The framework recovers bilateral 3D scattering structure from one forward pass using a single radar, without relying on learned representations of the scene.

</details>

*Hanwen Zhao,Weicheng Dai,Luoyao Chen,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeepSoMo，从潜在动力学视角解决本体感知部分可观测下的可变形体运动估计，通过自监督潜在预测约束强化潜在时间一致性，并在有限元仿真中验证其优于架构匹配的循环基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人可变形体运动估计面临部分可观测性：内部本体感知仅间接反映全局运动、接触与形状演化，单帧观测不足，长时程估计需要时间一致的潜在表征。

Method: 将问题建模为潜在动力学估计，提出DeepSoMo学习框架，以自监督潜在预测约束在潜在动力学中引入预测结构，使潜在状态保持对未来观测的可预测性。

Result: 在高保真有限元仿真作为受控真值的典型可变形动力学设置中，DeepSoMo在轨迹估计、姿态恢复、形状重建和接触推断上相较架构匹配的循环基线均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 潜在时间一致性是本体感知部分可观测下长时程可变形运动估计的关键要求，DeepSoMo通过预测性潜在动力学有效提升多任务估计性能。

Abstract: Estimating the motion of deformable bodies in soft robotics from proprioceptive sensing is challenging due to partial observability, as internal observations provide only indirect access to global motion, contact, and shape evolution. In such settings, instantaneous observations are insufficient, and accurate estimation requires temporally consistent latent representations. This paper studies deformable motion estimation under proprioceptive partial observability from a latent dynamics perspective. We argue that a key requirement for long-horizon estimation is latent temporal consistency, where latent states remain predictive of future observations. To this end, we present DeepSoMo, a learning-based framework that enforces predictive structure in latent dynamics through a self-supervised latent prediction constraint. We evaluate the approach in a canonical deformable dynamics setting using high-fidelity finite-element simulation as a controlled source of ground truth. Results demonstrate consistent improvements in trajectory estimation, orientation recovery, shape reconstruction, and contact inference over architecture-matched recurrent baselines.

</details>

*Zhongxi Chen,Yi Yang,Xinyi Chen,Mingzhe Ye,Yaping Li,Zhihong Liu,Congsheng Xu,Haonan Wang,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexForesight 通过将动作条件空间世界模型的动态表示对齐到灵巧操作策略潜空间，隐式学习空间预见；部署时仅保留策略，用 RGB、语言和机器人状态实现操作，在仿真和真机上均提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要对场景和机械手未来空间变化有丰富预见；现有 world-action 模型通常依赖显式未来预测来获得这种能力。

Method: 训练阶段利用离线场景深度、运动学手部几何和示范轨迹学习动作条件空间世界模型，预测 3D 手部点运动与视觉特征变化，涵盖关节手动态、场景变化及被遮挡手指运动；随后冻结世界模型，将策略查询与其预测性世界查询对齐，把空间动态迁移到策略潜变量并用于条件化动作生成。部署时仅保留策略，输入 RGB、语言和机器人状态，无需深度或显式未来预测。

Result: 在 11 个 DexJoCo 仿真任务上，整体成功率由基线的 49.4% 提升至 60.5%；在 3 个真实机器人灵巧操作任务上达到 58.3%，最强基线为 50.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过潜空间对齐隐式注入世界模型的空间动态知识，可在不依赖测试时深度输入和显式未来预测的情况下显著提升灵巧操作性能。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires rich spatial foresight over both the surrounding scene and the robot hand. Existing world-action models often acquire such predictive knowledge through explicit future prediction. We introduce DexForesight, a framework that learns spatial foresight implicitly by injecting dynamics-aware world representations into the latent space of a dexterous policy. During training, DexForesight learns an action-conditioned spatial world model from offline scene depth, kinematic hand geometry, and demonstration trajectories. The world model predicts 3D hand-point motion and visual feature changes, capturing both articulated hand dynamics and scene changes under demonstrated actions, including the motion of fingers occluded from the cameras. We then freeze the world model and align policy queries with its predictive world queries, transferring the learned spatial dynamics into policy latents and using them to condition action generation. At deployment, only the policy is retained, operating on RGB observations, language, and robot state without depth input or explicit future prediction. On 11 DexJoCo simulation tasks, DexForesight improves the overall success rate of the base policy from 49.4% to 60.5%. On three real-robot dexterous manipulation tasks, DexForesight achieves 58.3% success, compared with 50.0% for the strongest this http URL page this https URL

</details>

*Christian Marowski,Byung-Cheol Min*

TL;DR: 该教程面向差速驱动机器人，使用俯视相机、ArUco标记和Python，逐步实现圆形多机器人编队控制，涵盖位姿估计、编队几何、反馈控制、避障、系统集成与编队误差评估，并提供开源代码。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 用ArUco标记估计机器人位置与朝向，以期望成对位移向量定义圆形编队；比较实测与期望相对位置计算运动向量，并转换为差速驱动轮指令，同时加入基于接近传感器的基本避障。教程覆盖硬件软件搭建、机器人位姿估计、编队几何、反馈控制、系统集成和编队误差评估，GitHub仓库提供Python实现、搭建说明与演示。

Result: 形成了面向动手学习的完整教程和开源实现，支持多机器人系统的复现与进一步探索；摘要未给出定量实验结果或性能指标。

Conclusion: 该教程弥合了机器人教育中的理论实践鸿沟，提供了可操作的多机器人编队控制实现，其感知与编队控制原则可推广到其他差速驱动机器人及仿真环境。

Abstract: This tutorial presents a step-by-step introduction to multi-robot formation control using differential- drive robots, an overhead camera, and Python. ArUco markers are used to estimate robot positions and orientations, while desired pairwise displacement vectors define a circular formation. By comparing measured and desired relative positions, the controller computes movement vectors that are converted into differential-drive wheel commands, with basic proximity-sensor-based obstacle avoidance. The tutorial guides readers through hardware and software setup, robot pose estimation, formation geometry, feedback control, system integration, and formation-error evaluation. Motivated by the need to bridge the gap between theoretical understanding and practical implementation in robotics education, we emphasize how mathematical concepts are translated into coordinated behaviors on physical robots. Although the implementation uses a specific robotic platform, the underlying perception and formation-control principles can be adapted to other differential-drive robots and simulation environments. The accompanying open-source GitHub repository provides the Python implementation, setup instructions, and demonstrations to support hands-on learning and further exploration of multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [29] [MOSU2: Long-range Navigation System with Multi-modal Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11148)
*Jing Liang,Zhenyu Hou,Senthurbavan Kirubaharan,Md Safwan Mondal,Ashok Gudivada,Xuesu Xiao,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOSU2 是一个面向公共户外长距离导航的两阶段轨迹选择系统：先用上下文推理判断是否需要额外证据，再按需检索几何与动态信息，从而在行人、车辆、施工和交通信号等复杂场景中做出前进、让行或绕行决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外长距离机器人导航不仅需要避障，还必须判断场景演化（如车辆是否减速、骑行者是否会横穿）并选择合适的通行行为。现有VLM虽能理解视觉线索，但缺乏度量几何与运动信息；每步都提供此类证据又过慢，手工触发器也难以覆盖开放场景。

Method: MOSU2基于GPS路线规划，采用两阶段轨迹选择：第一阶段进行上下文推理，判断交互是否未解决以及需要检索何种证据；仅当交互未解决时触发第二阶段，检索几何与动态信息并与场景上下文对齐，再通过多模态可通行性分析过滤候选轨迹并选择机器人运动轨迹。

Result: 在包含停止手势、施工现场、人行横道及与车辆、骑行者和行人共享道路的真实实验中，MOSU2相较单图像前作将平均导航成功率从61.8%提升至94.2%，条件检测准确率从72.2%提升至91.4%，平均决策延迟约为前作的2.2倍。

Conclusion: 按需触发多模态证据检索的两阶段推理可在可接受的延迟代价下显著提升户外长距离导航的成功率与复杂条件检测能力，优于仅依赖单图像VLM推理的方案。

Abstract: Robots that travel long distances through public outdoor spaces must do more than avoid obstacles: they must remain on terrain suited to their mobility and decide when to proceed, yield, or detour around pedestrians, cyclists, vehicles, traffic signals, and temporary restrictions. These decisions are difficult because their correctness often depends on how a scene evolves, such as whether an approaching car is slowing or a cyclist will cross the robot's path. Vision-language models (VLMs) can interpret such cues, but they reason over images that lack metric geometry and motion. Supplying this evidence at every step is too slow for real-time navigation, and hand-crafted triggers cannot anticipate the open-ended variety of outdoor situations. We present MOSU2, a long-range navigation system with two-stage trajectory selection, in which first-stage contextual reasoning determines when additional evidence is needed and what to retrieve. Only when an interaction remains unresolved does the first stage trigger the second stage to retrieve geometric and dynamic information and align it with scene context to make navigation decisions. Building on MOSU, MOSU2 follows routes from GPS-based route planning and filters candidate trajectories through multi-modal traversability analysis before selecting a trajectory to move the robot. In real-world experiments spanning stop gestures, construction sites, crosswalks, and roads shared with vehicles, cyclists, and pedestrians, MOSU2 raises mean navigation success from 61.8% to 94.2% and condition-detection accuracy from 72.2% to 91.4% over its single-image predecessor, at about 2.2 times its mean decision latency. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [30] [From Festival Video to the Stage: A Sim-to-Real Ladder for Long-Horizon Expressive Whole-Body Dance on a Commercial Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11127)
*Hesham Shehata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套从单目视频将阿波舞迁移到Unitree G1 EDU人形机器人的完整流水线，并在真实硬件上验证长期稳定表演与控制器交接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将传统日本阿波舞从单目视频迁移到商用双足人形机器人，打通从人体动作捕捉、仿真训练到机载部署的工程链路，并解决真实部署中的隐性故障与稳定性问题。

Method: 流水线结合人体网格恢复、运动学重定向、仿真强化学习动作跟踪和基于ONNX的机载部署；在单张RTX 5070上训练，并用11版硬件部署阶梯评估三类干预：检测并修复部署产物的静默损坏、建模训练中的状态估计器漂移、编辑参考动作以支持稳定执行和受控结束。

Result: 最终策略实现连续3次无绳完成43秒套路、46秒自动交接表演，以及由捕获动作合成的连续9.7分钟无绳表演；现场单次远程请求即可让机器人从厂商行走控制器启动并返回，测得45–140 ms的无扭矩窗口。

Conclusion: 工作报告了训练动态与硬件结果，表明该流水线可实现传统舞蹈在商用双足人形机器人上的稳定部署；同时指出合规地板和单目捕捉遮挡等失败边界。

Abstract: We present a pipeline for transferring Awa Odori, a traditional Japanese festival dance, from monocular video to a commercial Unitree G1 EDU humanoid. The pipeline combines human mesh recovery, kinematic retargeting, reinforcement learning for motion tracking in simulation, and onboard deployment via ONNX. Training ran on a single RTX 5070 GPU. An eleven-version deployment ladder evaluated on hardware examines three interventions. We detect and repair silent corruption of deployment artifacts, model state-estimator drift during training, and edit the reference motion to support stable execution and a controlled ending. The final policies achieved three consecutive rope-free completions of a 43-second routine, a 46-second performance with automatic handoff, and a continuous 9.7-minute rope-free performance composed from the captured motion. In a live handover, a single remote request let the robot start from and return to the manufacturer's walking controller on stage, with a measured torque-free window of 45-140 ms. We report the training dynamics and hardware outcomes and discuss failure boundaries involving compliant flooring and occlusion in monocular capture.

</details>


### [31] [DreamLander: A 6-DOF World Model based Reinforcement Learning Planetary Lander](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12824)
*George Nehma,Patrick Quinn,Madhur Tiwari,Camilo Riano-Rios*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DreamLander：基于 DreamerV3 的模型强化学习算法，用于真实 6 自由度进入、下降与着陆（EDL）问题的最优制导与控制生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软着陆对高精度、高安全载荷交付至关重要，但燃料严格受限，需要最优制导轨迹与控制；模型强化学习可简化复杂最优制导生成过程。

Method: DreamLander 采用基于 DreamerV3 的模型强化学习方法，利用状态传感器观测，针对 6 自由度 EDL 问题学习制导与控制策略，旨在获得样本高效且可泛化的模型。

Result: 方法在多种场景下保持较高精度，着陆点误差在指定着陆区 2 米以内，速度范数小于 0.3 米/秒，并具备样本高效和较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: DreamLander 展示了模型强化学习在真实 6 自由度 EDL 最优制导与控制中的有效性，并向基于传感器观测的端到端 EDL 解决方案迈进。

Abstract: Soft powered descent landings are becoming evermore important in the field of space exploration and scientific advancement, because of their ability to deliver mission critical payloads, both highly accurately and safely. The high efficiency restriction on planetary landings due to the severely limited fuel supply results in the need for optimal guidance trajectories and control. Model based reinforcement learning is proving to be an important tool in simplifying the highly complex process of generating optimal guidance trajectories and control. In this work, we present DreamLander, a state of the art model based reinforcement learning algorithm based on DreamerV3, to generate optimal guidance and control for a realistic 6 degree of freedom entry descent landing problem. The model based approach creates a sample efficient and highly generalizable model that can be applied to a variety of scenarios whilst maintaining a high degree of accuracy, landing within 2 meters of the designated landing zone, and with velocity norm less than 0.3 meters per second. DreamLander makes use of state sensor observations, making progress toward End-to-End solutions of the Entry Descent Landing problem.

</details>


### [32] [Simulating Vein Compressibility for Ultrasound-Guided Femoral Access: Speed, Accuracy, and Phantom Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12818)
*Amit Manicka,Laurent Coulon,Darwin Ang,Michael Loushin,Timothy M. Kowalewski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文验证了在NVIDIA Isaac Sim for Healthcare中，基于物理的超声引导股血管穿刺仿真可行性：采用Stable Neo-Hookean组织模型与单GPU Vertex Block Descent求解，在保持临床相关“静脉先于动脉塌陷”信号的同时，将每帧求解时间从29,700 ms降至30.4 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声探头压力会使组织变形并改变机器人所观察到的血管解剖结构，因此仿真器必须快速复现这些力学效应以支持机器人交互；但实现“静脉先于动脉塌陷”这一临床有意义信号所需的计算量尚不明确。

Method: 构建已知几何与力学参数的低保真圆柱台式体模，由机器人按压；在NVIDIA Isaac Sim for Healthcare中使用Stable Neo-Hookean组织模型和单GPU Vertex Block Descent求解。将仿真与物理体模对比，评估动脉/静脉预测及塌陷行为；扫描单元尺寸、时间步和求解迭代数，分析精度与每帧墙钟时间；并测试包含超声和图形渲染的完整栈性能；最后以文献力学参数初始化数字腿部模型。

Result: 仿真在每个深度对动脉预测误差在5个百分点以内；在25.4 mm按压的前11 mm内对静脉误差在3个百分点以内，并跟随低保真物理体模的塌陷行为。每帧墙钟时间随求解工作量线性缩放，但增加工作量未提升精度。所选设置将每帧求解时间从29,700 ms降至30.4 ms，加速976倍，且与最细参考设置误差在1.03个百分点以内。仅物理求解器每帧30.4 ms，完整栈每帧56.4 ms。数字腿部模型在所有设置下静脉压缩均远大于动脉，复现了用于区分血管的“静脉先于动脉塌陷”预期临床信号。

Conclusion: 结果表明，NVIDIA Isaac Sim平台可能适用于增强强化学习训练或在线仿真，以支持超声引导下的自主股血管穿刺。

Abstract: In ultrasound-guided femoral access, probe pressure deforms the tissue and changes the vascular anatomy a robot observes. A simulator must reproduce these mechanics fast enough for robotic interaction, yet the computational effort needed for a clinically meaningful signal like vein collapse before artery is unclear. We test the feasibility of a physics-based simulation in NVIDIA's Isaac Sim for Healthcare, with Stable Neo-Hookean tissue solved by Vertex Block Descent on one GPU. A simulation of a low-fidelity cylindrical benchtop phantom with known geometry and mechanical properties, pressed by a robot, predicted its artery within 5 percentage points at every depth. It matched the vein within 3 points through 11 mm of a 25.4 mm press and followed the collapse behavior of the low-fidelity physical phantom. Across element size, time step, and solver iterations, wall clock time per frame scaled linearly with solver work, but accuracy did not improve with more work. The chosen setting reduced solver time per frame by a factor of 976, from 29,700 ms to 30.4 ms, while remaining within 1.03 points of the finest reference setting. The physics solver alone took 30.4 ms per frame, while the full stack, including ultrasound and graphics rendering, took 56.4 ms per frame. Finally, when seeded with realistic literature-based mechanical properties, the digital leg model compressed the vein far more than the artery at every setting, reproducing the expected clinical signal of vein collapse before artery, which is used to distinguish them. In total, these results suggest that the NVIDIA Isaac Sim platform may be suitable for augmenting reinforcement learning training or online simulation for autonomous femoral access with ultrasound.

</details>


### [33] [Learning Heterogeneous Tactile Representation for General Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12806)
*Bing Han,Yichuan Peng,Haoyu Dong,Yuanmeng Hu,Yongyi Jia,Fangjing Wang,Kecheng Zheng,Yiming Zhang,Yanhong Zeng,Xiaoqing Zhang,Chen Song,Rundong Zou,Anqi Dong,Yuan Guan,Ka Leong Cheng,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Nan Xue,Wei Ning*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LingBot-Touch触觉基础模型，实现跨压阻、磁性和视触觉传感器的统一触觉表征，并以即插即用触觉token增强多种策略模型的灵巧操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对接触密集的灵巧操作不可或缺，但商用触觉传感器发展仍较早，不同类型各有优劣；核心挑战是让触觉理解摆脱特定传感器的物理特性，从而有效利用异构触觉数据。

Method: 构建LingBot-Touch触觉基础模型：学习跨不同触觉传感器的统一表征，并可扩展到新传感器类型；生成的触觉token可即插即用地集成到扩散策略及π0.5、LingBot-VLA等视觉-语言-动作模型中。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人、多硬件平台上的大量实验表明，该触觉表征能持续提升操作灵巧性，显著受益于复杂操作任务，同时保持较强计算效率。

Conclusion: 研究展示了利用跨传感器数据扩展触觉学习的潜力，并为未来触觉硬件与具身模型设计提供了有益启示。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is an indispensable modality for contact-rich dexterous manipulation, a capability that hints at the ultimate frontiers of embodied intelligence. However, commercially available tactile sensors are still at a relatively early stage of development, with each type of sensor offering irreplaceable strengths alongside its own drawbacks. A key challenge is thus to free tactile understanding from sensor-specific physics, so that embodied models can effectively leverage heterogeneous tactile data. To this end, we develop LingBot-Touch, a tactile foundation model characterized by the following properties. (i) Our model yields a unified representation across different tactile sensors (i.e., piezoresistive, magnetic, and visuotactile sensors), and is easily extensible to new sensor types. (ii) The resulting tactile tokens are plug-and-play: they can be seamlessly integrated into policy models across paradigms, from diffusion policy to vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $\pi_{0.5}$ and LingBot-VLA, consistently enhancing manipulation dexterity. (iii) Extensive experiments, spanning simulation and real-robot deployments across multiple hardware platforms, demonstrate that our tactile representation substantially benefits complex manipulation tasks while maintaining strong computational efficiency. We believe the findings in this work demonstrate the potential of leveraging cross-sensor data to scale tactile learning, and offer encouraging insights for the design of future hardware and embodied models.

</details>


### [34] [Benchmarking Real-World Robotic Manipulation through Stochastic Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12804)
*Elias Gläßer,Gülhan Şikaroğlu,Pavlo Kupyn,Marc Pollefeys,Zuria Bauer,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“随机挑战”范式，用可复现实景中的同分布随机任务样本，对移动操作机器人进行跨实验室、可比较的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作是交互式任务，无法仅靠静态观测数据集评估；仿真基准又难以覆盖真实世界测试的复杂性。因此需要兼顾真实场景可复现性、系统级可比性以及隐藏测试集严谨性的评测方法。

Method: 作者提出随机挑战作为机器人基准测试的通用范式，并实现一个移动操作新基准：场景可复现，任务样本从同一分布随机抽取，以成功率比较不同实验室中的不同机器人。

Result: 在移动操作基准上，作者比较了两个不同实验室测试的两台机器人；基于同分布随机任务样本的成功率实现跨平台比较。摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 随机挑战可作为机器人基准测试的通用范式，既支持系统级创新与跨平台可比性，又具备隐藏测试集式的严谨性和随机性。

Abstract: A challenging bottleneck in manipulation research is reproducibility and comparability of methods in real-world environments. Since manipulation is an interactive task, it cannot be evaluated on a static dataset of observations.
