Benchmarking in simulation is a common remedy, but cannot cover the whole complexity of real-world tests. This paper proposes a new paradigm of stochastic challenges. Like the well-known YCB objects, it is based on reproducible real-world scenes and therefore allows system-level innovation and comparability between different platforms. Due to their stochastic nature, our challenges however, mimic the rigor and surprise of hidden test sets. To this end, we show how different robots in different labs can be compared based on their success rate over different random task samples, if these samples come from the same distribution. We propose stochastic challenges as a general paradigm for robot benchmarking and implement this paradigm in a novel benchmark for mobile manipulation, on which we compare two robots tested in two different labs.

</details>


### [35] [Sliding into Balance: Benefits of a Sliding Payload for Load Distribution and Disturbance Rejection in Dual-UAV Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12754)
*Jorge Vicente-Martinez,Francisco José Mañas-Álvarez,Edgar Ramirez-Laboreo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究两架无人机用单根绳索悬挂并可沿绳滑动的负载协同运输，建立含粘性摩擦的平面无量纲动力学模型，并与总绳长相同的固定负载方案比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双机吊运常将负载分别系于两根独立绳索，存在绳索张力不均和松弛风险；本文探索负载沿单绳滑动能否改善张力分配、可达范围与抗扰性。

Method: 建立滑动负载的平面无量纲动力学模型并考虑粘性摩擦；在无人机静止条件下，与总绳长相同的固定负载配置在平衡态和扰动下进行解析比较，并进行真实阵风剖面下的动态仿真。

Result: 解析结果表明：滑动负载在任意无人机相对位置下均均衡两段绳索张力，平衡时无绳索失张；其可达区域扩大一倍；除低摩擦且无人机间距较大情形外，可容许扰动集合增大。动态仿真显示，在真实阵风下，相比固定负载方案，两机间平均张力不平衡降低。

Conclusion: 单绳滑动负载构型可改善双无人机吊运的张力均衡性、扩大可达范围和扰动容许性，并在阵风条件下减小张力不平衡，但低摩擦与大间距组合是需注意的限制情形。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the cooperative transportation of a payload suspended from two drones in which, instead of being attached to two independent ropes, the payload is allowed to slide along a single rope connecting both UAVs. First, a planar nondimensional dynamical model of the sliding motion with viscous friction is presented. Then, considering stationary drones, this configuration is compared with a fixed payload with the same total rope length, both at equilibrium and under disturbances. The analytical results show that the sliding payload equalizes the tension in both rope segments for any relative position of the drones, so that no rope loses tension at equilibrium; it doubles the region reachable by the payload and, except for low friction combined with a large drone separation, it enlarges the set of admissible disturbances. Finally, a dynamic simulation also shows that under realistic gusty wind profiles, the average tension imbalance between the drones is reduced with respect to the fixed-payload solution.

</details>


### [36] [Library of Hands: Fix the Hands, Not the World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12678)
*Hasaan Ahmad,Qizhen Ying,Yuxuan Sun,Tianjin Huang,Guangming Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LoH 是一种无需训练的方法：保持世界模型冻结，仅通过冻结模型从两帧图像变化中推断并校正“命令到实际动作”的映射（hand），并缓存已识别映射以识别回归设置，从而提升世界模型规划器对相机/控制器变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型加规划器在相机移动或控制器改变时会失效：同一命令产生不同动作，例如命令旋转90度时，使用LeWM的冻结规划器成功率从77.5%降到7.8%。现有修复需重训、需训练数据，或在设置回归时遗忘。

Method: 提出Library of Hands（LoH）：不训练、不增加传感器，保持世界模型冻结，只校正命令到施加动作的映射（hand）。LoH利用冻结模型寻找最能解释两幅相机图像间变化的动作，称为一次reading；存储识别出的每个hand，使返回的设置可被识别而非重新学习。

Result: 闭环测试中，LoH在三个仿真任务上将成功率从28.6%提升到62.3%，超过两倍；无变化时仅损失0.6个百分点。它无需改动即可用于另外两个世界模型，并在三个真实机器人数据预训练模型中的两个上有效；其收益随reading质量提高而增大。

Conclusion: LoH证明可通过推断并缓存动作映射校正，使冻结世界模型规划器适应命令语义变化，避免重训、额外数据和灾难性遗忘，并具有跨模型与真实数据预训练模型的迁移潜力。

Abstract: A world model predicts how a scene changes after an action, and a planner uses it to search for actions that reach a goal. If the camera moves or the controller changes, the same command moves the robot differently: when its commands are turned by 90 degrees, a frozen planner with the LeWM world model that succeeds in 77.5% of episodes succeeds in only 7.8%. Current fixes retrain the model, need training data, or forget a set-up when it returns. We propose Library of Hands (LoH), a training-free method that keeps the world model frozen and corrects only the map from commands to applied actions, which we call the hand. LoH reads the hand through the frozen model, with no extra sensor: it finds the action that best explains the change between two camera images (a reading). It stores every hand it identifies, so a returning set-up is recognised rather than re-learnt. In closed-loop tests, LoH more than doubles success on three simulated tasks, from 28.6% to 62.3%, and loses only 0.6 percentage points when nothing changes. It also works, unchanged, with two other world models and on two of three models pretrained on real robot data, and its gain grows with the quality of its readings.

</details>


### [37] [VLA Adaptation with Real and Simulated Data: A Case Study on Robotic Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12676)
*Matěj Kripner,Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了VLA模型在精细操作任务微调中的数据量、训练更新、检查点选择与仿真数据利用问题，并发现学习率冷却和仿真预训练关键，而多种自我改进方法未优于普通行为克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA基础模型需针对每个下游任务和机器人微调，实践中缺乏关于所需数据量、训练更新次数、检查点选择以及能否用仿真降低真实数据采集成本的明确指导。

Method: 在SO-101机械臂将国际象棋王从e4移动到a5等仅由格子名称指定的任务上微调π0.5，比较不同数据集规模和更新预算，使用遥操作示教及随机化脚本专家生成的仿真示教；在真实机械臂上用最多256条遥操作示教微调，并以每个策略25次试验评估10个策略。

Result: 仿真中成功率随数据和更新增加；短学习率冷却最多提升31个百分点且对检查点比较必要；验证损失和动作误差不适合模型选择。真实实验中，仿真示教预训练显著有帮助，真实数据稀缺时最明显。负结果：用策略自身rollout进行过滤行为克隆、RECAP式优势条件、自动教练纠正或残差强化学习，均未较普通行为克隆带来可测提升。

Conclusion: 该研究表明VLA微调需重视学习率调度与检查点选择标准，仿真示教可有效缓解真实数据不足，但在该设置下多种自我改进/强化学习方案未显现优势；作者释放模拟器、专家和真实示教数据。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are trained as general-purpose foundation models for robot learning, and require fine-tuning for each downstream task and robot. This raises practical questions such as how much data and training updates are needed, how checkpoints should be selected, and whether simulation can be leveraged to alleviate the cost of real data collection. We study these questions on a task that requires both precise manipulation and spatial understanding of the scene. In this task, an SO-101 arm moves a chess king between two squares that are given only by their names, such as e4 and a5. We fine-tune $\pi_{0.5}$ with various dataset sizes and update budgets, using teleoperated demonstrations as well as simulated demonstrations from a randomized scripted expert that imitates the variability of human demonstrations. In simulation, the success rate scales with both data and updates. Importantly, we find that a short learning-rate cool-down adds up to 31 points and is necessary for comparing checkpoints, and that validation loss and action error are unreliable for model selection. On the real arm, we fine-tune on up to 256 teleoperated demonstrations and evaluate ten policies in 25 trials each. Partial results indicate that pretraining on the simulated demonstrations helps substantially, most clearly when real data is scarce. We also present a negative result, in which training on the policy's own rollouts with filtered behavior cloning, RECAP-style advantage conditioning, corrections from an automatic coach, or residual reinforcement learning did not yield a measurable improvement over plain behavior cloning in our setting. We release the simulator, the expert and the real demonstrations.

</details>


### [38] [Correctness Boundaries of Typed Plans for Natural-Language Robot Fleet Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12654)
*Artem Tuzov,Dmitrii Vizitei,Evgenii Shlomov,Senya Belykh,Anastasia Malakhova,Alexander Maloletov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文分析自然语言机器人集群控制中计划验证与执行报告实际能证明什么，并主张将有效分配、目标与报告结果同原始命令核对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证与执行报告可能只证明计划结构或部分命令要求，难以区分可执行动作变化与完成报告覆盖要求的变化，因此需要明确其证据边界。

Method: 在三级ROS 2实现中用20台仿真机器人追踪命令经类型化计划、确定性修正、集群协调和结果检查；构建44例离线基准检查计划结构与选定命令要求；在无响应schema的5种本地配置中测试，并分析选定18次仿真序列、保存的计划/日志及编队轨迹。

Result: 5种配置响应通过率为70.0%–87.3%；18次仿真均满足基于位姿的任务条件，含软件变更后两次成功重复以替换不完整运行；日志记录重复拾取分配和原任务区内目标调整；编队轨迹显示结构上接受但队形错误、路线被拒，以及重规划后到达报告遗漏请求机器人；搬运完成记录同一机器人拾取及后续投放区域存在。

Conclusion: 这些案例区分了可执行动作变化与完成报告要求变化，表明应针对原始命令核查有效分配、目标及报告结果。

Abstract: We examine what plan validation and execution reports establish in natural-language robot fleet control. Commands are traced through typed plans, deterministic corrections, fleet coordination and outcome checks in a three-level Robot Operating System 2 implementation with twenty simulated robots. An offline benchmark of 44 cases checks plan structure and selected command requirements. Across five local configurations without a response schema, 70.0-87.3% of responses pass. All runs in a selected 18-run simulation series meet their pose-based task conditions. This series includes two successful repeats that replaced incomplete runs after a software change. Saved plans and logs document duplicate pickup assignments and goal adjustments within the original task zones. Separate formation traces show a structurally accepted plan with the wrong formation shape and a rejected route, and an arrival report that omitted a requested robot after replanning. Carry completion records pickup and subsequent drop-off zone presence for the same robot. The cases distinguish changes to executable actions from changes to the requirements covered by a completion report. They motivate checking the effective assignments, goals and reported outcome against the original command.

</details>


### [39] [Modeling and Estimation of a Towed Marine System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12568)
*Josef Matouš,Simon Hoff,Damiano Varagnolo,Marius S. Rundhovde,Kristin Y. Pettersen,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究由两艘自主水面航行器（ASV）、脐带缆、拖曳体及尾环缆组成的拖曳海洋系统的建模与状态估计，并提出基于ASV机载传感器测量的降阶观测器和完美量测扩展卡尔曼滤波（PMEKF）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对拖曳海洋系统复杂耦合动力学与有限测量条件下的状态估计需求，旨在仅利用ASV自身传感器信息实现拖曳体及缆绳相关状态的估计。

Method: 采用集总质量法对缆绳和拖曳体建模，以兼顾关键动力学与计算可行性；通过基于Lyapunov的分析验证模型稳定性与收敛性；在此基础上提出降阶观测器和PMEKF两种状态估计方法。

Result: 数值仿真与实验结果验证了所提建模方法和两种状态估计方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 集总质量建模结合Lyapunov稳定性分析可为该类拖曳系统提供可计算且稳定的模型基础，降阶观测器与PMEKF能够在仅使用ASV机载传感器的条件下有效完成状态估计。

Abstract: This paper addresses the modeling and state estimation of a towed marine system consisting of two autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs), a pair of umbilical cables, towfish, and a trailing loop cable. Inspired by the existing literature, we employ a lumped-mass modeling approach for the cables and towfish, capturing the essential dynamics while maintaining computational tractability. A Lyapunov-based analysis is conducted to verify the stability and convergence properties of the proposed model. Building on this model, we propose two state estimation methods, namely a reduced-order observer and a perfect-measurement extended Kalman filter (PMEKF), using only the measurements from the ASVs' onboard sensors. Numerical simulations and experimental results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [40] [Galilean Robots, Aristotelian Sensors: Joint Spatiotemporal Trajectory Estimation and Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12526)
*Abhishek Goudar,Jonathan Kelly,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出基于特殊伽利略群与亚里士多德子群的联合轨迹估计与时空标定框架，将运动与时间偏移的耦合直接编码进群结构，从而避免单独参数化连续时间轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器状态估计依赖异构传感器间精确的空间与时间标定；现有方法通常将外参表示为刚体变换加标量时间偏移，使空间与时间参数通过分离机制进入估计，时间偏移需借助运动补偿、插值或连续时间轨迹在偏移时刻求值。

Method: 以特殊伽利略群表示机器人轨迹，以亚里士多德子群表示传感器外参，通过群结构直接建模运动与时间偏移的耦合；构建twist驱动和IMU驱动运动先验，兼容理想与非理想时钟，并使用相机、位置与测距测量进行评测。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明，在多种异构传感器配置下，该框架的标定与轨迹估计性能与已有方法相当。

Conclusion: 该简化框架在不牺牲性能的前提下提升了现有方法的易用性，为联合轨迹估计与时空标定提供了更简洁的建模范式。

Abstract: Multi-sensor state estimation requires accurate spatial and temporal calibration between heterogeneous sensors. Existing spatiotemporal calibration methods typically represent sensor extrinsic parameters as a rigid-body transformation together with a scalar temporal offset, causing the spatial and temporal parameters to enter the estimation problem through separate mechanisms. While the spatial transformation composes directly with the robot pose, accounting for the temporal offset requires evaluating the trajectory at a shifted time, typically through motion compensation, interpolation, or a continuous-time trajectory representation. In this work, we formulate joint trajectory estimation and spatiotemporal calibration using the special Galilean group for the robot trajectory and the Aristotelian subgroup for sensor extrinsics. This representation incorporates the coupling between motion and temporal offset directly through the group structure, avoiding the need for a separately parameterized continuous-time trajectory. We develop twist- and IMU-driven motion priors, accommodate both ideal and non-ideal clocks, and evaluate the framework with camera, position, and range measurements. Simulated and real experiments demonstrate calibration and trajectory-estimation performance comparable to established methods across heterogeneous sensor configurations. Our simplified framework therefore offers a quality-of-life improvement over existing methods without compromising performance.

</details>


### [41] [V3DSAM: Semantic-Geometric Loop Verification and Geometry-Anchored Persistent Instance Mapping for Feed-Forward Dense SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12452)
*Maolin Ye,Dongqing Zhao,Linyang Li,Shuai Xue,Yuan Liu,Yiyuan Wang,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V3DSAM 是一个开放词汇语义稠密 SLAM 系统，通过结合 VGGT-SLAM 2.0 的前馈几何估计与 SAM3 的开放词汇语义观测，在场景级和对象级建立物理对应，以解决长序列前馈稠密 SLAM 中跨视角关联不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列前馈稠密 SLAM 中的跨视角关联容易不可靠，现有方法往往依赖骨干网络内部特征或帧间时序 ID 传播，难以支撑稳健的长期关联。

Method: 系统融合 VGGT-SLAM 2.0 的前馈几何估计与 SAM3 的开放词汇语义观测；提出语义-几何双尺度回环验证，利用像素级双向重投影和对象级 3D 空间对应识别错误回环，且不访问骨干内部特征；提出几何锚定的持久实例建图，在优化后的全局坐标系中通过类别约束的 3D 连通性定义物理实例，并在不进行帧间身份传播的情况下分配全局 ID。

Result: 在 TUM RGB-D 评估中，所提出的验证器拒绝了全部四个错误回环候选；在长序列 ScanNet 实验中，实例身份一致性得到提升。

Conclusion: V3DSAM 降低了对骨干网络内部特征和时序 ID 传播的依赖，从而提升长期跨视角关联的稳健性。

Abstract: To address unreliable cross-view association in long-sequence feed-forward dense SLAM, this letter presents V3DSAM, an open-vocabulary semantic dense SLAM system that establishes physical correspondences at both the scene and object levels. The system combines feed-forward geometric estimates from VGGT-SLAM 2.0 with open-vocabulary semantic observations from SAM3. We introduce semantic-geometric dual-scale loop verification, which identifies false loop closures using pixel-level bidirectional reprojection and object-level 3D spatial correspondences, without accessing backbone-internal features. We further propose geometry-anchored persistent instance mapping, which defines physical instances through class-constrained 3D connectivity in an optimized global coordinate system and assigns global IDs without inter-frame identity propagation. The proposed verifier rejects all four false loop candidates in our TUM RGB-D evaluation, while long-sequence ScanNet experiments show improved instance identity consistency. Thus, V3DSAM reduces dependence on internal backbone features and temporal ID propagation for robust long-term cross-view association.

</details>


### [42] [Bridging Task Planning and Execution for Language-Conditioned Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12409)
*Emanuele Cuzzocrea,Giuseppina Iannotti,Luca Rossini,Arturo Laurenzi,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本视觉语言框架，将执行可行性推理融入双臂人形机器人的自然语言操作任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实家庭与物流场景存在杂乱环境，双臂人形平台还需协调两个机械臂的交互与运动学约束，因此需同时推理任务目标与物理执行可行性。

Method: 采用结构化场景表示联合语义物体关系与执行约束，选择障碍物搬移和执行方式并保持任务语义；将兼容动作组织为带显式前置/后置条件的并行双臂阶段；层次化验证器在感知不确定性下评估条件，并利用阶段级执行历史支持局部恢复或定向重规划。

Result: 在CENTAURO混合轮腿人形机器人上覆盖九个语言条件操作场景，任务成功率高于非执行感知基线；在专门失败场景中，层次化验证比始终重规划以更少模型查询恢复执行失败。

Conclusion: 将执行推理直接结合到任务规划中可提升杂乱环境下双臂人形机器人语言条件操作的成功率与失败恢复效率。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly expected to execute natural-language manipulation tasks in real-world environments. This is particularly relevant in household assistance and logistics, where clutter requires reasoning about both task objectives and execution feasibility. This challenge becomes even more pronounced for bimanual humanoid platforms, where coordinating two manipulators introduces additional interaction and kinematic constraints. We present a zero-shot vision-language framework for execution-aware bimanual manipulation that jointly reasons about task objectives and physical feasibility. Planning is grounded in a structured scene representation that combines semantic object relations with execution constraints, enabling the selection of obstacle relocations and execution choices while preserving task semantics. Compatible actions are organised into parallel bimanual stages with explicit pre- and post-conditions. A hierarchical validator evaluates these conditions under perceptual uncertainty and uses stage-level execution history to support local recovery or targeted replanning. Experiments on the CENTAURO hybrid wheeled-legged humanoid robot across nine language-conditioned manipulation scenarios show higher task success than a non-execution-aware baseline. In dedicated failure scenarios, hierarchical validation recovers from execution failures with fewer model queries than always replanning. These results demonstrate the benefits of combining execution reasoning directly into task planning. Videos and additional material are available on the Project Webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [43] [PCTac: Mechanochromic Photonic-Crystal Tactile Sensor for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12383)
*Ruilin Zhang,Xuanshuo Liu,Junzhe Dai,Kefei Wu,Xuhao Qin,Ziyuan Jiao,Wanlin Li,Yurui Qu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源机械致变色光子晶体指尖PCTac，以密集色调场作为触觉表征，并接入Diffusion Policy在真实机器人操作中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉传感器多通过成像柔性界面变形感知接触，力相关信息通常需借助标定、几何重建或学习映射间接推断；机械致变色光子晶体可将局部应力直接编码为空间分辨的结构色场，但较少用于闭环机器人学习。

Method: 设计PCTac机械致变色光子晶体指尖，将密集色调场直接作为触觉观测；开展加载表征以验证法向载荷与色调响应的可重复关系；将该表征集成到Diffusion Policy，并在三个真实机器人操作任务上评估。

Result: 在90次物理 rollout 中，PCTac Hue成功68次（75.6%），高于PCTac RGB的60次（66.7%）、Wedge RGB的57次（63.3%）和纯视觉的29次（32.2%）；PCTac Hue在每个评估任务上均取得最高成功率，包括扰动试管插入任务16/30，优于触觉RGB基线的13/30和纯视觉的1/30。

Conclusion: 结果表明机械致变色结构色可作为紧凑、载荷敏感的触觉表征，用于机器人学习和闭环操作。

Abstract: Modern visuotactile sensors primarily sense contact by imaging the deformation of a compliant interface. Using techniques such as photometric stereo, they can recover detailed contact geometry, often represented as surface normals or depth. However, force-related information typically still needs to be inferred indirectly through calibration, geometric reconstruction, or learned mappings. Mechanochromic photonic-crystal (PC) materials offer an alternative physical encoding mechanism: contact loading changes their periodic microstructure and spectral response, directly transforming local stress into a spatially resolved structural-color field. Despite this potential, PC materials have rarely been explored as visuotactile sensors for closed-loop robot learning. We present PCTac, an open-source mechanochromic PC fingertip that uses a dense hue field as its tactile observation. Loading characterization demonstrates a repeatable relationship between the applied normal load and the observed hue response. We further integrate this representation directly into a Diffusion Policy and evaluate it on three real-robot manipulation tasks. Across 90 physical rollouts, PCTac Hue achieves 68 successes (75.6\%), compared with 60 (66.7\%) for PCTac RGB, 57 (63.3\%) for Wedge RGB, and 29 (32.2\%) for vision-only. PCTac Hue also achieves the highest observed success rate on each evaluated task, including Perturbed Test-Tube Insertion, where it achieves 16/30 successes versus 13/30 for tactile RGB baselines and 1/30 for vision-only. These results demonstrate the potential of mechanochromic structural color as a compact, load-sensitive tactile representation for robot learning and closed-loop manipulation. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [44] [AutoVLA-D: Learn a Trajectory-Geometric Condition from Reasoning for Autonomous Driving VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12382)
*Yuguang Yang,Zhewen Tan,Canyu Chen,Linlin Yang,Cheng Chi,Chunyang Liu,Kehua Sheng,Jinyu Yang,Baochang Zhan,Bo Zhang,Yan Wang,Xianbin Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AutoVLA-D（Double），将离散轨迹几何条件 TrajCond 引入 VLA 规划，在自回归生成中同时输出离散与连续轨迹，以提升最终连续轨迹预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 VLA 规划通常直接预测连续轨迹作为最终输出，但驾驶指令更多描述动作而非自车状态，导致对最终连续轨迹的直接条件约束较弱。

Method: 将 TrajCond 表示为 1D-Brohan 差分动作轨迹，并采用两阶段训练框架，在最终连续轨迹预测前学习该离散轨迹几何条件；通过自回归同时生成离散和数值轨迹，形成 AutoVLA-D。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest、nuScenes 和 NeuroNCAP 上，离散轨迹作为直接输出较弱，但作为 TrajCond 更有效，并在闭环与开环驾驶基准上一致提升最终连续轨迹预测。

Conclusion: 离散轨迹更适合作为 VLA 规划的条件而非最终输出；TrajCond 与 AutoVLA-D 能有效增强连续轨迹预测，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autoregressive VLA planning usually predicts continuous trajectories as the final output, but driving instructions often describe actions rather than ego states, which weakens direct conditioning on the final continuous trajectory. We address this gap with TrajCond, which introduces a discrete trajectory-geometric condition for VLA planning. Specifically, we represent TrajCond as a 1D-Brohan differential action trajectory and learn it with a two-stage training framework before final continuous trajectory prediction. With the simultaneous autoregressive generation of discrete and numeric trajectories, we name our method AutoVLA-D (Double). Experiments on NAVSIM navtest, nuScenes, and NeuroNCAP show that discrete trajectories are weaker as direct outputs but more effective as TrajCond, consistently improving final continuous trajectory prediction across closed-loop and open-loop driving benchmarks. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [Safety-Constrained Intelligent Control of a 2-DOF Robotic Manipulator Using Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12378)
*Gabriel da Silva Lima,Made Widhi Surya Atman,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种共享RBF神经网络补偿与辅助学习校正的CBF滤波控制框架，用于参数不确定下二连杆机械臂的约束跟踪控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBF滤波器依赖动态模型，但其参数和外力不确定时模型易不准确，从而影响约束满足与跟踪性能。

Method: 设计RBF神经网络补偿器，其输出同时供笛卡尔跟踪控制器和关节空间CBF滤波器使用；引入由请求力矩与实际施加力矩之差驱动的辅助状态来校正学习信号，以降低滤波器干预对自适应学习的直接影响；在二连杆机械臂上开展约束调节、消融、匹配不确定性与扰动试验，以及工作范围和时序研究。

Result: 结果表明，所提控制器在参数不确定性下改善了跟踪性能，同时维持关节、速度和力矩约束；共享补偿与辅助校正均对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: 共享神经网络补偿结合辅助学习校正可有效缓解CBF滤波器对不准确动态模型的依赖，在保证约束的同时提升不确定条件下的跟踪效果。

Abstract: Control barrier function (CBF) filters depend on dynamic models that can be inaccurate when their parameters and external forces are uncertain. This paper investigates a radial basis function (RBF) neural network compensator whose output is shared by a Cartesian tracking controller and a joint-space CBF filter. An auxiliary state driven by the difference between requested and applied torques corrects the learning signal, reducing the direct influence of filter intervention on adaptation. A two-link manipulator is evaluated through constrained regulation, ablations, matched uncertainty and disturbance trials, and operating-range and timing studies. The results demonstrate that the proposed controller improves tracking while maintaining joint, velocity, and torque limits under parameter uncertainty, and that both the shared compensation and the auxiliary correction contribute to this improvement.

</details>


### [46] [UniOMA: Unified Optimal-Transport Multi-Modal Structural Alignment for Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12351)
*Xinrui Zu,Mark Hoogendoorn,Kevin Sebastian Luck,Shujian Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在对比损失上加入几何感知 Gromov-Wasserstein 距离正则的多模态对齐方法，通过动态学习的 GW 重心减少跨模态结构差异，在机器人多模态感知任务中提升结构对齐与下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多通过条件或高阶统计依赖对齐模态，未显式考虑跨模态的结构一致性；机器人中的力/触觉等模态数据还存在样本密度差异问题。

Method: 在现有对比损失基础上增加基于最优传输的几何感知 GW 距离正则：编码各模态的模态内几何结构，并利用动态学习的 GW 重心降低跨模态结构差异，从而在实例级对比对齐之外促进结构一致性；该方法可嵌入多数 InfoNCE 类目标。

Result: 在五个机器人基准和多种下游任务（包括机器人与环境状态预测）上，该方法相比最先进的多模态对比基线改善了结构对齐和下游性能，并适用于力、触觉等较少探索且样本密度变化大的模态。

Conclusion: 将 GW 正则与对比学习结合可有效增强多模态表示的结构一致性和泛化对齐能力，且具有良好的可集成性。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable and well-aligned multimodal representations remains a core challenge in robot learning. While recent approaches have attempted to align modalities by modeling conditional or higher-order statistical dependencies, they do not explicitly consider the structural coherence across modalities. In this work, we propose a novel multimodal alignment method that augments existing contrastive losses with a geometry-aware Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) distance-based regularization. To this end, we encode intra-modal geometry for each modality and reduce cross-modal structural discrepancies through a dynamically learned GW barycenter, thereby encouraging structural consistency in addition to instance-level contrastive alignment. We apply this optimal-transport-based alignment strategy to robot perception tasks involving underexplored modalities such as force and tactile signals, where modality data often exhibit varying sample densities. Extensive experiments on five robotic benchmarks show that our method improves structural alignment and downstream performance over state-of-the-art multimodal contrastive baselines across a wide range of downstream tasks, including robot and environment state prediction. Moreover, our GW-based regularization can be seamlessly integrated into most InfoNCE-like objectives.

</details>


### [47] [ExoStack: A Modular and Reconfigurable Software Framework for Wearable Robotics Using ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12306)
*Debadrata Sarkar,Upamanyu Ray,Soumen Sen,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExoStack是一个模块化ROS 2框架，用于打通可穿戴机器人从体表传感、步态解释、辅助生成到执行器驱动的软件链，并支持个性化参数与测量响应的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴机器人需要将体表传感、步态解释、辅助生成和执行驱动连接成完整软件链；个性化还要求每次测量响应都与产生该响应的辅助参数保持关联。

Method: 提出ExoStack，跨嵌入式枢纽与主机运行；其ESA库将ESP32配置为microROS传感与执行枢纽；ExoCore以配置实例化节点提供多枢纽同步、步态事件检测、轮廓生成、运动规划、代价评估和人机闭环优化；PASE桌面应用用于编辑配置、启动处理阶段并可视化ROS 2话题；硬件驱动和算法插件可通过定义接口扩展而无需修改通用处理节点。

Result: 在三种嵌入式计算机上表征了枢纽与规划器时序，并在两套气动外骨骼上演示了复用。

Conclusion: ExoStack为可穿戴机器人提供模块化、可复用且可扩展的ROS 2软件链，支持个性化控制流程及驱动/算法插件集成。

Abstract: Wearable robots depend on a software chain that links body-worn sensing to gait interpretation, assistance generation, and actuation. Personalization further requires each measured response to remain associated with the assistance parameters that generated it. This paper presents ExoStack, a modular ROS 2 framework for implementing that chain across embedded hubs and a host computer. Its Embedded Sensing and Actuation (ESA) library configures ESP32 boards as microROS sensing and actuation hubs. ExoCore supplies multi-hub synchronization, gait-event detection, profile generation, motion planning, cost evaluation, and human-in-the-loop (HIL) optimization as configuration-instantiated nodes. The PASE desktop application edits configurations, launches the processing stages, and visualizes ROS 2 topics. Hardware drivers and algorithm plugins can be added through defined interfaces without modifying the generic processing nodes. We characterize hub and planner timing on three embedded computers and demonstrate reuse on two pneumatic exosuits.

</details>


### [48] [Be My Eye: Learning to See Beyond a Robot's Own Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12295)
*Baifu Huang,Shilin Fang,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任务导向的主动上下文共享框架，将机器人观测压缩为任务相关的紧凑表示，并在预训练VLA策略中进行双向交换，以有限通信预算提升多机器人协作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中的机器人存在部分可观测性，多机器人协作时需要利用伙伴的互补观测；但被动传输原始观测或通用视觉特征效率低，应根据任务目标与执行进度选择性编码任务相关信息。

Method: 采用两阶段训练：第一阶段将机载观测与任务指令联合编码为紧凑上下文表示，并通过任务导向未来预测和软任务进度对应学习进行协作无关压缩；第二阶段将学习到的表示集成到预训练Vision-Language-Action策略中，实现双向上下文交换，各机器人结合伙伴互补信息与本地观测生成动作，并用动作监督联合优化上下文表示与策略。

Result: 受控实验表明在受限通信预算下取得显著性能提升；三项真实世界实验验证了该框架在复杂双机器人任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法推动从被动视觉传输转向主动、任务导向的上下文共享，使信息共享直接服务于协同决策。

Abstract: Robots operating in the real world are inherently subject to partial observability, particularly in multi-robot collaboration, where individual observations may be insufficient for effective task execution. A key challenge is leveraging complementary observations from partner robots to support collaborative decision-making. We argue that information sharing should go beyond passively transmitting raw observations or generic visual features. Instead, robots should selectively encode task-relevant information according to task objectives and execution progress. To this end, we propose a task-oriented active context sharing framework that transforms robot observations into compact representations, preserving critical information under constrained communication budgets. The framework adopts a two-stage training paradigm. In the first stage, onboard observations and task instructions are jointly encoded into compact context representations. Task-oriented future prediction and soft task-progress correspondence learning guide this collaboration-agnostic compression, preserving information about task objectives and execution progress. In the second stage, the learned representations are integrated into a pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy, enabling bidirectional context exchange. Each robot combines complementary partner information with local observations to guide action generation. Action supervision jointly refines the context representations and policy, aligning information sharing with collaborative decision-making. Controlled experiments demonstrate substantial performance gains under constrained communication budgets, while three real-world experiments validate the framework in complex dual-robot tasks. These results highlight a shift from passive visual transmission toward active, task-oriented context sharing that directly supports collaborative decision-making.

</details>


### [49] [VLA-MOPD: On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Task VLA Policy Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12286)
*Yongxuan Lv,Zhen Fang,Lichen Man,Hao Li,Pu Li,Hengyi Wang,Langfeng Cao,Lin Chen,Jie Huang,Xiu Su,Shan You,Fei Wang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VLA-MOPD，一种用于整合流匹配视觉-语言-动作（VLA）专家的多教师同策略蒸馏协议，在机器人执行轨迹上对齐冻结教师并监督中间 ODE 状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能需要智能体将语言、观测和动作结合并保留技能，但任务特定的 VLA 后训练会形成分散专家；混合任务强化学习奖励稀疏，离线蒸馏覆盖不足，端点蒸馏又无法监督中间动作生成状态。

Method: 先获得任务或任务套件专家，再让共享学生在机器人 rollout 的每个访问观测及流匹配 ODE 中间状态查询按指令路由的冻结教师；用分离的每流步转移目标，通过掩码局部匹配损失监督有效动作块，并可选加入参考策略项以保留其他任务能力。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，从每任务一条轨迹开始，VLA-MOPD 将 mean success-once 从 75.4% 提升到 98.9%，高于端点动作蒸馏的 98.15%；RoboTwin 2.0 平均成功率从 73.0% 提升到 89.3%；Piper 机器人两轮整合将平均成功率从 70.0% 提升到 90.0%。在近似匹配的 Spatial 适配研究中，参考匹配将 Object 套件下降限制在 3.0 个百分点，而无参考项为 13.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: VLA-MOPD 能有效将多个流匹配 VLA 专家整合为共享学生，在多个机器人基准和真实机器人上显著提升成功率，并通过参考策略项缓解其他任务能力遗忘。

Abstract: Physical intelligence requires embodied agents to ground language in observations and execute actions while retaining useful skills. Vision-language-action (VLA) policies offer a shared interface, but task-specific post-training often produces separate specialists. Consolidation is difficult: mixed-task reinforcement learning provides sparse trajectory-level credit, while offline distillation may miss student-visited observations and endpoint distillation does not supervise intermediate action-generation states. We introduce VLA Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (VLA-MOPD), a protocol for consolidating flow-matching VLA specialists. After acquiring task or suite experts, a shared student collects robot rollouts and queries the instruction-routed frozen teacher at each visited observation and intermediate ordinary differential equation (ODE) state. Detached per-flow-step transition targets supervise valid action chunks through a masked local matching loss; an optional reference-policy term supports retention on other tasks. Starting from one trajectory per LIBERO task, VLA-MOPD raises mean success-once from 75.4% to 98.9%, compared with 98.15% for endpoint action distillation. It improves mean RoboTwin 2.0 success from 73.0% to 89.3%; on a Piper robot, two consolidation rounds improve average success from 70.0% to 90.0%. In a near-matched Spatial-adaptation study, reference matching limits the Object suite decrease to 3.0 points, versus 13.2 without the reference term.

</details>


### [50] [Hierarchical Multi-Channel Tip-Over Prediction via Isolated Temporal Models for Planetary Rover Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12261)
*Siddhant Shete,Raul Dominguez,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于隔离时序学习的分层多通道行星车倾覆预测框架，融合多 horizon TCN 的渐进失稳预测与 GRU 前向动力学残差检测，经决策级融合和优先级仲裁输出分级安全动作，并在 Rock/Orocos RTT 中以 100 Hz 实时部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星车在非结构化地形中运行且通信延迟使人工实时干预不可行，可靠倾覆预防至关重要；现有学习型方法通常只优化单一预测目标，难以同时捕捉渐进失稳演化与突发地形扰动。

Method: 构建分层多通道倾覆预测框架：多 horizon TCN 从 IMU 惯性测量中学习渐进失稳模式；基于 GRU 的前向动力学模型预测名义运动，并通过预测残差检测意外动态偏差；两类互补信号经决策级融合和优先级仲裁生成早期预警与紧急制动等分级安全动作；系统作为 Orocos RTT 安全组件集成于 Rock 框架，在行星车软件栈上以 100 Hz 运行。

Result: 在异构地形采集的 IMU 样本实验中达到 97.58% 事件预测覆盖率与 1.82 s 中位提前时间，优于单一预测通道。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将隔离时序模型与分层决策融合相结合，可为不确定地形下行星车实时安全提供鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable tip-over prevention is critical for planetary rovers operating in unstructured environments where communication delays prevent real-time human intervention. Existing learning-based approaches typically optimize a single prediction objective, limiting their ability to capture both gradual instability evolution and abrupt terrain disturbances. This work presents a hierarchical multi-channel tip-over prediction framework based on isolated temporal learning. A multi-horizon Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) learns progressive instability patterns from inertial measurements, while a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU)-based forward dynamics model predicts nominal rover motion and detects unexpected dynamic deviations through prediction residuals. These complementary signals are integrated through decision-level fusion and priority-based arbitration to generate graded safety actions, including early warning and emergency braking. The proposed system is deployed as an Orocos Real-Time Toolkit (RTT)-based safety component within the Rock robotics framework, operating at 100 Hz on a planetary rover software stack. Experiments on Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) samples collected across heterogeneous terrains demonstrate 97.58% event prediction coverage with a 1.82s median lead time, outperforming individual prediction channels. The results validate that isolated temporal models combined through hierarchical decision fusion provide a robust solution for real-time rover safety in uncertain terrain.

</details>


### [51] [A Game-Theoretic MPC Planner for Cooperative and Adversarial Drone Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12226)
*Zdeněk Bouček,Miroslav Flídr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并评估一种用于无人机协作目标到达与对抗追逃的博弈论模型预测控制规划器，结合3D预测、个体目标、可选共享分离约束和轨迹跟踪接口，并通过仿真与实飞验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多无人机在合作与对抗场景中的冲突消解和实时规划需求，评估博弈求解器与计算性能，为后续控制器开发提供基础。

Method: 构建博弈论MPC规划器；比较合作博弈求解器寻找可行无冲突轨迹的能力；计算研究分析无人机数量、预测时域和move blocking对计算时间与数值接受率的影响；move blocking将控制输入在连续预测步分组保持恒定以减少独立变量；在PyBullet中仿真协作目标到达和追逃，并用实验室实飞验证轨迹跟踪接口。

Result: 合作博弈求解器比较给出冲突消解可行性；计算研究量化无人机数量、预测时域、move blocking对计算时间和数值接受率的影响，move blocking降低计算时间；计时测量确定测试配置下实时规划的实用边界；仿真展示协作目标到达与追逃，实飞验证轨迹跟踪接口。

Conclusion: 该规划器可作为进一步评估和控制器开发的基础。

Abstract: We present and evaluate a game-theoretic model predictive control planner for cooperative goal reaching and adversarial pursuit-evasion with drones. The planner combines 3D prediction, individual objectives, optional shared separation constraints and trajectory-tracking interfaces. A comparison of cooperative game solvers assesses their ability to find feasible trajectories that resolve conflicts. A computational study quantifies how number of drones, prediction horizon and move blocking affect computation time and numerical acceptance. Move blocking holds control inputs constant over groups of prediction steps, reducing the number of independent control variables and lowering computation time. Timing measurements identify the practical limits of real-time planning in the tested configurations. PyBullet simulations demonstrate cooperative goal reaching and pursuit-evasion. A laboratory flight complements the simulations by validating the trajectory-tracking interfaces on physical drones. The planner can serve as a basis for further evaluation and controller development.

</details>


### [52] [Graph-Based RL for Sequential Rendezvous Planning with Multiple Patrolling USVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12205)
*Seung Woo Han,Hyeon Bin Yeo,Seung Heon Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向多无人水面艇巡逻任务，提出融合栅格路径规划、代理航行时间预测与图强化学习的指挥艇会合调度框架，以最小化任务完成时间和相对优先截止期的总延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多USV巡逻中，指挥USV需依次与各巡逻USV会合，需同时解决运动可行路径规划与优先级感知的会合排序问题，并降低计算开销。

Method: 采用考虑洋流与通过多阶段机动近似转艏率约束的栅格路径规划，并用轨迹跟踪仿真验证可行性；构建可行转移图表示候选会合事件及时间可行转移，利用图神经网络学习时空会合关系；用基于洋流与运动紧凑特征的代理模型预测航行时间，避免FTG构建和策略训练中的重复路径搜索。

Result: 基于栅格路径规划的评估显示，相较贪心、MLP-RL和2步前瞻贪心，平均目标分别降低10.5%、3.5%和1.5%，且计算时间约为后者的1/35；在未见优先级层级设置下仍优于MLP-RL，且舰队规模增大时增益更大。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效实现运动可行、优先级感知且计算高效的指挥USV会合路径规划与排序，并具备良好泛化性和可扩展性。

Abstract: In patrol missions involving multiple unmanned surface vehicles (USVs), a designated command USV can support fleet management by sequentially rendezvousing with each patrolling USV. These operations require motion-feasible route planning and priority-aware rendezvous sequencing, with the objective of minimizing mission completion time and total tardiness relative to priority-dependent due dates. We propose a framework combining grid-based path planning, surrogate travel-time prediction, and graph-based reinforcement learning. The path planner accounts for ocean currents and approximates turn-rate constraints through multi-stage maneuvers, with path feasibility empirically validated through trajectory-tracking simulations. For rendezvous sequencing, a Feasible Transition Graph (FTG) represents candidate rendezvous events and temporally feasible transitions, enabling a graph neural network to exploit spatiotemporal rendezvous relationships for policy learning. A surrogate predicts travel times from compact current and motion features to avoid repeated path searches during FTG construction and policy training. Evaluation using grid-based path planning shows statistically significant average objective reductions of 10.5%, 3.5%, and 1.5% compared with greedy, MLP-RL, and 2-step lookahead greedy, respectively. The policy achieves these improvements while requiring approximately 1/35 of the latter's computation time. It maintains its advantage over MLP-RL under unseen priority-tier settings, with larger gains as fleet size increases.

</details>


### [53] [Embodied First Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12177)
*Kangrui Wang,Qineng Wang,Changhe Chen,Pingyue Zhang,Chi Gui,Feishi Wang,Canyu Chen,Wenli Xiao,Xiaoyu Huang,Tonghe Zhang,Linjie Li,Zihan Wang,Minyeong Kim,Yiyou Sun,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou,Qi Zhu,Han Liu,Eric P. Xing,Huaxiu Yao,Zhiwen Fan,Yuejiang Liu,Dawn Song,Pieter Abbeel,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Yuke Zhu,Guanya Shi,Ruohan Zhang,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向编码智能体的具身机器人统一评测框架 Embodied First Exam，覆盖 47 种机器人、1,156 个任务与三种评测设置；最强智能体在 456 个核心任务的 Standard 设置下成功率仅 34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准主要面向视觉运动策略学习，尚不清楚编码智能体能否在物理世界约束下可靠控制多样机器人，因此需要一个智能体原生、可自选控制方式的综合评测基准。

Method: 构建统一评测框架，允许编码智能体选择直接控制、编写代码控制或训练策略控制；包含 35 个套件、1,156 个任务、47 种机器人，其中 9 个新套件覆盖空间谜题、灵巧操作、多机器人交互、动态全身控制和 sim-to-sim 工程；每任务支持 Standard、Realtime 和 Privileged 三种设置，并对失败尝试所处阶段进行标注与跨智能体、设置和阶段类别比较。

Result: 在 456 个核心任务上，8 个智能体中最强的 GPT-6 Astra in Codex 在 Standard 设置下成功率为 34%；Privileged 设置提供完整模拟器状态和无限重置，其成功率可作为近似上界；通过失败阶段标注分析了智能体失败位置及不同条件下的差异。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在多样机器人物理约束任务中仍存在显著能力差距，Embodied First Exam 可作为其进入物理世界前的首轮综合考试，并为定位失败阶段和后续改进提供依据。

Abstract: Early successes on agentic robotics point to a potential paradigm shift toward using coding agents to control robots. Existing robotics benchmarks are built around visuomotor policy learning, leaving it open whether coding agents can reliably control diverse robots under physical-world constraints. We therefore introduce the Embodied First Exam, a unified, comprehensive, and agent-native evaluation framework where coding agents can choose their own control approach: direct control, code to control, or train a policy to control. We hope it serves as a first exam for coding agents before they enter the physical world, with 1,156 tasks in 35 suites on 47 robots, including 9 new suites that go beyond pick-and-place to spatial puzzles, dexterous manipulation, multi-robot interaction, dynamic whole-body control, and sim-to-sim engineering. Each task can be run in up to three evaluation settings. The Standard Setting mimics the real world: the agent receives only sensor observations and, by default, cannot reset the environment. Its Realtime variant keeps the environment running while the agent reasons. The Privileged Setting gives the agent access to the full simulator state and unlimited resets, allowing it to search for an oracle solution for training purposes. Success rates in this setting serve as an approximate upper bound. On the 456 core tasks, the strongest of eight agents, GPT-6 Astra in Codex, achieves a success rate of 34% in the Standard Setting, leaving much room for improvement. To see where agents fail, we label the stage at which failed attempts go wrong and compare failures across agents, settings, and stage categories.

</details>


### [54] [GeoMind: A Benchmark for Spatial Understanding in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12175)
*Jinghe Wang,Xinrui Cao,Duo Wu,Chenghao Gu,Yong Zhong,Linjia Kang,Tianyi Xiong,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoMind 是一个面向桌面操作的结构化空间理解基准，包含空间推理与空间记忆任务，用于系统评估具身智能体的空间能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界机器人操作需要稳健的空间理解，包括在复杂布局中识别目标（空间推理）以及适应交互中持续变化的场景（空间记忆）；现有具身基准常通过强视觉线索绕过空间推理，且多聚焦静态场景，难以评估空间记忆能力。

Method: 提出 GeoMind 基准，围绕空间推理和空间记忆组织 54 个评估任务变体，并提供可扩展的自动化流水线，用于生成和标注任务实例，确保目标可验证且交互可执行。

Result: 对多种具身智能体的广泛评估显示其在空间理解上存在一致弱点：难以在空间变化后跟踪目标身份，也难以将关系或几何约束转化为可执行动作。

Conclusion: GeoMind 揭示了现有具身智能体在结构化空间理解上的不足，并公开基准以促进相关研究与评估。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation has advanced rapidly in mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, reliable manipulation in open-world environments requires robust spatial understanding: the ability to identify targets in complex layouts (spatial reasoning) and adapt to scenes that continuously evolve through interaction (spatial memory). Yet existing embodied benchmarks fall short in evaluating spatial understanding: they often introduce strong visual cues that bypass spatial reasoning, and focus on static scenes that offer little insight into an agent's capacity for spatial memory. To address this gap, we introduce GeoMind, a systematic benchmark for structured spatial understanding through tabletop manipulation. GeoMind comprises 54 evaluated task variants organized around spatial reasoning and spatial memory. It also incorporates a scalable pipeline that automates the generation and annotation of task instances with verified targets and executable interactions. Extensive evaluations across diverse embodied agents reveal their consistent weaknesses in spatial understanding. Our findings highlight that these agents struggle to track identities after spatial changes and convert relational or geometric constraints into executable actions. We release the GeoMind benchmark at this https URL .

</details>


### [55] [BioMARS 2.0: a robotic biologist for formulating and adapting biological experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12139)
*Yibo Qiu,Zan Huang,Yilin Qiao,Ziyang Ling,Yi Wang,Zhengyang Lu,Hangke Peng,Haoliang Ye,Ao Tian,Shu'ang Sun,Handi Liu,Yuqing Long,Mohan Yang,Kan Ding,Zhenzhen Zhou,Di Wu,Yu Xiong,Yaxuan Han,Jun'ao Song,Haoran Zhang,Ronald X Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BioMARS 是一个人在回路的多智能体机器人系统，将知识引导的实验决策、机器人执行与生物反馈连接起来，在实验流程、培养参数和生物干预三个层面实现反馈驱动的自动化生物实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物实验不能简化为单一优化问题，需要在流程转化、条件适应和干预选择等多个抽象层级做决策；仅自动化执行不足以决定下一步实验及如何根据生物反馈调整。

Method: 作者提出 BioMARS 多智能体机器人系统：流程层面生成有文献依据且可追踪的行动方案；参数层面利用机器人视网膜类器官培养的图像表型选择后续实验条件；干预层面基于生物医学证据提名药物重定位候选。

Result: BioMARS 在四个模型后端上提升专家评审的协议质量评分；在三轮实验中，基于图像表型选择条件使后续轮次报告评分更高；提名药物重定位候选并推动六个候选进入研究人员湿实验测试。

Conclusion: 研究展示了反馈驱动的机器人生物实验框架，可支持实验流程、培养条件和生物干预等多层级决策。

Abstract: Biological experimentation is not reducible to a single optimization problem, but requires decisions at multiple levels of abstraction: translating scientific knowledge into experimental procedures, adapting culture conditions to measured phenotypes and selecting interventions that test biological hypotheses. Automating experimental execution does not, by itself, determine which experiments should be performed next or how they should change in response to biological feedback. Here we introduce BioMARS, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent robotic system that connects knowledge-guided experimental decision-making with robotic execution and biological feedback. At the procedural level, BioMARS generated literature-grounded protocols and traceable action plans, improving expert-reviewed protocol quality scores across four model backends. At the parametric level, BioMARS used image-derived phenotypes from robotic retinal organoid culture to select subsequent experimental conditions, with higher reported scores in later rounds of the three-round experiment. At the intervention level, BioMARS nominated drug-repurposing candidates from biomedical evidence and advanced six candidates to wet-lab testing by researchers. Together, these studies demonstrate feedback-driven robotic biological experimentation within a framework that supports decisions about experimental procedures, culture conditions and biological interventions.

</details>


### [56] [LHNav: Long-Horizon Humanoid Navigation via Multirate Coordination and Physics-Grounded Action Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12136)
*Xueyang Wang,Zehong Shen,Dingkun Wei,Huaijin Pi,Changpeng Wang,Xing Zhu,Hujun Bao,Yujun Shen,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LHNav异步系统，将长时程语言指令下的多目的地人形导航分解为任务推理、局部运动生成与全身执行三个时间尺度角色，实现连续、自然且路线忠实的长时程导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目的地长时程语言导航要求机器人跨连续目标保持进度，并在狭窄空间中持续生成自然稳定的全身运动；现有任务分解不能保证执行全程进度，而路点导航难以保证自然且路线忠实的全身运动。

Method: 核心思想是三类互补角色：Brain负责任务推理，Cerebellum负责局部运动生成，Brainstem负责全身执行；LHNav以进度与到达反馈异步协调导航目标、VLA运动token预测和全身控制。为训练Cerebellum，构建物理 grounded 数据引擎，沿碰撞感知路线提升运动参考的物理跟踪，并将演示与渲染视觉观测配对。

Result: 仿真中导航VLA在单目标成功率和运动平滑性上优于OmniNav与DualVLN，碰撞和跌倒更少；仅用单目标演示训练的VLA使LHNav在长时程任务达到75.56%成功率。Unitree G1实机部署展示了单目标导航及真实环境中的零样本长时程任务执行，包括物体重新摆放布局。

Conclusion: LHNav通过多时间尺度异步协调与物理 grounded 运动数据训练，使任务规划与连续运动并行，并将单目标导航扩展到多目的地长时程导航，兼顾自然、路线忠实的全身运动与实机泛化能力。

Abstract: We study humanoid navigation across multiple destinations under long-horizon language instructions. This task is challenging because robots must maintain progress across successive goals while continuously producing natural, stable whole-body motion through confined spaces. However, task decomposition in existing methods does not by itself ensure progress throughout execution, while waypoint-based navigation does not guarantee natural, route-faithful whole-body motion. Our key idea is to organize navigation into three complementary roles operating at different time scales: a Brain for task reasoning, a Cerebellum for local motion generation, and a Brainstem for whole-body execution. We realize this idea in LHNav, an asynchronous system that uses progress and arrival feedback to coordinate navigation goals, VLA motion-token prediction, and whole-body control. To train the Cerebellum for natural, route-faithful motion, we develop a physics-grounded data engine that improves physical tracking of motion references along collision-aware routes and pairs the resulting demonstrations with rendered visual observations. Together, these designs allow task planning to proceed alongside continuous movement and extend single-goal navigation to successive destinations with natural, route-faithful whole-body motion. In simulation, our navigation VLA outperforms OmniNav and DualVLN in single-goal success and motion smoothness, with fewer collisions and falls. Using a VLA trained only on single-goal demonstrations, LHNav achieves a 75.56% success rate on long-horizon navigation tasks. Deployment on the Unitree G1 further demonstrates single-goal navigation and zero-shot long-horizon task execution in real-world environments, including layouts with repositioned objects. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [Compact and Wide-Force-Range Magnetorheological Hand Exoskeleton for Kinesthetic Feedback in Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12130)
*Xianlong Mai,Rui Yu,Rui Chen,Daniele Leonardis,Wenbo Li,Domenico Chiaradia,Antonio Frisoli,Shuaishuai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于磁流变制动器的手部外骨骼系统，在紧凑可穿戴结构下实现宽范围力觉反馈，并验证其在机器人手遥操作中提升刚度辨识、抓取安全性和沉浸感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部力觉反馈对机器人手遥操作中的力与位移感知至关重要；渲染不同刚度物体尤其是高刚度物体需要宽范围力反馈，但现有方法难以兼顾宽范围、紧凑性和可穿戴性。

Method: 设计采用磁流变（MR）制动器的手部外骨骼，制动扭矩范围0.01–0.918 Nm，总质量仅39.8 g；外骨骼可渲染最高21 N指尖力，同时保持1.01 N反向驱动力。通过物体刚度辨识实验和遥操作抓取任务评估性能，并结合问卷分析用户感受。

Result: 用户仅凭力觉反馈即可辨别物体刚度；遥抓取任务中两种条件下成功率均为100%，但力觉反馈相比仅视觉反馈使物体接触力降低28.2%，提升物体安全性和操作者沉浸感；问卷表明力觉反馈提高了可靠性、真实感和用户信心。

Conclusion: 该紧凑可穿戴MR制动器外骨骼能够提供宽范围手部力觉反馈，并在遥操作中改善刚度感知、抓取安全性和用户体验，验证了其在机器人手遥操作中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Hand kinesthetic feedback is crucial for restoring and enhancing human perception of force and displacement in robotic hand teleoperation. Wide perception range of force feedback is vital in rendering objects of different stiffness, especially for high-stiffness rendering. However, existing approaches struggle to achieve wide range of force feedback while maintaining compact and wearable design. This paper presents a hand exoskeleton system employing magnetorheological (MR) brakes to provide a wide range kinesthetic feedback during teleoperation. The MR brake provides a braking torque ranging from 0.01 to 0.918 Nm, while maintaining a compact structure with a total mass of only 39.8 g. The proposed exoskeleton can render fingertip forces up to 21 N while maintaining back-driving force of 1.01 N. Experimental results show that the system allows users to discriminate object stiffness using kinesthetic feedback alone. In a tele-grasping task, when both conditions achieved a 100% success rate, kinesthetic feedback led to a 28.2% reduction in object contact force compared to visual feedback only, enhancing object safety and operator immersion. Questionnaire results further indicate that kinesthetic feedback improves reliability, realism, and user confidence during teleoperation.

</details>


### [58] [Motion Belief Action: From a Visual Scene Map to Physical Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12113)
*Dibyendu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Motion Belief Action（MBA）：在场景地图上融合物体被移动频率、所需力、负载与阻塞状态等隐物理信念，在力限制和任务代价决定的风险下选择重新观察、轻推、打开、支撑或拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人群中工作需判断看不见的物体是否仍在原处，以及拉门/抽屉是否值得冒险；现有物体变化地图不含力信息，物理属性地图在固定风险水平回答问题，视觉-语言-动作模型不显式估计隐藏物理。

Method: MBA 作为场景地图上的决策层，依据人移动物体的频率、所需力、承载负载和是否阻塞，在力上限下选择 look again、nudge、open、brace 或 decline，并由任务成本设定风险。

Result: 在 9 个厨房真实活动中，MBA 预测物体是否在原处优于 FreMEn 和 Perpetua，并以更低成本决定何时重看，优于二者及 persistence filter，原因是约四分之一放置会在 30 秒内被取走；在 17 个位置由人用测力夹爪真实拉门/抽屉时，地图力估计优于部件类型平均值，按成本设风险也优于已发表固定风险规则；仿真中将力限设为训练部件中位力时，MBA 通过拒绝拉动提升 SmolVLA 与 Octo 得分，多数被拒门拉会失败但抽屉更多本可打开，部件类型平均仅获约一半增益；将已发表风险规则设为由成本隐含的风险后，在主要设置中与 MBA 单次拉动规则表现相当。消融显示开合与保持力关联、试探性轻推、相机跟踪动作先验在主要设置无帮助，真实拉动扫描中轻推略有成本。

Conclusion: MBA 将隐藏物理估计、力限制与任务代价风险结合，是可提升物体状态判断和拉门/抽屉决策质量的场景图决策层，但部分复杂先验与轻推策略收益有限。

Abstract: A robot that works among people must judge what it cannot see: whether an object is still where it was last seen, and whether a pull on a door or drawer is worth its risk. Maps that track object change hold nothing about force, maps of physical properties answer a risk question at a fixed risk level, and vision-language-action models keep no explicit estimate of hidden physics. We propose Motion Belief Action (MBA), a decision layer on the scene map. From how often people move an object and how much force it needs, how much load it holds and whether it is blocked, MBA chooses under a force limit to look again, nudge, open, brace or decline, with its risk set by the task's costs. On real activity in 9 kitchens, it predicts whether an object is still in place better than FreMEn and Perpetua, and decides when to look again at lower cost than these and the persistence filter, because about a quarter of placements end in a pick-up within 30 s. On real pulls of doors and drawers in 17 locations, made by a person with a force-sensing gripper, the map's force estimates decide better than a part-type average. Setting the risk from the costs also beats a published risk rule at its fixed level. In simulation, with the force limit at the training parts' median force, MBA raises SmolVLA's and Octo's scores by declining pulls; most declined door pulls would fail, but on drawers more would have opened, and a part-type average gets about half of this gain. In both, a published risk rule set to the risk these costs imply is MBA's rule for a single pull and decides as well in the main settings. A link between opening and holding force, a probing nudge and an action prior for camera tracking do not help in the main settings, and over the real-pull sweep the nudge costs a little.

</details>


### [59] [GAP: Generalized Adaptation Prior for Dexterous Piano Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12111)
*Jinyi Chen,Hongyi Li,Lei Jiang,Xianwei Liu,Yanhong Liang,Yulin Chen,Yucheng Tao,Yongbin Jin,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GAP（通用适应先验），在仿真预训练中学习“如何适应”而非“如何行动”，并将适应先验迁移到真实灵巧手，实现样本高效的钢琴演奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手策略在仿真训练后迁移到硬件常因非理想因素退化，且高维动作空间使真实世界适应成本高；钢琴演奏需要精确协调的多指运动，是代表性测试场景。

Method: GAP在仿真预训练阶段学习适应先验：在重复执行之间利用反馈调整下一次动作；训练梯度引导网络从成对执行数据中学习动作调整对演奏性能的影响，并用其梯度指导动作调整；随后将同一仿真预训练先验迁移到真实灵巧手。

Result: 仿真中，在相同交互预算下，GAP使灵巧钢琴演奏的F1分数提升约为现有快速适应基线的2.4倍，并泛化到预训练未见的曲目；在真实灵巧手上，同一仿真预训练先验在三首难度不同的曲目上经每首约7分钟适应后，F1分数提升3.6%-23%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习并迁移适应先验可减少部署灵巧手所需的真实世界交互，为灵巧手实际部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Dexterous hands enable versatile manipulation, but policies trained in simulation often degrade on hardware owing to non-idealities, and the high-dimensional action space makes real-world adaptation costly. Piano playing, which requires precise and coordinated multifinger motion, offers a representative testbed. We propose GAP (Generalized Adaptation Prior for Dexterous Piano Playing), a sample-efficient adaptation framework that learns how to adapt, rather than how to act, during simulation pretraining and transfers this adaptation prior to hardware. Between repeated executions, GAP uses feedback to adjust the actions of the next one: a gradient guidance network learns from paired executions how adjusting actions affects playing performance, and its gradients guide these adjustments. In simulation, GAP yields approximately 2.4 times the F1 scores improvement of the existing fast-adaptation baseline for dexterous piano playing under the same interaction budget. The learned prior also generalizes to musical pieces held out from pretraining. On a physical dexterous hand, the same simulation-pretrained prior improves F1 scores by 3.6%-23% across three pieces of varying difficulty after approximately 7 min of adaptation each. These results suggest a practical route to reduce the real-world interaction needed to deploy dexterous hands.

</details>


### [60] [SPARK: Simple Post-training for Adapting pRetrained Knowledge to Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12102)
*Taekyung Kim,Jeongeun Park,Byeongho Heo,Sungjoon Choi,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARK 是一种将预训练视觉基础模型适配到机器人控制的简单后训练方法，通过时序抽象与知识保持提升机器人操作的成功率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型的预训练目标并非为机器人表示学习设计，其嵌入更偏向全局语义，而机器人控制需要时序敏感且与动作相关的表示；同时从零训练机器人模型受数据规模、采集成本和计算负担限制。

Method: SPARK 基于两个原则：时序抽象，促使编码器学习紧凑特征并保留理解时序变化和动作相关场景结构所需的信息；知识保持，将 patch 级表示与原始基础模型对齐，以保留有用的预训练语义。二者共同产生语义有意义且保留先验知识的紧凑视觉状态表示。

Result: 在多个机器人基准上，SPARK 相较原始基础模型持续提升成功率和泛化能力，并进一步证明这些增益可迁移到真实机器人操作任务。

Conclusion: SPARK 表明通过简单的后训练即可有效适配基础模型用于机器人控制，在避免从零训练的同时获得更适合控制的紧凑时序敏感表示，并具备实际机器人应用价值。

Abstract: Large-scale representation learning has produced public foundation models that provide strong general-purpose visual features. However, their pretraining objectives are not designed for robot representation learning, so the resulting embeddings tend to emphasize global semantics rather than the temporally sensitive representations required for robot control. Training robot models from scratch is also undesirable due to the limited scale and high collection cost of robotics data, as well as the substantial computational burden of pre-training. In this paper, we propose SPARK (Simple Post-training for Adapting pRetrained Knowledge), a post-training method that adapts foundation models to robot control. SPARK is built on two principles: temporal abstraction, which encourages the encoder to derive compact features that preserve information essential for understanding temporal changes and action-relevant scene structure, and knowledge preservation, which aligns patch-level representations with those of the original foundation model to retain useful pretrained semantics. These objectives yield compact visual state representations that remain semantically meaningful while preserving useful prior knowledge. Experiments across multiple robotics benchmarks show that SPARK consistently improves success rates and generalization over vanilla foundation models, and further demonstrate that these gains transfer to real-world robot manipulation.

</details>


### [61] [SAM-VTP: Video Memory and Measured Geometry for Continuous Traversability Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12099)
*Lucas Hoefs,Hui Xie,Senjian An,Fangyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAM-VTP 将 SAM2 的视频记忆通路微调用于稠密、免提示的可通行性分割，并以深度几何测量和离线连续监督生成[0,1]逐像素目标；在合成数据集和闭环实地试验中提升精度、时序一致性与路径可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的可通行性预测多为逐帧处理，时序不一致；仅用二值标签难以表达条件可行性与连续风险。

Method: 方法包括：1）微调 SAM2 视频记忆通路，使每帧利用历史观测的持久记忆进行稠密免提示分割；2）从深度计算坡度、粗糙度、高度并融合到图像特征，而非仅从外观推断地形；3）构建离线连续监督流程，基于深度几何与语义先验生成[0,1]逐像素可通行性目标，无需机器人部署和逐像素标注。

Result: 在 TartanGround 上，相较逐帧 SAM2 基线 SAM-TP，模糊 Jaccard 从0.908升至0.924，二值 IoU 从0.854升至0.886（种子间差异<0.001），帧间闪烁降低31%。两处实地闭环试验中，在相同规划器/控制器下，基线掉线导致无可行路径的频率在铺装路面高3.3倍、崎岖地面高6.9倍；SAM-VTP 在机器人搭载的 Orin Nano 上达21.4 FPS。

Conclusion: 结果表明，视频记忆、几何测量与连续监督相结合可提升可通行性分割的时序一致性、精度和闭环导航可靠性，并具备机载实时部署能力。

Abstract: Traversability prediction is a core component of autonomous navigation, yet most vision-based methods operate frame-wise and produce temporally inconsistent predictions. We present SAM-VTP (Video Traversability Prediction), which fine-tunes SAM2's video memory pathway for dense, prompt-free traversability segmentation, conditioning each frame on a persistent memory of prior observations. Rather than inferring terrain geometry from appearance, we measure it: slope, roughness and height are computed from depth and fused into the image features. We also introduce an offline continuous supervision pipeline that generates dense per-pixel traversability targets in [0,1] from depth-derived geometry and semantic priors, requiring no robot deployment and no per-pixel annotation, capturing conditional feasibility that binary labels cannot represent. On TartanGround, a large-scale synthetic ground-robot dataset, SAM-VTP improves fuzzy Jaccard from 0.908 to 0.924 and binary IoU from 0.854 to 0.886 over SAM-TP, a frame-wise SAM2 baseline, against a seed-to-seed spread below 0.001, and cuts frame-to-frame flicker by 31%. In closed-loop trials at two field sites, driving the same planner and controller, the baseline's dropouts leave the planner without a feasible path 3.3x more often on a paved course and 6.9x on rough ground, while SAM-VTP runs at 21.4 FPS on the Orin Nano the robot carries.

</details>


### [62] [Learning Contextual Motion Constraints for Object Rearrangement Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12072)
*Lexing Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Gang Zeng,Hangxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MoCo-Net双流神经网络，从部分视角点云联合预测铰接部件的连续可行运动范围与障碍物热力图，并用于机器人高效重排场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要只考虑物体自身铰接属性，忽略外部障碍对铰接部件运动的约束；实际场景中运动范围同时受内在关节限位和外在障碍限制。

Method: MoCo-Net采用双流网络，从部分视角点云中同时预测铰接部件的连续运动范围以及标定潜在碰撞区域的障碍物热力图，从而联合建模内部与外部运动约束。

Result: 实验表明双流设计相互增强，获得更准确的可行运动范围预测和障碍物识别，并提升对新物体和新场景的泛化能力；预测结果可构建紧凑的场景配置空间。

Conclusion: MoCo-Net通过联合预测内在关节约束与外在障碍约束，为机器人构建紧凑配置空间，并支持定制规划器高效完成场景重排。

Abstract: The motion of an articulated part in cluttered surroundings is constrained not only by an object's intrinsic joint limits but also by extrinsic obstacles. However, existing approaches primarily consider object articulation properties alone. Here we present Motion Constraint Prediction Network (MoCo-Net), a two-stream neural network that jointly predicts the continuous motion ranges of articulated parts and obstacle heatmaps highlighting potential collision regions from partial-view point cloud observation. By capturing both internal and external motion constraints, MoCo-Net produces more accurate feasible motion range prediction and obstacles recognition, improves generalization for novel objects in new scenes, and enables robots to construct a scene configuration space for efficient exploration of rearrangement actions. Our experiments show that MoCo-Net's two-stream design enhances each other to deliver more accurate predictions, which subsequently form a compact configuration space for a customized planner to efficiently rearrange the scene.

</details>


### [63] [DPGS-SLAM: Diffusion-Prior and Provenance-Aware Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Dynamic Visual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12038)
*Yifan Luo,Takafumi Matsumaru,Vibekananda Dutta,Xin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DPGS-SLAM：一种面向动态环境的单目RGB稠密SLAM框架，结合扩散先验补全与来源感知的3D高斯泼溅建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体会污染相机跟踪与场景重建，违背现有3DGS-SLAM的静态场景假设；直接剔除动态观测又会导致被遮挡静态背景缺失或约束不足。

Method: 通过光流运动发现与SAM2时序一致掩码传播识别动态区域；对关键帧使用微调扩散修复模型补全被遮挡背景，并用Metric3D v2从补全图像预测度量深度以初始化临时高斯原语；用来源标签区分生成原语与观测支持原语，生成内容不参与相机位姿估计和观测地图优化；当原遮挡背景可见时移除符合条件的生成原语，并基于真实RGB观测与预测深度独立初始化观测支持原语。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D和BONN RGB-D上平均ATE RMSE分别为1.2 cm和2.7 cm；在StaDy4D上仅用单目RGB输入，平均PSNR较WildGS-SLAM提升4.60 dB。

Conclusion: DPGS-SLAM通过扩散背景补全与来源感知高斯建图，在动态环境下实现有竞争力的跟踪精度并提升重建完整性，同时避免生成内容污染观测约束。

Abstract: This paper presents DPGS-SLAM (Diffusion-Prior and Provenance-Aware Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM) for dense mapping in environments containing dynamic objects. Dynamic observations can contaminate camera tracking and scene reconstruction in visual SLAM (simultaneous localization and mapping). Although 3DGS (3D Gaussian splatting) enables efficient dense mapping, existing 3DGS-SLAM systems may exhibit instability when dynamic objects violate their underlying static-scene assumption. Excluding dynamic observations can reduce their influence on tracking and mapping, but may leave occluded static backgrounds missing or weakly constrained. Our proposed DPGS-SLAM is a monocular RGB SLAM framework that supports provisional representations of insufficiently observed static backgrounds through asynchronous diffusion-based background completion and provenance-aware Gaussian mapping. Dynamic regions are identified through optical-flow-based motion discovery and temporally consistent mask propagation by SAM2 (segment anything model 2). For selected keyframes, a fine-tuned diffusion inpainting model generates plausible background appearance in occluded regions, while Metric3D v2 predicts metric depth from the completed images to initialize provisional Gaussian primitives. Provenance labels distinguish generated primitives from observation-supported primitives, excluding generated content from camera-pose estimation and observed-map optimization. When previously occluded backgrounds become visible, eligible generated primitives are removed, and observation-supported primitives are independently initialized from real RGB observations and predicted depth. Experimental results demonstrated competitive camera tracking, with average ATE RMSE values of 1.2 cm on TUM RGB-D and 2.7 cm on BONN RGB-D. On StaDy4D, DPGS-SLAM improved average PSNR by 4.60 dB over WildGS-SLAM using only monocular RGB input.

</details>


### [64] [Grasp-Taxonomy Conditioning as a Mode Switch for Dexterous Grasping of Climbing Holds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12017)
*Rumi Loghmani,Abraham George,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BetaGrasp 在 DP3 风格点云扩散策略上引入四类抓握标签，使机器人能按物体几何选择抓握类型，并在攀岩支点滑移测试中显著提升抓握稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓握需要根据物体几何选择不同手型；仅靠点云输入时，策略难以区分抓握类型，容易退化为单一抓握。

Method: 提出 BetaGrasp：以四类抓握标签（crimp、sloper、pinch、jug）条件化 DP3 风格点云策略，部署于 Franka 机械臂与 16-DoF LEAP 手；并构建带棘轮滑移力读数的攀岩支点弹簧测试基准。

Result: 点云编码器未能区分四类支点，无标签 DP3 基线在仿真中从复位姿态出发均退化为 jug 抓握，抓握类型正确率 25%，BetaGrasp 为 94%；80 组配对硬件试验中，平均滑移力由 9.6 N 提升至 14.3 N，完全滑脱比例由 44% 降至 10%。

Conclusion: 显式抓握标签可作为策略选择抓握类型的有效开关，显著改善抓握选择与抗滑移性能；仅依赖点云扫描不足以可靠区分抓握需求。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping in robotics relies on distinct hand configurations depending on object geometry. We introduce BetaGrasp, a 3D diffusion policy that conditions a DP3-style point-cloud policy on a four-class grasp label (crimp, sloper, pinch, and jug), running on a Franka arm with a 16-DoF LEAP hand. We also introduce a climbing-hold benchmark: a spring testbed whose ratchet gives a slip-force reading in newtons. In our analysis the policy's point-cloud encoder did not separate the four holds, so the scan alone gave it little to choose a grasp from. Without the label (a DP3-style baseline), simulated rollouts from the robot's reset pose all end in a jug grasp, whatever the hold (25% correct grasp type, versus 94% for BetaGrasp). The label works as a switch for which grasp the policy performs. On 80 paired hardware trials, BetaGrasp raises average slip force from 9.6 N to 14.3 N, and grasps slipping fully off the hold drop from 44% of trials to 10%.

</details>


### [65] [OASIS: Optimization-Aware Subgoal Inference with Safety Constraints for Autonomous Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11996)
*Arian Hajizadeha,Javad Pourrafia,Negar Shenavara,Sana Bashiria,Seyed Majid Esmailzadeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OASIS将NMPC的优化结果反馈给基于SAC的高层子目标策略，在动态障碍导航中提升成功率，且无需额外优化调用、平均控制器时间相近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的移动机器人需在运动限制和避障约束下向目标推进；学习型子目标策略可帮助MPC突破局部预测视野，但几何上合适的子目标不一定对应可成功求解的控制问题。

Method: 提出分层导航框架OASIS：高层SAC策略生成局部子目标；底层非线性模型预测控制器在运动限制、几何避碰约束和嵌入式高阶控制障碍函数约束下跟踪子目标；将每个子目标区间内的控制器优化结果汇总，并在下一次决策时输入策略，且不增加额外优化调用。

Result: 在五个含静态与移动障碍、密集交互和传感不确定性的导航场景中评估；与分层及直接目标控制变体相比，OASIS取得最高总体导航成功率；三个训练种子的匹配比较显示，其相对无优化状态输入的相同分层结构具有一致成功率优势；选定策略比较显示主要优化失败更少，平均控制器时间相近。

Conclusion: 将控制器优化状态纳入学习型子目标生成，可提升分层导航的成功率与鲁棒性，并保持可接受的计算开销。

Abstract: Navigation in dynamic environments requires mobile robots to make progress toward their goals while respecting motion limits and avoiding surrounding obstacles. Learned subgoal policies can guide model predictive control beyond its local prediction horizon, but a geometrically suitable subgoal does not necessarily lead to a successfully solved control problem. This paper proposes OASIS, a hierarchical navigation framework that incorporates recent optimization outcomes into reinforcement learning-based subgoal generation. A high-level policy trained using Soft Actor--Critic generates local subgoals, while a nonlinear model predictive controller tracks them subject to motion limits, geometric collision-avoidance constraints, and embedded high-order control barrier function constraints. The outcomes of controller optimizations over each subgoal interval are summarized and provided to the policy at its next decision, without requiring additional optimization calls. The framework is evaluated in five navigation scenarios with static and moving obstacles, including dense interactions and sensing uncertainty. Comparisons with hierarchical and direct-goal control variants show that OASIS achieves the highest aggregate navigation success. Matched comparisons across three training seeds further show a consistent success advantage over the otherwise identical hierarchy without optimization-status input. The selected-policy comparison also shows fewer primary optimization failures with similar mean controller time.

</details>


### [66] [Thermo-VLA: A Thermal-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model with Gated Residual Modulation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11981)
*Jingxuan Chen,Junshi Luo,Chencheng Dong,Xiaojian Nie,Tongge Huang,Qiang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Thermo-VLA与ThermoRes Encoder，将热像分解为冷/热属性表示，并通过语言条件残差调制注入RGB token，以实现温度感知的关系推理与连续操作；在Unitree G1真实机器人任务上取得优于pi_0.5的性能，并验证该机制可迁移至颜色属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽引入热感，但多将热像当作普通视觉输入，难以支持操作中的温度感知关系推理，因此需要显式建模冷热属性并实现温度驱动的目标选择与连续操作。

Method: 设计ThermoRes Encoder，将热图像分解为冷、热属性表示；利用语言条件残差调制将这些表示注入RGB token，从而增强温度感知的目标选择与操作能力。构建双机械臂Unitree G1平台上的六个真实机器人任务，覆盖基础grounding到关系推理；并将同一残差调制机制迁移至颜色属性进行验证。

Result: 在四个依赖热感的任务上平均成功率为0.86，较直接融合RGB-热观测的pi_0.5基线高18个百分点；在未见物体与场景变化下平均成功率为0.78，为对比方法中最佳泛化表现；将残差调制迁移至颜色属性后，在仿真和真实机器人实验中均获得一致提升。

Conclusion: Thermo-VLA通过语言条件残差调制显式注入冷热属性表示，有效提升温度感知关系推理与操作性能及泛化能力；该机制具有超越热属性的可迁移潜力，可扩展至颜色等其他属性。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have begun incorporating thermal sensing, but existing approaches typically treat thermal images as ordinary visual inputs, providing limited support for temperature-aware relational reasoning in manipulation. To address this limitation, we propose Thermo-VLA with a ThermoRes Encoder, which decomposes thermal images into cold and hot attribute representations and injects them into the RGB token through language-conditioned residual modulation, enabling temperature-driven target selection and continuous manipulation. We further construct six real-world robotic tasks on a dual-arm Unitree G1 platform, spanning basic grounding to relational reasoning. Experiments show that Thermo-VLA achieves an average success rate of 0.86 across four thermal-dependent tasks, outperforming a pi_0.5 baseline that directly incorporates RGB-thermal observations by 18 percentage points. Under unseen-object and scene variations, Thermo-VLA maintains an average success rate of 0.78, achieving the best generalisation performance among the compared methods. Beyond thermal sensing, we transfer the same residual modulation mechanism to colour attributes and observe consistent improvements in both simulation and real-robot experiments, indicating its potential to generalise beyond thermal attributes. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [67] [CSG-Nav: Confidence-Aware Semantic-Geometric Fusion for Robust Exploration and Accurate Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11978)
*Shoubin Chen,Rui Xu,Zehao Wu,Kai Zhang,Tao Huang,Zhou Liu,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CSG-Nav 是一个面向零样本目标物体导航的置信度感知多模态融合框架，通过语义源融合、空间置信度融合和语义-几何决策融合，提升不确定感知下的探索与目标终止可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本目标物体导航要求智能体在未见环境中探索并定位目标，现有基础模型方法虽具泛化能力，但仍易受远距离感知不可靠、幻觉导致的方向偏置以及语义合理但几何错误的停止决策影响。

Method: 提出 CSG-Nav，包含三种互补融合机制：1）语义源融合，结合开放词汇检测结果、检测置信度、场景观测与关系推理，生成更可靠的方向级语义分数并抑制幻觉方向偏置；2）空间置信度融合，利用高斯衰减函数对多视角语义证据加权，抑制不可靠远距离观测并累积空间一致的探索偏好；3）语义-几何决策融合，将语义目标检测与基于深度的三维定位结合，支持物理落地的停止决策。

Result: 在 HM3Dv1、HM3Dv2 和 MP3D 上取得具有竞争力的零样本性能，成功率分别为 58.9%、71.6% 和 44.3%；融合级消融实验及室内外真实环境部署进一步验证了有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: CSG-Nav 通过置信度感知的多模态融合有效缓解不可靠感知与错误终止问题，在不确定感知条件下实现稳健的零样本目标物体导航，并具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation requires autonomous agents to explore unseen environments and locate target objects without task-specific training, posing challenges in perceptual reliability under observation uncertainty and physically grounded goal termination. Although foundation-model-based approaches improve generalization, they remain vulnerable to unreliable long-range perception, hallucination-induced directional bias, and semantically plausible but geometrically incorrect stopping decisions. To address these limitations, we propose CSG-Nav, a zero-shot navigation framework centered on confidence-aware multimodal information fusion. CSG-Nav integrates three complementary fusion mechanisms. First, semantic-source fusion integrates open-vocabulary detection results, detection confidence, scene observations, and relational reasoning to produce more reliable direction-level semantic scores and suppress hallucination-induced directional bias. Second, spatial-confidence fusion weights multi-view semantic evidence using a Gaussian decay function, suppressing unreliable distant observations while accumulating spatially consistent exploration preferences. Third, semantic-geometric decision fusion combines semantic target detection with depth-based 3D localization to support physically grounded stopping decisions. Experiments on HM3Dv1, HM3Dv2, and MP3D demonstrate competitive zero-shot performance, achieving success rates of 58.9%, 71.6%, and 44.3%. Fusion-level ablations and real-world deployments in indoor and outdoor environments further verify the effectiveness and practicality of CSG-Nav for robust navigation under uncertain perception. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [68] [The Relative Value of Demonstration Data: Execution Time Limits and Operator Mixtures in Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11947)
*Jiaxuan Guo,Xingyu Ren,Youran Sun,Jingxin Yang,Kejia Zhang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出用“数据倍数”衡量不同经验水平遥操作者演示数据的等价性，并在 robomimic 的 Square 和 Can 任务上训练行为克隆策略，发现 Worse 演示并非按固定比例等价于 Better 演示，混合数据还存在额外效率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据集常混合不同经验水平遥操作者的数据，数据采集团队需要知道一组演示需要多少条才能匹配另一组演示的效果。作者用数据倍数定义：两组演示使策略达到相同成功率时所需演示数量的比值。

Method: 在 robomimic 的仿真 Square 和 Can 任务上，使用被标注为 Better 和 Worse 的两类遥操作者的演示训练行为克隆策略，改变数据集大小和 Worse 演示占比；比较不同时间限制下的成功率与所需数据量，并分析混合数据、按长度比例采样与均匀采样，以及遥操作者时薪对成本的影响。

Result: 在 Square 的 25 秒限制下，全 Worse 训练集需要全 Better 训练集 1.55 倍的演示；差异主要来自完成较晚，时间限制放宽到 100 秒时倍数降至 1.11。Can 上大部分差异在 100 秒时仍存在。若按全 Worse 集的 1/1.55 等价比例推算，Square 上一半 Better 一半 Worse 的数据集应需全 Better 的 1.22 倍演示，但实际还需再多 13%，且该额外损失在 20 至 200 秒各时间限制下持续存在，并在均匀采样时仍存在。Square 的 25 秒限制下，只有在 Worse 遥操作者时薪低于 Better 的三分之一时，改用 Worse 遥操作者才在同等成功率下更便宜，因为 Worse 演示录制时间也更长。

Conclusion: 数据倍数可量化不同经验水平演示数据的等价性；Worse 演示不能简单视为按固定比例折价的 Better 演示，混合数据集会产生超出线性加权的额外数据需求。实际采集中若考虑录制时间成本，使用 Worse 遥操作者只有在时薪显著更低时才具成本优势。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets combine demonstrations from teleoperators of uneven experience, so collection teams need to know how many demonstrations from one group match those from another. We measure this with a data multiple: the ratio of the numbers of demonstrations two groups need for their policies to reach the same success rate. On robomimic's simulated Square and Can tasks, we train behavior-cloning policies on demonstrations from the two teleoperators robomimic labels Better and the two it labels Worse, varying dataset size and the share of Worse demonstrations. On Square, within robomimic's 25-second time limit, an all-Worse training set needs 1.55 times as many demonstrations as an all-Better set. Most of this difference is late completion: policies trained on Worse demonstrations finish later, as the Worse teleoperators do, and with a 100-second limit the multiple falls to 1.11. On Can, most of the difference remains at 100 seconds. If each Worse demonstration were worth 1/1.55 of a Better one, as in an all-Worse set, a half-Better, half-Worse set on Square would need 1.22 times as many demonstrations as an all-Better set. In fact, it needs 13% more than that, and this excess persists at every time limit from 20 to 200 seconds. The excess remains when training draws demonstrations uniformly instead of in proportion to their length. On Square, within the 25-second limit, switching to these Worse teleoperators is cheaper at equal success only if they earn less than a third of the Better teleoperators' hourly pay, because Worse demonstrations also take longer to record.

</details>


### [69] [Flip Anything with Agentic Robotics Self-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11941)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示编码智能体可通过物理仿真中的迭代学习，自主开发并精炼双臂夹爪机器人翻转多样物体的策略；任务成功率由24.7%提升至95.0%，并迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 翻转是工业检测、装配和工具使用中的常见操作，但让带夹爪的双臂机器人对多样物体稳定完成180°翻转的代码化策略极具挑战。

Method: 提出让编码智能体在物理仿真中针对不同物体几何进行迭代学习，逐步获得从基础交接、倾倒、推动，到鲁棒抓取与感知，以及薄物体操作与恢复等技能，并生成融合高层策略选择与反应式控制的图结构代码。

Result: 渐进式技能获取将任务成功率从24.7%提升到95.0%；生成的图结构代码支持多阶段翻转，并在真实机器人上执行。

Conclusion: 编码智能体结合仿真迭代学习与图结构控制代码，可自主形成可迁移的物体翻转策略，为双臂机器人复杂操作编程提供有效路径。

Abstract: Flipping is a fundamental robotic manipulation skill that requires reorienting a target object in a stable pose on a planar support surface by rotating it 180$^\circ$ about an axis parallel to the support surface -- think of flipping a coin from heads to tails. Although flipping is a common objective in industrial tasks such as parts inspection, mechanical assembly, and tool use, coding a bimanual robot with grippers to flip diverse objects is a significant challenge. In this paper, we show that a coding agent can autonomously develop and refine object-flipping strategies through iterative learning in physics simulations across diverse object geometries. Progressive skill acquisition, from basic handover to toppling and pushing, robust grasping and sensing, and thin-object manipulation with recovery, improves task success from 24.7% to 95.0%. The resulting graph-based code integrates high-level strategy selection with reactive control, enabling multi-stage flipping on physical robots.

</details>


### [70] [Reliability-Adaptive Multimodal Relative Pose Tracking for Underwater Leader-Follower Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11940)
*Mingyang Yang,Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Jun Wang,David Chen Wang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可靠性自适应的多模态感知框架，用于两艘移动水下机器人之间的六自由度相对位姿跟踪，并在自建AUV平台上通过648次水池试验和闭环实验验证了精度、实时性与编队稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下领航-跟随编队控制需要在感知退化、通信/测量受限和机载计算资源有限的条件下持续估计相对位姿，单一模态或高复杂度融合方法难以兼顾鲁棒性与实时性。

Method: 框架融合视觉、声学测距和水动力测量，考虑各模态物理特性与时变可靠性，自适应加权多模态贡献，并以线性计算复杂度建模时序依赖，从而支持嵌入式硬件上的高效位姿估计。

Result: 在自建AUV平台上完成648次水池试验，覆盖两类领航器、三种光照条件和九种轨迹模式，平均平移误差3.8 cm、平均姿态误差2.4°；在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上推理延迟17.62 ms，相比Transformer融合基线加速2.69倍；闭环实验验证了移动领航下的稳定编队保持。

Conclusion: 该可靠性自适应多模态融合方法能够在机载计算约束下实现高精度、低延迟的六自由度相对位姿跟踪，并支持水下编队闭环控制；相关648次试验的多模态数据集将公开。

Abstract: Underwater leader-follower formation control requires continuous relative-pose estimation despite degraded sensing conditions and limited onboard computation. This paper presents a reliability-adaptive multimodal perception framework for six-degree-of-freedom relative-pose tracking between two moving underwater robots. The framework integrates complementary visual, acoustic-ranging, and hydrodynamic measurements while accounting for their distinct physical characteristics and time-varying reliability. It adaptively weights modality contributions and models temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity, enabling efficient pose estimation on embedded hardware. Extensive experiments were conducted using a custom-built AUV platform, with 648 pool trials spanning two leader vehicle types, three illumination conditions, and nine trajectory patterns. Evaluation yields an average translational error of 3.8 cm and an average orientation error of 2.4°. On an onboard NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, the fusion network achieves an inference latency of 17.62 ms, providing a 2.69-fold speedup over the evaluated Transformer-based fusion baseline.
