Closed-loop experiments further demonstrate stable formation maintenance with a moving leader under onboard computational constraints. A multimodal dataset containing measurements from all 648 trials will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [71] [Distilling Instructions into Weights: Closed-Form Edits Fix a Robot Policy's Misreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11894)
*Ali-Adeeb Abbas,Esmaeil Seraj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究无需梯度、训练数据或修改指令，仅通过编辑策略权重来修复通用机器人策略的局部失败，并将显式改写的逐层效应蒸馏进语言模型MLP权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略常在本来能完成的任务的某一步失败；作者追问此类失败能否直接通过权重编辑修复，而不是重新训练或改指令。

Method: 以RoboLab中的零样本pi0.5视觉-语言-动作策略为例，发现其将“in the bowl”错误绑定到物体；提出闭式指令蒸馏，把显式改写的逐层效应复制到语言模型MLP权重中，并与随机同规模更新、不同模块放置及缩小参数存储变体比较。

Result: 在128个留出种子上，成功率从27升至106（81个修复、2个破坏，p=7×10^-22）；同规模随机方向更新无效果。该编辑还提升另外两个任务且对其余13个无可检测损害；仅存储0.16%参数的变体保留大部分效果。放在动作专家或动作头帮助较小或无帮助。新留出种子上端到端复现从8/64到36/64。针对感知、缺失抓取、接触物理或夹爪阈值的编辑无效；7个盲测Claude Opus编辑器中，仅获得误读指令信息者通过留出评分。

Conclusion: 权重编辑最适合在已有已知良好输入可供蒸馏时使用；由于权重不变的重跑也会翻转约三分之一配对结果，评估应配对种子、重跑未编辑策略，并在留出种子上确认增益。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies often fail at one step of a task they can otherwise perform. We ask whether such a failure can be repaired by editing the policy's weights directly, without gradients, training data, or a change to the instruction. Told to put the cube then the banana in the bowl, a zero-shot $\pi_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy in RoboLab releases the cube before it reaches the bowl. Replaying 21 of these decisions under rewordings shows that it binds "in the bowl" to the wrong object. Closed-form instruction distillation copies the per-layer effect of an explicit rewording into the language model's MLP weights. On 128 held-out seeds, success rises from 27 to 106 episodes, with 81 fixed and 2 broken ($p=7\times10^{-22}$), while an update of the same size in random directions leaves success unchanged. The edit also lifts two other tasks without detectable harm on the remaining 13, and a variant that stores 0.16% of the parameter count keeps most of its effect. Placed in the action expert or action head instead, the same distillation helps less or not at all. Our released tool reproduced the fix end to end on new held-out seeds, from 8/64 to 36/64. Edits aimed at perception, a missing grasp, contact physics, or the gripper threshold did not help, and of seven blind Claude Opus editors, only the one given the misread instruction produced an edit that passed held-out scoring. These results suggest that weight edits work best when a known-good input already exists to distil. Because reruns with unchanged weights flip about a third of paired outcomes, evaluations should pair seeds, rerun the unedited policy, and confirm gains on held-out seeds.

</details>


### [72] [Design of a hybrid tendon-linkage finger with coupled and adaptive motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11885)
*Hyojae Kang,Giup Seo,Jae-Young Lee,Yeong Jae Park,Jongwoo Park,Hyunmin Do,So-Hee Park,Dongil Park,Jeongdo Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合腱-连杆机器人手指：4个机械自由度仅由3个驱动器驱动，实现MCP/PIP耦合运动、DIP被动耦合及接触自适应，并验证其运动与抓握性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧机器人手指需要协调的关节运动和对物体接触的适应性，但为多个关节分别配置驱动器会增加传动机构复杂度，因此需在减少驱动器数量的同时保持多自由度运动与自适应能力。

Method: 设计混合腱-连杆手指：MCP外展/内收由锥齿轮驱动，MCP与PIP屈伸由独立双向腱传动驱动；DIP不独立驱动，而通过带扭转弹簧和机械限位的四杆机构与PIP耦合；腱绳路径还引入MCP-PIP机械耦合。开展运动学分析描述耦合与自适应运动，并用基于ArUco的实验评估关节运动特性。

Result: ArUco测量表明耦合运动与理论预测高度一致，但自适应运动受连杆部件干涉部分限制；样机对圆柱、长方体及不规则形状物体表现出不同接触姿态，平均峰值指尖力为4.56 N。

Conclusion: 该机构无需为DIP关节增加额外驱动器，即可同时提供耦合运动和依赖接触的自适应能力，为减少驱动器数量的灵巧手指设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic fingers require coordinated joint motion and adaptability to object contact, while independently actuating multiple joints increases the complexity of the transmission mechanism. This paper presents a hybrid tendon--linkage robotic finger with four mechanical degrees of freedom driven by three actuators. The MCP abduction/adduction joint is driven through a bevel gear, while MCP and PIP flexion/extension are driven by separate bidirectional tendon transmissions. The DIP joint is not independently actuated but is coupled to the PIP joint through a four-bar linkage with a torsional spring and mechanical stopper. The tendon routing also introduces mechanical coupling between the MCP and PIP joints. Kinematic analysis was conducted to describe the coupled and adaptive motions, and experiments were performed to evaluate the joint motion characteristics. ArUco-based measurements showed that the coupled motion closely matched the theoretical prediction, whereas the adaptive motion was partially limited by interference between linkage components. The fabricated finger demonstrated different contact postures with cylindrical, cuboid, and irregularly shaped objects, and the mean peak fingertip force was 4.56 N. The proposed mechanism provides both coupled motion and contact-dependent adaptation without requiring an additional actuator for the DIP joint.

</details>


### [73] [Action Representation for Spatial Generalization in Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11869)
*Yuhang Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究用受控机器人剥离基准，比较世界、物体和表面坐标框中的动作表征，探讨机器人世界模型的空间泛化取决于动作表征能否保留跨空间构型仍有效的物理关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同坐标可描述同一交互，而相似运动在局部物理条件变化时可能产生不同结果；现有学习预测对这类差异如何影响空间泛化认识不足。

Method: 构建匹配的世界坐标、物体坐标和表面坐标动作表征，在机器人剥离任务中开展全局位姿与接触重定位实验，并考察去除剥离厚度信息、表面相对坐标、坐标框不确定性、表面与工具联合居中、接触几何错误和执行偏差等条件。

Result: 有限训练数据下，同一交互若获得不同空间描述会产生代价；去除厚度信息导致不可约歧义，使相同预测输入对应不同材料去除结果。准确局部观测下，表面相对坐标提升后果预测和反事实动作选择，但预测器仍易受坐标框不确定性影响；联合居中可消除共同原点平移敏感性，而错误接触几何和实际执行偏差引入不同失效机制。

Conclusion: 有效的空间泛化不在于最小化坐标变化，而在于保留决定物理后果的动作—状态关系；这为设计可跨空间构型复用物理知识、同时保留预测与规划所需区分性的机器人世界模型提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Spatial generalization in robot world models depends on whether action representations capture the physical relationships that remain valid across changing spatial configurations. Yet different coordinates can describe the same interaction, whereas similar motions can produce different outcomes when local physical conditions change. How these distinctions affect learned prediction remains insufficiently understood. This study evaluates matched action representations defined in world, object, and surface coordinate frames using a controlled robotic peeling benchmark. Global pose and contact relocation experiments reveal a cost associated with limited training data when equivalent interactions receive different spatial descriptions. Removing information about peel thickness creates an irreducible ambiguity because identical predictive inputs can correspond to different material removal outcomes. Coordinates defined relative to the surface improve consequence prediction and counterfactual action selection under accurate local observations, but learned predictors remain vulnerable to uncertainty in the coordinate frame. Joint centering of the surface and tool removes sensitivity to translation of their common origin, whereas incorrect contact geometry and actual execution deviations introduce different failure mechanisms. These results show that effective spatial generalization depends on preserving the relationships between actions and states that determine physical consequences, rather than simply minimizing coordinate variation. This perspective provides a principled basis for designing robot world models that reuse physical knowledge across spatial configurations while retaining the distinctions essential to prediction and planning.

</details>


### [74] [Fade to... Red? Scheduling Coloured Noise Exploration Supports Efficient Robot Learning with Reduced Movement Jerk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11868)
*Francisco M. López,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文系统研究连续控制强化学习中动作噪声颜色随训练调度的效果，发现将噪声逐步褪向红噪声可在不损失学习效率与最终性能的情况下显著降低探索抖动，推荐作为离线策略机器人学习的默认探索调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续控制深度强化学习常用时间不相关的高斯白噪声进行探索，但会导致动作抖动且探索受局部限制；已有研究表明时间相关的有色噪声（尤其粉噪声）可提升探索效率，因此作者追问训练过程中动态改变噪声颜色是否能优于白噪声。

Method: 在Fetch机械臂和Shadow灵巧手的四个稀疏奖励操作任务上，系统比较白噪声与红噪声之间的49种线性调度，并与广义状态相关探索（gSDE）进行对比。

Result: 训练末端的噪声颜色不影响学习效率或最终性能；但向红噪声褪变可使每个任务中的探索抖动减半。红噪声在综合学习效率、最终性能和平滑性时表现最佳：其学习效率与粉噪声相当，而运动抖动约少2倍。

Conclusion: 推荐将向红噪声褪变的探索调度器作为离线策略机器人学习的默认策略。

Abstract: Exploration in deep reinforcement learning (RL) for continuous control is commonly driven by temporally uncorrelated Gaussian (white) action noise, which leads to jerky motions with locally constrained exploration. In contrast, temporally correlated coloured noise, particularly pink noise, has been shown to support more efficient exploration in RL. Motivated by these findings, we inquire whether scheduling changes to the noise colour over the course of training can outperform white noise for continuous control robotics. We perform a systematic study of 49 linear schedules between white and red noise, together with generalised state-dependent exploration (gSDE), on four sparse-reward manipulation tasks with a Fetch arm and a Shadow Dexterous Hand. We find that the noise colour at the end of training does not affect learning efficiency or end performance, whereas fading towards red noise halves the jerk of exploration in every task. Balancing learning efficiency, end performance and smoothness, red noise emerges as the preferred strategy: it learns as efficiently as pink noise with almost 2-fold less movement jerk. We therefore recommend exploration schedulers that fade towards red noise as the default for off-policy robot learning.

</details>


### [75] [Decoupled Asynchronous System for Humanoids (DASH): Latency-Agnostic Scaling of Vision-Language-Action Models for Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11854)
*Bingzhang Wang,Nathan Zhao,Jialeng Ni,Jiajin Zheng,Yin Zhang,Kehua Chen,Jian Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DASH 是一种面向人形机器人灵巧操作的解耦异步 VLA 系统，将慢速视觉语言模型与快速流匹配动作头分离，使控制频率不再受骨干网络规模限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环控制中，VLA 的视觉语言骨干位于控制关键路径上；骨干推理期间机器人持续运动，导致执行动作基于越来越 stale 的观测。骨干越大，反应性越差，尤其影响人形机器人灵巧操作。

Method: DASH 将策略拆为慢速视觉语言模型（System 2）和快速流匹配动作头（System 1），通过 LCM 协议并发运行，使控制频率仅由动作头决定。动作头融合冻结骨干的 stale 语义与自身 ResNet 编码器的新鲜视觉；采用去同步训练暴露部署时的 stale 状态，并用自适应时序聚合器在可变推理延迟下对齐重叠动作块。

Result: 在按模拟时间计入推理延迟的异步 RoboTwin 基准上，DASH 在 0.8B 到 8B 骨干下保持 20 Hz 控制频率，而同步方法从 9.7 Hz 降至 4.1 Hz，且各规模下成功率更高。真实人形灵巧手平台上，DASH 的 pick-and-place 成功率明显高于同步流水线。

Conclusion: 将视觉语言骨干与动作头解耦并异步执行，可把骨干移出控制关键路径，在保持大模型语义能力的同时显著提升闭环反应性和操作成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models pair a vision-language backbone with an action head, but in closed-loop control the backbone sits on the control-critical path: while it computes, the robot keeps moving, so each executed action is conditioned on an increasingly stale observation. A larger backbone therefore directly blocks reactivity, especially in humanoid dexterous manipulation. We present DASH, a Decoupled Asynchronous System for Humanoids that removes this coupling. DASH splits the policy into a slow vision-language model (System 2) and a fast flow-matching action head (System 1) that run as concurrent processes over the LCM communication protocol, so the control rate is set by the action head alone, independent of backbone size. The action head fuses stale semantics from a frozen backbone with fresh vision from its own ResNet encoder, desynchronized training exposes it to the staleness it meets at deployment, and an adaptive temporal aggregator aligns overlapping action chunks under variable inference latency. On an asynchronous RoboTwin benchmark that charges inference latency in simulated time, DASH holds 20 Hz control rate from 0.8B to 8B backbones while the synchronous rate falls from 9.7 to 4.1 Hz, with higher success at every size. On a real humanoid with dexterous hands, DASH achieves clearly higher pick-and-place success than the synchronous pipeline.

</details>


### [76] [A Benchmark and Layered Evaluation Protocol for Evidence-Grounded Shared Belief Updating in Connected Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11842)
*Ranulfo Bezerra,Kimitoshi Yamazaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出将连通机器人中的共享推理评估为“证据到信念”的轨迹，并发布CREG基准，用350个确定性场景在每条证据前缀上检验信念更新、矛盾处理与决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多只打分最终答案或动作，无法判断方法是否正确归因证据、更新信念、处理矛盾并证明决策，因此需要可度量中间推理轨迹的基准。

Method: 构建Connected Robotics Evidence Graph（CREG）基准：350个确定性生成场景，覆盖七个机制（合理修正、信念保持、关系关联、矛盾出现与解决、陈旧/延迟证据、来源可靠性），每个证据前缀均给出规范化后验参考，并经独立对数几率检查器和盲审专家审核；设置九种表示条件以分离关系内容与序列化、顺序和元数据；评估四个开放权重指令模型和六个透明融合基线。

Result: 发现三点：实体 grounding 解释弱模型缺陷的很大一部分；在关系依赖场景中删除语义关系使严格信念状态准确率下降15.3分（Holm校正p<0.001），但信息匹配的结构化列表优于完整图，打乱图也不损害性能，说明关系内容在需要时重要而图序列化无一致优势；信念准确率沿证据序列强烈非单调，存在仅评估端点无法检测的中途失败。

Conclusion: CREG将共享推理从端点评分转为证据到信念轨迹的可测评估，表明需要关注实体 grounding、关系内容和中间信念更新；所有场景、参考、提示、指标与分析代码均开放以支持离线可复现研究。

Abstract: Connected physical systems fuse observations from robots, IoT sensors, and edge-AI modules into a shared account of the world, yet most evaluations score only the final answer or action and cannot tell whether a method attributed the evidence, updated its belief, handled contradictions, and justified its decision. We argue that shared reasoning in Connected Robotics should be evaluated as an evidence-to-belief trajectory, and introduce the Connected Robotics Evidence Graph (CREG) Benchmark to make that trajectory measurable. CREG comprises 350 deterministically generated scenarios across seven regimes (justified revision, belief preservation, relation association, contradiction onset and resolution, stale/delayed evidence, and source reliability), each exposing a normative posterior reference at every evidence prefix, validated by an independent log-odds checker and screened by a blinded expert audit. Nine representation conditions separate relation content from serialization, ordering, and metadata. Evaluating four open-weight instruction models and six transparent fusion baselines yields three findings. First, entity grounding accounts for a substantial part of the weak-model deficit. Second, deleting semantic relations costs 15.3 points of strict belief-state accuracy on relation-dependent scenarios (Holm-corrected p<0.001), but an information-matched structured list outperforms the full graph and shuffling the graph does not hurt: relation content matters where required, while graph serialization confers no consistent advantage. Third, belief accuracy is strongly non-monotonic along the evidence sequence, with mid-trajectory failures that endpoint-only evaluation cannot detect. All scenarios, references, prompts, metrics, and analysis code are released for offline, hardware-free reproduction.

</details>


### [77] [Budget-aware Correspondence-driven View Selection for Real-time 3D Perception of Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11809)
*Vaishnav Ramesh,Jacob Carter,Shivali J. Patel,Sanjeev J. Koppal,Md Jahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出预算感知、对应关系驱动的无位姿视图采样算法BCVS，可在CPU上以22+ FPS在线过滤冗余图像，服务于移动机器人高效3D感知与场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署常产生长且冗余的图像序列，拖累下游处理；现有方法多依赖完整SfM流程或相机位姿估计，计算开销大，难以满足移动机器人在线过滤需求。

Method: BCVS采用纯对应关系评分策略，优先选择视图新颖性，同时保持足够重叠以维持几何稳定性；在用户给定预算下迭代选取最独特帧并抑制近重复帧，生成保持连通性且最小化重建损失的紧凑子集。

Result: 基准实验表明，BCVS在紧预算下优于CPU友好基线，预算增大时仍具竞争力；航空与海洋机器人现场试验中，冗余最多降低16%，同时保持稳健的SfM注册和高质量3D重建。

Conclusion: BCVS无需位姿估计、CPU友好且可扩展，适合移动机器人在线视图过滤与高效3D感知，并已在大规模真实机器人数据中验证实用性。

Abstract: 3D visual perception and scene reconstruction are critical for autonomous robots and intelligent transportation, yet real-world deployments often generate long, redundant image sequences that hinder downstream processing. This report investigates BCVS (budget-aware correspondence-driven view sampling), a pose-free view selection algorithm that offers 22+ FPS on a CPU, enabling online filtering for efficient 3D perception by mobile robots. Unlike existing approaches that rely on full structure-from-motion (SfM) pipelines or camera pose estimation, BCVS employs a purely correspondence-based scoring strategy that prioritizes view novelty while maintaining sufficient overlap for geometric stability. Given a user-specified budget, BCVS iteratively selects the most distinct frames and suppresses near-duplicates, producing compact subsets that preserve connectivity and minimize reconstruction loss. Extensive experiments on benchmark show that BCVS outperforms CPU-friendly baselines at tight budgets and remains competitive as budgets scale. Field trials with large aerial and marine robotics data further validate its practicality and scalability: BCVS reduces redundancy up to 16% while sustaining robust SfM registration and high-quality 3D reconstructions.

</details>


### [78] [PAIR: Proprioception-Aligned Image-Ray Representations for Viewpoint-Robust Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11798)
*Sung-Wook Lee,Xuhui Kang,Dylan Losey,Yen-Ling Kuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAIR 是一种利用机器人本体感知对齐图像-射线联合表示的对比学习框架，用于提升视觉运动策略在视角变化下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习在机器人操作中进展显著，但视觉运动策略对视角变化仍很脆弱：图像外观受相机投影影响，任务相关状态与视角相关外观相互纠缠，导致学习低效且难以泛化到未见视角。现有相机射线条件或几何表征方法分别存在未充分 grounding 视觉表示或忽略视角投影的问题。

Method: 提出 PAIR 对比学习框架，利用机器人本体感知对齐联合图像-射线表示，使相似机器人状态下的观测在不同视角下在表示空间中保持接近，从而为学习视角鲁棒特征提供结构化 grounding 信号。PAIR 可适配 Diffusion Policy 训练，也可用于 VLA 模型后训练。

Result: 在存在相机变化的仿真和真实机器人操作基准上，PAIR 分别比未使用射线条件的行为克隆基线提升 12.7 和 26.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: PAIR 通过本体感知监督的联合图像-射线对比对齐，有效缓解视角相关外观与任务状态的纠缠，并显著增强策略对未见视角的泛化能力。

Abstract: While imitation learning has made substantial progress toward generalizable robot manipulation through scaling, viewpoint robustness remains a major bottleneck. Visuomotor policies rely heavily on visual inputs, yet images provide an inherently ambiguous signal under viewpoint change: similar scenes may appear markedly different across viewpoints, while distinct states may share certain visual overlaps. A policy must therefore infer not only the task-relevant state, but also how it is visually projected under a particular camera configuration. This entanglement of viewpoint-dependent appearance and task-relevant state can make learning inefficient and limit generalization to unseen viewpoints. Recent work has begun to address this challenge through camera-ray conditioning and representation learning using geometric cues to induce invariance. However, providing camera information as additional context does not by itself ground visual representations in how the task scene is visually projected, while geometric alignment without camera context can introduce spurious correspondences by ignoring viewpoint-dependent projection. We propose PAIR, a contrastive learning framework that aligns joint image-ray representations using robot proprioception. PAIR encourages observations with similar robot states to remain close in representation space across viewpoints, providing a structured grounding signal for learning viewpoint-robust features. PAIR is a highly adaptable framework: it can be used to train Diffusion Policy (DP) or post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models. In simulated and real-world manipulation benchmarks with camera variation, PAIR consistently outperforms the behavior cloning baseline without ray conditioning by 12.7% and 26.7% points, respectively, demonstrating stronger generalization to unseen viewpoints. Project website: this http URL .

</details>


### [79] [HolidayFactory 1.0: Simulating, Generating, and Evaluating Industrial Robot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11789)
*Holiday Robotics,Jeongmin Lee,Taehyeon Kim,Dohyun Jang,Minji Lee,Seonghun Oh,Dongwoo Park,Geonhyup Lee,Jaehyung Kim,Jaemin Yoon,Jinseop Bok,Jungwoo Kim,Minseok Yang,Philsik Chang,Soomin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0，用于在持续场景状态下模拟、生成并评估工业机器人重复作业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人评估多从重置后的回合开始，而工业工作由连续重复作业构成，前序结果会影响后续操作；大规模评估需要任务关键物理交互仿真、工业环境构建以及随场景状态持续更新的性能度量。

Method: 框架根据人工指定的工作场所与任务定义世界，将场景变体与流程逻辑组成场景，并配套定义作业、指标和评分标准的评估。其包含三部分：HOLIDAY-SIM 在 Newton 基于 SDF 的液压弹性接触上增加本构律与半隐式 primal-dual 求解器，将带状态执行器动力学耦合到关节运动，并从仿真状态导出触觉与关节观测；Agentic world construction 通过几何与交互检查生成可仿真资产和场景变体，并提出任务条件评估供人工确认与可执行检查；24/7-BENCH 提供七类工业任务，在不重置场景的情况下评估作业，测量完成、失败、未完成作业数、吞吐量、延迟和电机能耗。

Result: 受控研究刻画了所提出的接触、执行器和触觉模型，并检验验证引导世界构建的有效性；场景与评估生成实验揭示了模型评审器的局限，以及确保生成评估忠实反映任务要求的困难。作者用脚本轨迹（如 code-as-policy）考察了作业级评估，但尚未在持续运行中比较不同策略。

Conclusion: HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0 支持面向持续工业作业的仿真与作业级评估，但生成评估的任务保真性和持久运行下的策略比较仍是待解决问题。

Abstract: Robots are typically evaluated in episodes that begin from a reset, while industrial work consists of repeated jobs whose outcomes affect subsequent operations. Evaluating such work at scale requires simulation of task-critical physical interactions, construction of industrial environments, and measurements of performance as scene state persists. We present HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0, a framework for simulating, generating, and evaluating industrial robot work. Given a human-specified workplace and task, the framework defines a world pairing a scenario of scene variants and process logic with an evaluation defining a job, metrics, and a rubric. HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0 comprises three components. HOLIDAY-SIM extends Newton's SDF-based hydroelastic contact with additional constitutive laws and a semi-implicit primal-dual solver, couples stateful actuator dynamics to articulated motion, and derives tactile and joint observations from simulated states. Agentic world construction generates simulation-ready assets and scene variants using geometric and interaction checks, and proposes task-conditioned evaluations for human confirmation and executable checks. 24/7-BENCH introduces seven industrial tasks in which jobs are evaluated without resetting the scene, measuring completed, failed, and unfinished jobs, throughput, latency, and motor energy. Controlled studies characterize the proposed contact, actuator, and tactile models and examine the effectiveness of verification-guided world construction. Experiments on scene and evaluation generation further reveal the limitations of model critics and the difficulty of ensuring that generated evaluations faithfully capture task requirements. We examine job-level evaluations with scripted trajectories (e.g., code-as-policy) but have not yet compared policies over persistent runs.

</details>


### [80] [Planners Exploit Deterministic Error: Dithered Low-Bit World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11780)
*Yong Hwi Song,Keon Myung Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出低比特世界模型在采样规划器中失败的关键不是单步预测误差大小，而是量化误差是否跨规划迭代持续并跨候选共享；用每次迭代重采样的共享抖动可显著缓解规划崩溃，并实现W8A4加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI/CEM等采样规划器每控制步要查询世界模型数千次，低比特量化是降低模型式控制成本的直接手段；但作者发现无校准的round-to-nearest激活量化会让规划成功率最多下降100个点，而单步latent预测误差却未必更大，因此需要解释并修复这种规划层面的失效。

Method: 分析TD-MPC2、DINO-WM和多任务TD-MPC2中的低比特激活量化；比较round-to-nearest与stochastic rounding，并检验误差的持续性、候选间共享性、固定dither与每次迭代重采样dither的影响；同时在Ampere GPU上用CUDA graphs评测W8A4整数世界模型的规划延迟，并测试block-scaled NVFP4/MXFP4。

Result: RNE误差会跨规划器迭代持续并被所有候选共享，导致迭代规划器漂移到该误差偏好的候选；将dither在同一步或同一episode内固定会重现漂移。所有候选共享且每次迭代重绘的dither在相同比特宽度下无需训练即可消除大部分损害：TD-MPC2的19个受影响Meta-World单元中至少减半、中位数95%，DINO-WM 5个单元中4个，317M多任务TD-MPC2全部8个受影响任务。W8A4整数TD-MPC2的规划rollout比FP32快1.9-3.3倍，完整规划步快1.3-1.5倍，但不快于预转换权重的BF16；共享dither最多增加2.3%延迟。NVFP4消除大部分损害，MXFP4仅部分，失败与共享尺度的粗网格相关。

Conclusion: 低比特世界模型用于采样规划时，应优先控制量化误差的时间持续性和候选间共享性，而非只看单步预测误差；共享重采样dither是一种无需训练的有效修复，并能在W8A4下带来实际加速，但相对BF16的收益有限。作者在测试数据前固定了假设、阈值和分析代码，并报告了失败假设。

Abstract: Sampling-based planners such as MPPI and CEM query a learned world model thousands of times per control step, so low-bit world models are an obvious way to make model-based control cheaper on a robot. With round-to-nearest activation quantization and no calibration, we find that planning can collapse, by up to 100 points of success, while the world model's latent one-step prediction error stays no larger than that of a stochastic rounding scheme that plans well. At the same bit width, what decides the outcome is not the size of the rounding error but whether it persists across planner iterations and is shared across candidates. Round-to-nearest leaves an error that persists, and an iterating planner drifts toward the candidates that this error favors; in TD-MPC2, holding the same dither fixed within a step or an episode brings the drift back. Dither shared by all candidates and redrawn at every iteration removes most of the damage at the same bit width and without training: at least half of it in all 19 affected Meta-World cells (task $\times$ bit width) of TD-MPC2 (median 95%), in 4 of 5 DINO-WM cells, and in all 8 affected tasks of a 317M-parameter multi-task TD-MPC2. Captured as CUDA graphs on an Ampere GPU, a W8A4 integer TD-MPC2 world model runs its planning rollouts 1.9-3.3$\times$ and the whole planning step 1.3-1.5$\times$ faster than FP32, though not faster than BF16 with pre-converted weights; shared dither adds at most 2.3% latency. In TD-MPC2, block-scaled NVFP4 removes most of the damage and MXFP4 part of it, which ties the failure to coarse grids with a shared scale. Hypotheses, thresholds and analysis code were fixed before the data that test them, and the hypotheses that failed are reported.

</details>


### [81] [Graph-Sparse Distributed Predictive Control for Multi-Robot Motion Planning vianEdge-Consensus ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11755)
*Josep Borras-Borras,Pablo Segovia,Carlos Ocampo-Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向双积分动力学平面机器人避碰运动规划的分布式MPC架构，通过时变预测冲突图与边共识ADMM降低协调需求，并在必要时由集中式HOCBF-QP监督执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式完整图MPC协调需求高、计算扩展性差；目标是减少机器人间不必要的成对交互，在保持任务性能的同时提升大规模集群避碰规划的实时性与可扩展性。

Method: 采用时变预测冲突图选择相关成对交互；每次MPC更新时利用热启动轨迹将避碰约束凸化一次，形成固定凸QP；再用边共识形式的ADMM分解该QP，实现机器人本地并行优化及到分离半空间的独立闭式投影；当分布式候选解不满足直接执行条件时，由集中式高阶控制屏障函数QP选择性监督。

Result: 在8、16和32个机器人的C++仿真中实现无碰撞执行，显著提升计算可扩展性，并保持与集中式完整图基线相当的任务级性能。

Conclusion: 该分布式MPC架构能有效降低协调负担并提升大规模机器人避碰规划的计算扩展性，同时通过选择性集中监督保障执行安全性。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed model predictive control (MPC) architecture for collision-avoiding motion planning of planar robots with double-integrator dynamics. To reduce coordination demands, a time-varying predicted conflict graph selects relevant pairwise interactions. The associated collision-avoidance constraints are convexified once per MPC update using warm-start trajectories, yielding a fixed convex quadratic program (QP). The resulting QP is then decomposed using an edge-consensus formulation of the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), enabling parallel robot-local optimization and independent closed-form projections onto separating half-spaces. Execution is selectively supervised by a centralized high-order control barrier function (HOCBF) QP when the distributed candidate does not satisfy the direct-execution conditions. Evaluated in C++ simulations involving 8, 16, and 32 robots, the proposed architecture achieves collision-free execution and substantially improves computational scalability while maintaining task-level performance comparable to a centralized complete-graph baseline.

</details>


### [82] [Particle Filtering with Generative Trajectory Models for State Estimation and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11732)
*Lukas Lao Beyer,Samvit Das,Sertac Karaman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在不修改预训练扩散模型的前提下，将粒子滤波引入推理过程，利用模型生成的联合状态、动作和观测假设及其预测观测，同时改进机器人状态估计与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型常直接作为策略，根据观测历史采样动作；但联合建模状态、动作和观测的模型还能预测机器人状态与环境演化。作者探究能否通过改变推理流程而非重新训练模型，利用这些预测提升状态估计和控制。

Method: 维护多个状态历史假设的粒子；由单个预训练扩散模型为粒子生成状态历史、预测观测和动作建议；新测量到来时，根据预测观测与真实测量的匹配程度对粒子重新加权；除用于状态估计外，还将权重用于动作选择，偏好能更好解释观测的假设所对应的动作建议。

Result: 在部分可观测导航和基于视觉的机械臂操作仿真实验中，与使用同一预训练模型但不进行滤波的动作生成相比，状态估计和任务性能均有提升，且未使用额外测试时信息。

Conclusion: 联合生成模型的预测可通过粒子滤波式推理得到有效利用，无需修改预训练模型即可同时改善状态估计与机器人控制。

Abstract: Generative models can support both state estimation and control in robotics. For control, a common approach is to use the model as a policy that directly samples actions conditioned on observation histories. However, models that jointly represent states, actions, and observations can also predict how the robot state and its environment evolve. We investigate whether these predictions can improve state estimation and control through a change in inference procedure, without modifying the pretrained model. To this end, we use particle filtering to maintain competing hypotheses about state histories. A single pretrained diffusion model generates these hypotheses along with their predicted observations and associated action proposals. As new measurements arrive, particles are reweighted according to how well their predicted observations match the measurements. While particle filtering conventionally uses these weights only for state estimation, we also use them to guide action selection, favoring proposals associated with hypotheses that better explain the observations. Simulation experiments in partially observed navigation and vision-based manipulation demonstrate improvements in both state estimation and task performance compared to action generation without filtering, using the same pretrained model and no additional test-time information.

</details>


### [83] [Accelerated Reinforcement Learning for Soft Robotics: Hours Instead of Years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11718)
*Roshan S. Kaundinya,Hehui Zheng,Robert K. Katzschmann,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出用基于数据学习得到的低维aSSM约化多项式ODE作为强化学习训练环境，在气动软体机械臂上实现高效轨迹跟踪策略学习与零微调部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人非线性动力学使强化学习面临交互数据量大或可靠仿真器难以获得的问题；作者利用软体动力学中的时间尺度分离，将系统约化到低维不变流形（aSSM）以降低建模与训练成本。

Method: 从预采集数据中学习一个仅含6,924个参数的多项式常微分方程作为aSSM约化模型，并将其作为RL训练环境；在六维aSSM约化模型上训练轨迹跟踪策略，随后直接部署到气动软体机械臂，无需微调。

Result: 策略训练仅需3小时，等效于约3年硬件交互；部署后对动态目标的跟踪误差比在4.8M参数RNN或1.7M参数Transformer上训练的相同策略低40%，而这两者识别分别需13小时和100小时，本文模型仅需5分钟。

Conclusion: 数学上可解释的低维aSSM约化模型可作为高效、可靠的RL训练替代环境，显著降低软体机器人策略学习的数据、时间与模型复杂度成本，并支持零微调实机部署。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) requires large amounts of interaction data or a reliable simulator, both hard to obtain for nonlinear soft robotic hardware. Recent work shows that a time-scale separation inherent to soft robot dynamics justifies their reduction to low-dimensional invariant manifolds, known as adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs). We demonstrate that an aSSM-reduced model, a simple polynomial ordinary differential equation learned purely from pre-collected data, can serve as an RL training environment. On a pneumatically actuated soft arm, we train a trajectory-tracking policy in 3 hours on a six-dimensional aSSM-reduced model and deploy it without fine-tuning, whereas reproducing this policy training extrapolates to 3 years of hardware interaction. Our policy tracks dynamic targets with 40% lower error than otherwise identical policies trained on a 4.8-million-parameter recurrent neural network (RNN) or a 1.7-million-parameter transformer, which take 13 and 100 hours to identify, against 5 minutes for our mathematically justified 6,924-parameter model.

</details>


### [84] [Data-Driven Precision Control of Soft Helicoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11709)
*Roshan S. Kaundinya,Mark Leone,Roberto Sabella,Hugo Buurmeijer,Luis Pabon,John Irvin Alora,Marco Pavone,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将基于绝热谱子流形（aSSM）的纯数据驱动建模方法从仿真扩展到硬件软体机器人，学习软螺旋躯干机器人的5维aSSM约化非线性ODE，并结合模型预测控制实现高精度实时轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前aSSM方法仅在非线性软体机器人仿真器中验证，需证明其可直接利用硬件数据建模并控制真实软体机器人，同时克服现有数据驱动线性方法和无模型强化学习在精度与约束任务上的不足。

Method: 从软螺旋躯干机器人硬件平台采集数据，学习一个5维aSSM约化的非线性常微分方程（ODE）来表征其动力学，并将该ODE用于模型预测控制，完成从2D到3D、含静态与动态约束的闭环轨迹跟踪任务。

Result: 学习到的5维aSSM约化ODE能准确建模软螺旋躯干机器人动力学；在所有闭环轨迹跟踪任务中，该方法相比可用的数据驱动线性方法和无模型强化学习均提升了准确性与精度。

Conclusion: 该方法仅依赖硬件数据，不局限于特定软体机器人，因此可推广到更广泛的软体机器人系统的建模与实时精密控制。

Abstract: A purely data-driven method grounded in the theory of adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs) was recently used to model and control nonlinear soft robotic simulators. Here, we extend the methodology to learn aSSM-reduced models from data collected from hardware soft robotic platforms. Specifically, we learn a five dimensional (5D) aSSM-reduced nonlinear ordinary differential equation (ODE) that accurately models the dynamics of a soft-helicoid trunk robot. We then use this ODE in a model predictive control scheme to demonstrate real-time precision control across various challenging closed-loop trajectory-tracking tasks, ranging from 2D to 3D trajectories with static and dynamic constraints. We find that our methodology enhances accuracy and precision across all control tasks compared to available data-driven linear methods and model-free reinforcement learning. While we focus here on a soft-helicoid trunk robot, our methodology relies solely on hardware data and is therefore applicable to a broader class of soft robotic systems.

</details>


### [85] [Compute Regeneration through Re-Productization: Reorganizing Retired Edge AI Silicon into a Delivered Multilingual Service Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11698)
*Hongyu Luo,Sun Tie,John Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文将退役边缘硬件重新产品化视为系统设计问题，以基于退役 32 GB Jetson AGX Xavier 的多语言服务机器人 GEdgeBot 为例，验证保留计算模块并重构周边产品组织可恢复多类端侧 AI 与控制服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有价值的边缘硬件常在仍具计算能力时失去产品支持；作者主张通过“计算再生/再产品化”保留计算模块并重新设计产品组织，而非简单报废。

Method: 构建 GEdgeBot 案例：AI 与编排运行于 Xavier，有限电机脉冲生成下沉至 STM32F407；开展源依据清单、六模式控制状态机/就绪屏障/声源定向审计，结合历史单原型测量、11 月隔离推理栈复测与同一 6,000 个 50 ms 控制帧的双执行组织对比；并进行六项静态审计与 14 场景回放执行 main() 调度器。

Result: 系统暴露 5 项端侧 AI 服务、7 语言语音链、3 类感知域及 1 个确定性 7 字节控制边界；历史测量显示 YOLO、Whisper-small、NLLB-200 常驻时 GPU 内存 1,782 MB，并发 AI 负载下直接控制 p99 偏差由 0.24 ms 升至 49.93 ms，6/6 次触发跟随演示平均延迟 4.35 s 但会话语音不可用；有限脉冲边界消除脉冲串中的主机抖动但不消除命令起始相位，停止边界由主机强制执行，MCU 16 位脉冲字段未限幅；静态审计发现主机—MCU 队列不匹配，14 场景回放覆盖六模式、零物理设备打开、54 个契约检查帧。

Conclusion: 结果支持存在性主张和可复现的架构假设，而非车队级可靠性；208 的直接控制比值尚未复现，MCU 波形捕获与现场故障注入仍待开展。

Abstract: Useful edge hardware can lose product support while retaining substantial computation. We treat this as systems design: compute regeneration here means re-productization, retaining the compute module while redesigning the surrounding product organization. Our case is GEdgeBot, a multilingual service robot built around a retired 32 GB Jetson AGX Xavier. A source-grounded inventory shows the box alone supplies inference, while the combined system exposes five on-device AI services, a seven-language speech chain, three sensing domains, and one deterministic seven-byte control boundary. AI and orchestration stay on the Xavier; finite motor-pulse generation on an STM32F407. Audits cover the six-mode control state machine, readiness barriers and sound orientation. Historical single-prototype measurements report 1,782 MB GPU memory with YOLO, Whisper-small, and NLLB-200 resident, a direct-control p99 deviation rising from 0.24 to 49.93 ms under concurrent AI load, and six of six trigger-to-follow demonstrations at 4.35 s mean latency, with conversational voice unavailable. A November campaign restores an isolated inference stack, re-measures AI workloads, and repeats control-frame runs. Driving two execution organisations with the same 6,000 measured 50 ms frame intervals shows the finite-pulse boundary removes host jitter from the pulse train but not the command start phase; its stopping bound is host-enforced, while the MCU 16-bit pulse field is unclamped. A six-check static audit exposes a host--MCU queue mismatch, and a 14-scenario replay executes the audited main() dispatcher across six modes with zero physical device opens and 54 contract-checked frames. Results support an existence claim and a reproducible architectural hypothesis, not fleet-level reliability. The 208 direct-control ratio remains unreplicated; MCU waveform capture and on-site fault injection remain open.

</details>


### [86] [A Physical Testbed for Self-Driving Laboratories: Comparing Bayesian and Agentic Optimization in a Minimal Closed-Loop Color Formulation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11690)
*Tony Qin,Ziheng Wu,Maximilian Schommer,Roya Amini-Naieni,Aldair Gongora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作搭建了一个集成商用实验室自动化仪器与AI决策的物理测试平台，实现累计24小时无人值守的闭环自主实验，并在染料混合物的光谱匹配任务中比较了贝叶斯优化与智能体优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室（SDL）旨在通过机器人自动化与AI决策闭环实现设计-构建-测试-学习（DBTL），但现有物理测试平台多处于早期阶段，且与商用实验室自动化的集成不足。

Method: 作者组装并集成商用实验室自动化仪器，构建闭环SDL物理测试平台；在最小化闭环发现任务中，系统自主配制水溶液染料混合物以匹配目标吸收光谱。平台完成6个独立实验活动，每个活动约4小时、包含48次闭环迭代；由AI算法选择后续配方，无需人工干预。研究在固定总体积约束下比较传统贝叶斯优化（BO）与智能体式优化方法。

Result: 系统在累计24小时内实现连续、无人值守的闭环自主运行。智能体优化器匹配目标吸收光谱所需迭代次数少于BO，最多减少52%，并在一个BO在48次迭代内未能匹配的目标上收敛到解。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可评估既有与新兴AI策略（包括LLM引导优化）的SDL参考物理平台，有助于在真实高成本、耗时的实验部署前验证优化算法。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) integrate laboratory robotics and automation with AI-driven decision-making to close the design-build-test-learn (DBTL) cycle. However, physical testbeds that combine off-the-shelf hardware and AI into a functioning closed-loop workflow remain in early stages of development, and their integration with commercial laboratory automation is comparatively less developed. In this work, we develop a physical testbed that assembles and integrates commercially available laboratory automation instruments and demonstrate continuous, closed-loop, autonomous operation over 24 cumulative hours of unattended experimentation. We validate this platform on a minimal closed-loop discovery task in which the system autonomously formulates aqueous dye mixtures to match a target absorbance spectrum. The system completed six independent campaigns, each consisting of 48 closed-loop iterations over approximately four hours. In every campaign, the system autonomously selected, dispensed, and measured each mixture, with an AI-driven decision-making algorithm determining subsequent formulations without human intervention. Using this testbed, we compared the performance of two AI-driven decision-making algorithms, traditional Bayesian optimization (BO) and an agentic approach, in selecting mixtures under a fixed total-volume constraint. The agentic optimizer matched target absorbance spectra using fewer iterations than BO, with reductions up to 52%, and converged on a solution for one target that BO failed to match within 48 iterations. These results demonstrate a reference physical platform for SDLs that can evaluate both established and emerging AI strategies, including large language model (LLM)-directed optimization, before their deployment in real-world applications where experiments may be costly and time-consuming to prepare.

</details>


### [87] [Streaming Failure Forecasting: VLA Failure Monitors That Keep Learning After Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11688)
*Amit Jadhav,Shaurya Beriwala,David Q. Liu,Yanfei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出流式失败预测器SFF：部署后不冻结失败监控器，而是利用每回合仅有的成功/失败标签持续重拟合监控器最后一层线性读出，从而在任务或策略版本切换等漂移下保持失败排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的失败监控器通常离线训练后冻结；部署后光照、物体、相机漂移以及策略更新或换任务，会使旧任务上训练的冻结监控器在切换后对失败的排序接近随机。

Method: SFF保持离线监控器网络固定，仅在每个回合后重拟合最后的线性层；在Jetson CPU单核上每次策略调用耗时2.18 ms。其报警阈值只在成功后移动。所有设置先在测试前锁定，并在仿真未声明漂移和真实SO-101机械臂记录回放中评估。

Result: 仿真中，在未声明漂移下，SFF在5个测试套件中的4个优于冻结监控器，并在所有套件上优于检测到漂移后重置；仅当同时出现两种漂移时，误报接近目标阈值的效果例外。真实数据含4种VLA、434条记录运行，跨未声明任务或策略版本切换回放时，SFF对每个VLA的失败排序均优于冻结监控器，并在探索性比较中优于真实切换时的完全重置。

Conclusion: 部署后的失败监控器不必保持冻结；仅用回合结果廉价地重拟合其读出层，在所测试的任务或策略变化后即可提升失败监控性能。

Abstract: Failure monitors for vision-language-action (VLA) policies are trained offline and then frozen. After deployment, lighting, objects and cameras drift, and the policy itself is updated or moved to new tasks. On recorded real-robot runs, after a switch of task or policy version, a frozen monitor fit on the old task ranks failures little better than chance. We present Streaming Failure Forecasting (SFF), a monitor that keeps learning from the one label every episode provides: whether it succeeded. SFF keeps the offline monitor's network fixed and refits only its last linear layer after each episode, at 2.18 ms per policy call on a Jetson CPU core. Its alarm threshold moves only after successes, which in simulation kept false alarms near target except when two drifts came at once. With every setting locked before testing, SFF ranks failures better than the same monitor kept frozen on 4 of 5 simulated test suites under unannounced drift, and above resetting on detected drift on every suite. Carried with its locked learner settings to 434 recorded runs of four VLAs on real SO-101 arms, replayed across unannounced switches of task or policy version, it ranks failures better than the frozen monitor for every VLA and, in an exploratory comparison, above a full reset at the true switch. A deployed failure monitor need not stay frozen: refitting its readout from outcomes is cheap and helps after the changes we tested.

</details>


### [88] [Brain-Aligned Latent Actions: Imposing Premotor Cortex Geometry on a Robot's Action Vocabulary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11674)
*Amit Jadhav*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人离散潜在动作 tokenizer 的码本分配高度不稳定，作者利用这一自由度，将猕猴腹侧前运动皮层（PMv）画形状时的表征几何通过直通相似性损失注入机器人视频训练的 tokenizer，并在未见形状类别上获得与 PMv 较高的表征相似性，但存在运动学基线更强和标签打乱对照偏高的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略常通过无标签视频学习少量离散潜在动作，但训练过程未规定哪种运动应分配哪个码；作者发现该码分配极不稳定，因此尝试利用这种自由度把生物神经表征结构施加到 tokenizer 的码分配上。

Method: 先量化 Moto 潜在运动 tokenizer 的不稳定性：独立训练的两次运行对同一机器人片段分配几乎无关的码（调整兰德指数 0.001），bf16 与 fp32 推理间 74% token 被重新分配。随后在机器人视频上，将 tokenizer 的重建目标与一个直通相似性损失联合训练，以匹配猕猴 PMv 在画形状任务中的表征几何；每个目标仅一次训练，并在五个损失未见的形状类别上评估。

Result: PMv 训练的 tokenizer 与 PMv 的表征相似性达 0.52；已发布和无先验 tokenizer 为 0.20–0.26，随机运动学编码器 95 分位为 0.42。运动学、语言和随机目标不迁移（-0.17 至 -0.03）。限制包括：手工选择的运动学描述符“笔画初始方向”与 PMv 更匹配（0.65），标签打乱的 PMv 对照得分为 0.41。

Conclusion: 结果表明可利用 tokenizer 码分配的不稳定性注入神经表征先验，并在未见形状类别上获得一定 PMv 相似性；但强手工运动学基线和标签打乱对照削弱了特异性主张。作者在运行前预先指定了在可复现 Moto 已发表结果的 CALVIN 流程上的下游操作测试。

Abstract: Robot policies increasingly act through a small vocabulary of discrete latent actions learned from unlabeled video, yet training leaves open which motion receives which code. We find this choice highly unstable: two independently trained runs of Moto's latent motion tokenizer assign the same robot clips to nearly unrelated codes (adjusted Rand index 0.001), and evaluating one tokenizer in bf16 instead of fp32 reassigns 74% of its tokens. We use this freedom to impose the representational geometry of macaque ventral premotor cortex (PMv), recorded while a monkey drew shapes, on the tokenizer's code assignment, with a straight-through similarity loss trained alongside its reconstruction objective on robot video. In one training run per target, on five shape categories the loss never saw, the PMv-trained tokenizer reaches a representational similarity of 0.52 with PMv, against 0.20-0.26 for released and no-prior tokenizers and 0.42 for the 95th percentile of random kinematic encoders. Kinematic, language, and random targets do not transfer (-0.17 to -0.03). Two results limit the claim: a hand-picked kinematic descriptor, the stroke's initial direction, matches PMv better (0.65), and a label-shuffled PMv control scores 0.41. We specify the downstream manipulation test, on a CALVIN pipeline that reproduces Moto's published results, before running it.

</details>


### [89] [HINT-VLM: Humanoid Interactive Navigation over Rough Terrain with Embodiment-Grounded Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11665)
*Ziwon Yoon,Seok Hwan Kim,Yiyu Chen,Quan Nguyen,Lu Gan,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种面向户外崎岖杂乱地形人形机器人NAMO的混合规划框架：用VLM提供物体可移动性语义先验，用高斯过程残差模型融合机器人 embodied 交互数据校正先验，并将概率化可移动性估计纳入分层不确定性感知规划，从而联合利用行走与操作实现高效安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航多假设环境不可交互且只关注通行；现有NAMO研究多局限于平坦环境和反应式交互，缺乏对物体可移动性的预测与前瞻规划。在户外崎岖地形中，机器人必须同时推理地形可通行性与物体可移动性，因此需要新的NAMO方法。

Method: 提出混合规划框架：以Vision-Language Model提供语义层面的物体可移动性先验；通过Gaussian process residual model利用机器人 embodied interaction data对VLM先验进行残差校正，得到embodiment-grounded probabilistic movability estimates；再将这些概率估计纳入hierarchical uncertainty-aware planning，使humanoid能够联合利用locomotion与manipulation进行导航。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果；作者声称该方法使humanoid能够在杂乱户外崎岖地形中实现高效且安全的导航，并称其为首个通过predictive movability reasoning与probabilistic VLM integration解决rough terrain NAMO的方案。

Conclusion: 该工作将VLM语义先验、GP残差校正与不确定性感知分层规划结合，为人形机器人在户外崎岖杂乱环境中的NAMO提供了预测式可移动性推理框架，强调地形可通行性与物体可移动性的联合建模。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have achieved significant progress in locomotion and manipulation, enabling traversal over rough terrain and richer interaction with the environment, such as moving obstacles. However, most traditional navigation methods assume non-interactive environments and focus only on traversal. While Navigation Among Movable Obstacles (NAMO) approaches relax this assumption, prior efforts have largely focused on flat environments and reactive interaction, rather than predicting object movability and planning ahead. These limitations become critical in outdoor rough-terrain scenarios, where robots must jointly reason about terrain traversability and object movability. In this work, we address NAMO for humanoid robots in cluttered outdoor rough terrain by proposing a hybrid planning framework that combines semantic priors from a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with embodied robot interaction data through a Gaussian process (GP) residual model. The GP corrects the VLM-derived movability prior to produce embodiment-grounded probabilistic movability estimates, which are then incorporated into hierarchical uncertainty-aware planning. The proposed approach allows humanoids to jointly leverage locomotion and manipulation for efficient and safe navigation, representing the first solution to NAMO in rough terrain through predictive movability reasoning and probabilistic VLM integration.

</details>


### [90] [Enhancing Balance and Posture in Material Handling Tasks with a Wearable Robotic Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11662)
*Eisa Anwar,Sajeeva Abeywardena,Stuart C. Miller,Ildar Farkhatdinov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究开发了一种可穿戴机器人尾作为动态配重，在模拟货架装卸任务中，主动尾可改善平衡并降低躯干运动及竖脊肌、腓肠肌活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频繁负重搬运与肌肉骨骼劳损相关，下背痛影响高达40%的人工搬运者；受动物用尾巴保持平衡启发，需探索可穿戴机器人尾作为动态平衡配重。

Method: 10名参与者完成模拟货架装载/卸载任务，比较主动尾、被动尾和无尾三种条件；测量运动学、压力中心及腓肠肌、竖脊肌、胫骨前肌、肱二头肌肌电活动。

Result: 与无尾相比，主动尾使压力中心峰值位移降低>20 mm、躯干运动减少4.3度、竖脊肌和腓肠肌活动分别降低>9%和>20%；被动尾改善较小；主动尾较被动尾增加肱二头肌活动；踝关节运动学和胫骨前肌活动无变化。

Conclusion: 结果支持可穿戴机器人尾可用于改善物料搬运过程中的平衡控制。

Abstract: Frequent heavy lifting is linked to musculoskeletal strain, with lower back pain affecting up to 40% of manual labourers. Inspired by animals using tails for balance, we developed a wearable robotic tail as a dynamic counterbalance. Ten participants performed an imitated shelf loading/unloading task across three conditions: active tail, passive tail, and no tail. Kinematics, centre of pressure, and muscle activation (gastrocnemius, erector spinae, tibialis anterior, biceps brachii) were measured. Compared with no tail, the active tail achieved >20 mm reduction in centre of pressure peak displacement, 4.3 degree reduction in trunk motion, and >9% and >20% reductions in erector spinae and gastrocnemius activity, respectively. The passive tail produced smaller reductions, suggesting passive counterbalancing helps while active movement augments it further. Biceps brachii activation increased with the active versus passive tail. The ankle kinematics and tibialis anterior activation were unchanged. These findings support wearable robotic tails for improving balance during material handling.

</details>


### [91] [FlowEval: How Much Adaptation Does an Embodied Foundation Model Need at Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11650)
*Zhang Xi-Jia,Roman Yakunin,Shivang Chopra,Chengyue Huang,Che Chen,Siddharth Karamcheti,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowEval 是一组无需训练的诊断指标，利用 flow-matching 目标在目标域演示上探测具身基础模型，以预测模型与部署目标的匹配程度及所需适配数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练减少了具身基础模型部署适配成本，但预训练模型未必满足每个目标部署的性能要求；实践者需要在适配前判断模型与目标部署的匹配度，以及还需收集多少目标域数据。

Method: 提出 FlowEval：使用一组成功的目标域演示，通过 flow-matching 目标计算模型在成功演示上的预测不一致性，作为无需训练的指标来评估预训练模型对目标部署的拟合程度；在 Pi-0.5、MolmoAct2、GR00T、DreamZero 四类具身基础模型上验证，并微调 Pi-0.5 与 GR00T 于 17 个 RoboCasa365 操作任务。

Result: FlowEval 能在真实与仿真操作任务中区分已见与未见部署；微调实验表明 FlowEval 与增加适配数据后成功率提升程度相关。

Conclusion: FlowEval 可作为轻量、无需训练的诊断工具，预估模型到部署所需适配程度及目标域数据需求。

Abstract: Large-scale pretraining is reducing the amount of adaptation required to deploy embodied foundation models, but it is not guaranteed that a pretrained model already provide the level of performance required in every deployment. Practitioners therefore need to understand how well a pretrained model matches a target deployment and how much adaptation data is left to be collected. We study whether a small set of successful target-domain demonstrations can reveal whether and how much target-domain data will be required before any adaptation. We introduce FlowEval, a set of training-free metrics that use the flow-matching objective to probe an embodied foundation model with a set of demonstrations. FlowEval quantifies predictive disagreement on successful target demonstrations to assess how well a pretrained model fits a target deployment. Across four embodied foundation model families: Pi-0.5, MolmoAct2, GR00T, and DreamZero, FlowEval distinguishes seen from unseen deployments across real-world and simulated manipulation. Further, by finetuning Pi-0.5 and GR00T on 17 RoboCasa365 manipulation tasks, we showed that FlowEval correlates with how success rate improves as additional adaptation data is provided. Together, these results suggest that FlowEval can serve as a lightweight, training-free diagnostic of how much model--deployment adaptation may be required.

</details>


### [92] [PA-SaR: Perception-Aware Learning, Control, and Coordination for Multi-Robot Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11648)
*Jocubas Dimsa,Maria Grouev,Filip Keerberg,Nicolas Laplagne,Tigo Schutgens,Sven Schoonebeek,Anahita Jamshidnejad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出PA-SaR框架，用于在自主搜索与救援中围绕任务级目标协调异构局部控制、学习型探索、监督式任务分配和自适应受害者引导，并通过单/多机器人仿真分析各机制间的权衡与交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主SaR需要机器人探索未知或部分已知环境、规避危险、协调团队并引导受害者撤离；仅提升单个机器人能力未必改善任务结果，因为更安全的运动可能限制探索，更快发现可能增加救援需求，而有效引导依赖受害者响应。

Method: PA-SaR是一个数学与架构框架，区分环境真值、机器人特定感知和模拟受害者行为，并集成模型预测控制与学习型探索、基于感知的安全屏蔽、事件驱动任务分配及自适应接近—引导受害者策略；通过单机器人和多机器人仿真研究评估这些互补机制在任务各阶段的交互。

Result: 结果表明碰撞 avoidance 与搜索完成之间存在权衡；监督式任务分配在信息共享之外还能提升搜索完成率；增加探索并不必然带来更多救援；自适应引导相较持续引领提高了救援数量，但未检测到受害者平均/最终信任提升或死亡率降低。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持将局部控制、团队协调和 evacuation 视为自主SaR中相关但不同的评估维度，并表明需在任务级目标下综合分析与权衡各机制，而非仅优化个体机器人能力。

Abstract: Autonomous SaR requires robots to explore unknown or partially known environments, avoid hazards, coordinate team activities, and guide discovered victims to safety. Improving individual capabilities of robots does not necessarily improve mission outcomes: safer motion can restrict the exploration behavior, faster discovery can increase the rescue demand, and effective guidance depends on victim responses. We introduce PA-SaR, a mathematical and architectural framework for coordinating heterogeneous local control, learning-based exploration, supervisory task allocation, and adaptive approach-lead victim guidance around mission-level objectives. PA- SaR distinguishes environmental ground-truth, robot-specific perceptions, and simulated victim behavior. This provides a common basis for integrating complementary mechanisms and analyzing their interactions across mission stages. We demonstrate the framework through single- and multi-robot simulation studies incorporating model-predictive and learning-based exploration, perception-based safety shielding, event-driven task allocation, and adaptive victim guidance. The results show trade-offs between collision avoidance and search completion, indicate that supervisory allocation improves search completion beyond information sharing alone, and demonstrate that increased exploration does not necessarily yield more rescues. Adaptive guidance increases rescues relative to continuous leading, without a detected improvement in mean or final victim trust or a reduction in mortality. These findings support evaluating local control, team coordination, and evacuation as related but distinct aspects of autonomous SaR within the considered simulation environments.

</details>


### [93] [ColNav: Uncertainty-Aware Spacetime Collision Checking with Bounding Volume Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11646)
*Sanjeev Ramkumar Sudha,Lars Ivar Hatledal,Erlend M. Coates*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ColNav：一种基于几何体积的鲁棒碰撞检测公式，将目标与自车位置/速度不确定性编码为时空管，并用包围体层次结构实现整段时域的高效鲁棒碰撞检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态不确定环境中，移动机器人需规划避碰路径；传统前向传播并在离散时空采样点检测碰撞的方法可能漏检采样点之间的碰撞，且对状态估计不确定性不鲁棒。

Method: ColNav 将目标与自车的位置和速度不确定性分别用有界集和高斯描述编码为两类时空管；将时空管组织为包围体层次结构，通过一次树遍历覆盖整个预测时域而非逐采样时刻检测，返回鲁棒几何证书及碰撞时间估计；静态与动态障碍共享同一表示。

Result: 在预测模型失配下达到100%成功率，最佳基线为65%；密集交通中保持最高三倍于基线的安全间隙；并在实体机器人上验证了实时运行。

Conclusion: ColNav 通过时空管与包围体层次结构实现连续、鲁棒且高效的碰撞检测，可提升动态不确定环境下移动机器人导航的安全性和实时性。

Abstract: Safe navigation for mobile robots in dynamic and uncertain environments requires planning paths that avoid collisions with other moving targets. Typically this involves forward propagating the robot motion and checking for collisions at sampled points in space and time. Such methods can miss collisions between sampled points and are not robust to uncertainty in the state estimates. We propose ColNav, a robust collision check formulation with geometric volumes. The key aspect of our approach is encoding the uncertainty in the targets' and the ego robot's positions and velocities into spacetime tubes, in two variants that consume bounded-set and Gaussian uncertainty descriptions, respectively. The tubes are organised in bounding volume hierarchies, so that a single tree traversal covers the entire horizon, rather than testing sampled time instants, and returns a robust geometric certificate together with an estimate of the time to collision. Static and dynamic obstacles share the same representation. Our approach reaches the goal with a 100% success rate under prediction model mismatch, where the best baseline reaches 65%, and maintains up to three times the clearance in dense traffic. We also validate real-time operation on a physical robot.

</details>


### [94] [ScanDreamer: A Map-Grounded World Model for Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11605)
*Yunyi Gao,Yixing Shi,Yize Liu,Hai-Duong Tran,Nhat-Hoang P. Nguyen,Shuai Wang,Yi Deng,Shengming Liu,Sim Kuan Goh,Duc-Thinh Pham,Sameer Alam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ScanDreamer 通过在线二维扫描记忆地图、地图 grounded 世界模型与感知感知运动约束，显著提升四旋翼在未知杂乱环境中的避障导航成功率与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前瞻深度相机视场受限，现有世界模型智能体（如 AirDreamer）数据需求大，且缺乏显式几何记忆和对相机可观测范围的感知，难以在未知杂乱环境中可靠避障导航。

Method: 提出 ScanDreamer：由深度图像和机体位姿在线构建二维扫描记忆地图；世界模型、actor 与 critic 均以该地图为条件；利用地图定义测地进度奖励，并加入前向锥惩罚以抑制相机视场外的水平运动；在想象中于冻结地图上传播四旋翼位姿，并从地图几何导出进度与撞墙信号。

Result: 训练 0.8M 环境步（单 GPU 5.5 小时），在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 的 1560 个未见布局与参数范围 episode 上评估；三个种子均值±标准差：成功率 96.3±0.3%、碰撞率 3.6±0.3%，同在线交互预算下 AirDreamer 为 59.6±3.0% 与 28.8±2.8%；移除前向锥惩罚后碰撞率升至 9.9±3.1%。

Conclusion: 显式地图记忆、地图 grounded 想象与感知感知运动约束相结合，可显著提高四旋翼导航性能和数据效率。

Abstract: Quadrotors inspecting buildings and tunnels must navigate unknown clutter with a forward-facing depth camera whose limited field of view constrains scene understanding and collision-avoiding navigation. Recent world-model agents learn navigation through imagined experience (e.g., AirDreamer), but remain data-intensive and often lack explicit geometric memory and awareness of what the camera can observe. We propose ScanDreamer, a map-grounded world model that constructs an online 2D scan-memory map from depth images and vehicle pose. Our world model, actor, and critic are conditioned on this map, which also defines a geodesic progress reward. Moreover, a forward-cone penalty discourages horizontal motion outside the camera's viewing direction. During imagination, ScanDreamer propagates the quadrotor pose over a frozen map and derives progress and wall-contact signals from the mapped geometry. We train ScanDreamer for 0.8 million environment steps (5.5 h on one GPU) and evaluate it in NVIDIA Isaac Sim over 1560 episodes spanning unseen layouts and parameter ranges. Across three seeds (mean $\pm$ SD), ScanDreamer achieves 96.3 $\pm$ 0.3% success with 3.6 $\pm$ 0.3% collision, compared with 59.6 $\pm$ 3.0% success and 28.8 $\pm$ 2.8% collision for AirDreamer at the same online-interaction budget. Removing the forward-cone penalty increases collisions to 9.9 $\pm$ 3.1%. These results show that combining explicit map memory, map-grounded imagination, and perception-aware motion substantially improves navigation performance and data efficiency. The source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [95] [HARP-Bench: Benchmarking Robot Manipulation Around Active Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11123)
*Qinhong Zhou,Riley Zong,Chunru Lin,Diyi Hu,Shivraj Singh Bhatti,Hongxin Zhang,Lin Qiu,Hanqing Zeng,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 HARP-Bench，一个将主动参与的人类纳入机器人操作问题本身的基准，用于系统评估人机共享工作空间中的通用机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准通常假设机器人是唯一主动智能体，任务语义主要由指令和物体状态决定，忽视了真实场景中人类共享工作空间带来的隐式目标、任务贡献、物体可用性变化和安全运动约束等关键变化。

Method: HARP-Bench 将有人类参与的操作分为 Cueing、Contribution、Interruption 和 Co-presence 四类条件，并通过日常操作任务及与标准操作配对的人类在场变体进行受控比较；同时提供面向动态人类在场场景的演示生成流程。论文评估了 VLA 模型、模仿学习方法和启发式策略。

Result: 人类在场变体对现有策略构成挑战，且更高任务成功率并不稳定带来更高安全性；定量失败分析表明操作与恢复是剩余主要难点。使用 HARP-Bench 演示训练可提升仿真中的人类在场表现；真实世界单个任务族研究显示在人类贡献条件下性能提升，但在中断条件下未提升，说明数据有效性和迁移局限。

Conclusion: HARP-Bench 将人类活动视为操作问题的一部分而非背景变化，为人类共享工作空间中的机器人策略评估与训练提供了基准和演示生成工具，并揭示了当前策略在安全性、恢复和人机协作迁移方面的不足。

Abstract: Recent robot manipulation benchmarks have enabled systematic evaluation of general-purpose robot policies across diverse objects, scenes, instructions, and embodiments. However, most existing benchmarks still assume that the robot is the only active agent and that task semantics are determined primarily by the instruction and object state. This assumption overlooks a common source of real-world variation: humans actively sharing the workspace. In such settings, humans may reveal an implicit goal, complete part of the task, temporarily change object availability, or constrain safe robot motion while working nearby. We introduce HARP-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating robot manipulation policies in workspaces shared with active humans. HARP-Bench treats human activity as part of the manipulation problem, rather than as background scene variation. It organizes human-present manipulation into four conditions: Cueing, Contribution, Interruption, and Co-presence. These conditions are instantiated through everyday manipulation tasks, including paired human-present variants for controlled comparison with standard manipulation. HARP-Bench also provides a demonstration-generation pipeline for dynamic human-present settings. We evaluate VLA models, imitation-learning methods, and heuristic policies on HARP-Bench. Human-present variations challenge current policies, and higher task success does not consistently improve safety. Quantitative failure analysis identifies manipulation and recovery as the dominant remaining challenges. Training with HARP-Bench demonstrations improves human-present performance in simulation. A real-world study on one task family shows improved performance under human contribution, but not interruption, providing initial evidence of the data's utility and identifying limits of transfer. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [96] [Large-Scale Hierarchical Decision-Making in Robot Control via ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11090)
*Kai Pfeiffer,Jia Wang,Gordon Owusu Boateng,Yuquan Wang,Vincent Bonnet,Abderrahmane Kheddar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于ADMM的稀疏分层二次规划（SHQP）子问题求解器，用于机器人运动学与动力学控制中的ℓ0范数稀疏分层决策，通过高效替换ℓ0范数代理的辅助变量并优化线性代数表达式求值顺序，相比结构利用型内点法在低至中等精度区间最高减少23.5%计算时间，并扩展到人形机器人行走的全身动态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于ℓ0范数的稀疏分层非线性规划（SH-NLP）适合机器人运动控制中大规模候选末端执行器位置等分层决策问题；现有S-SHQP采用内点法求解SHQP子问题，虽能利用分层稀疏结构，但难以在连续且相关的控制步之间有效热启动。

Method: 构建交替方向乘子法（ADMM）以高效求解相关SHQP子问题；为表示ℓ0范数代理的辅助变量引入高效替换；针对可分离决策函数，通过按合适顺序计算线性代数表达式来提升求解效率。

Result: 在大规模运动学和动力学控制中，与结构利用型内点法相比，所提方法在低至中等精度区间将计算时间最多降低23.5%；进一步将分层决策扩展到人形机器人行走的全身动态控制，并突出该场景对高精度解的需求。

Conclusion: ADMM型SHQP子问题求解器能有效利用稀疏分层结构并改善连续控制步间的求解效率，但精度与计算时间存在权衡；将其扩展到人形机器人全身动态控制表明高精度求解在该类任务中仍然重要。

Abstract: Sparse hierarchical nonlinear programming (SH-NLP) based on the $\ell_0$-norm provides a powerful framework for integrated, efficient hierarchical decision-making in robot kinematic control, for example in the presence of a large set of possible end-effector candidate locations. While Sequential Sparse Hierarchical Quadratic Programming (S-SHQP) with interior-point method based SHQP sub-problem solvers exploit the hierarchical, sparse structure of such problems, they are less amenable to effective warm-starting between subsequent, coherent control steps. In this work, we formulate an Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) to efficiently solve related SHQP sub-problems. We introduce efficient substitutions for auxiliary variables representing $\ell_0$-norm surrogates. The solver efficiently handles separable decision functions by evaluating linear algebra expressions in the appropriate order. Results in large-scale kinematic and dynamic control show reduced computation times of up to 23.5% compared with a structure-exploiting interior-point method in low- to moderate-accuracy regimes. Furthermore, hierarchical decision-making is extended to whole-body dynamic control for humanoid walking, where the need for high-accuracy solutions is highlighted.

</details>


### [97] [Control-Aware Command Generation for Asynchronous Control and Action Inference with Vision-Based Policies towards Fast Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11089)
*Takayuki Osa,Kazuki Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种异步推理与控制框架，在不修改策略学习过程的前提下，通过将离散模型预测转化为连续目标位置的跟踪控制问题，并结合基于预测位置方差的自适应动作跳过，实现快速且平滑稳定的机器人任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉控制策略（含视觉-语言-动作模型）动作推理计算开销大，导致执行速度受限；作者希望仅从控制层面提升执行速度，而不改动底层策略学习。

Method: 构建异步推理与控制框架；把从不连续模型预测生成连续目标位置建模为跟踪控制问题，使异步推理适用于真实机器人；进一步根据预测位置方差自适应跳过动作以加速执行。

Result: 在双臂机器人上使用 Action Chunking Transformer 与 OpenVLA-OFT 进行实验，在保持高任务成功率的同时实现显著加速。

Conclusion: 无需修改底层策略学习，仅通过异步推理、跟踪控制与自适应动作跳过即可显著提升视觉控制策略的机器人执行速度，并保持运动平滑稳定与任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-based control policies, including vision-language-action models, have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex tasks. However, their execution speed is often limited by the high computational cost of action inference. In this work, we investigate what can be achieved at the control level without modifying the underlying policy learning process. We introduce an asynchronous inference and control framework that enables fast task execution while maintaining smooth and stable robot motions. By formulating the generation of continuous target positions from discontinuous model predictions as a tracking control problem, our method makes asynchronous inference practical for real robots. We further accelerate execution through adaptive action skipping based on the variance of predicted positions. Experiments with Action Chunking Transformer and OpenVLA-OFT on a bimanual robot demonstrate substantial speedups while preserving high task success rates.

</details>


### [98] [ObjectWorld: Scaling Object-Centric World Models with A Large-Scale 3D Full-Object Flow Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11085)
*Haonan Chen,Sicheng Isabella Lu,Haitian Li,Jiajin Wu,Hexiang Wei,Jiawei Zhang,Xin Fei,Yiwen Hou,Chenrui Tie,Chongkai Gao,Sheng Wang,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出3D全物体流（3D full-object flow）作为对象中心运动表示，并构建1210小时数据集与可预测完整3D物体运动的世界模型，用于可扩展机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从大规模视觉数据学习生成式世界模型有望实现可扩展机器人操作，但需要一种可大规模监督且对下游控制可操作的中间表示；现有可见表面物体流无法显式表达完整物体（尤其遮挡区域）的三维运动。

Method: 将可见表面物体流扩展为覆盖目标物体完整表面（含遮挡区域）的稠密3D点轨迹，统一适用于刚性与可变形物体；构建1210小时全物体流数据集，融合真实视频与物理仿真，并提供从真实观测恢复全物体运动的可扩展标注流程及标注质量评估指标；训练以图像和任务描述为输入、在重建物体几何上预测稠密全物体流的世界模型。

Result: 实验表明标注质量可靠，流预测具备数据可扩展性，并在新场景与新机器人本体上实现有效的下游操作。

Conclusion: 3D全物体流是一种可大规模监督且可用于控制的对象中心运动表示；配套数据集与世界模型能够提升完整3D物体运动预测及跨场景、跨本体的机器人操作泛化能力。

Abstract: Generative world modeling from large-scale visual data offers a promising path toward scalable robot manipulation. This calls for an intermediate representation that can be supervised at scale and remains actionable for downstream control. We propose 3D full-object flow, an object-centric motion representation that assigns dense 3D trajectories to points across the complete surface of a target object, including its occluded regions. By extending visible-surface object flow to the full object geometry, this representation makes complete object motion explicit and applies uniformly to both rigid and deformable objects. To realize the potential of this representation at scale, we introduce a 1210-hour full-object flow dataset that addresses the scarcity of large-scale supervision for complete 3D object motion. The dataset integrates diverse real-world videos and physical simulations, with a scalable annotation pipeline for recovering full-object motion from real-world observations and quality metrics for systematically assessing annotation quality. We then train a world model that takes an image and a task description as input and predicts dense full-object flow over the reconstructed object geometry. Experiments show reliable annotation quality, data-scalable flow prediction, and effective downstream manipulation across novel scenes and robot embodiments. Project website with additional results and videos: this https URL .

</details>


### [99] [Floquet-Parameterized Koopman-MPC for Tracking Periodic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11050)
*Kartik A. Pant,Vishnu Vijay,Yufei Zhu,Xintong Deng,Taewoo Nam,Inseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息Koopman学习框架，将周期轨道稳定性约束引入线性预测器，并用于MPC跟踪周期参考；在周期强迫小车倒立摆上与纯数据驱动基线对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统实时跟踪周期轨道困难，复杂耦合系统（如系留风筝）真值模型参数难获取；需要利用周期结构与稳定性先验学习物理一致的线性预测器。

Method: 结合周期动力学结构与周期状态-输入轨迹数据，在提升/可观测空间学习线性预测器；利用Floquet理论通过单周期状态转移矩阵（monodromy matrix）获取周期稳定性信息，并以开环monodromy约束和最小二乘EDMD学习Floquet参数化线性预测器，约束搜索空间；随后基于该预测器设计MPC以跟踪周期参考。

Result: 在周期强迫的cartpole系统上评估所设计框架，并与纯数据驱动基线比较；摘要未给出具体定量性能指标。

Conclusion: 将周期轨道稳定性先验嵌入Koopman/EDMD学习可约束搜索空间并促进物理一致的线性预测器学习，从而支持基于MPC的周期参考跟踪；实验用于验证该框架相对纯数据驱动基线的有效性。

Abstract: Designing real-time control for nonlinear systems to track periodic orbits can be challenging, especially for complex and coupled dynamical systems (e.g., tethered kites), where the true model parameters are hard to obtain. To address this, we propose a physics-informed Koopman learning approach that incorporates periodic-orbit stability directly into the learned linear predictor. The approach combines the underlying periodic structure of the dynamics along with periodic state-input trajectory data to learn physically consistent linear predictors (in lifted/observable space). We leverage Floquet theory, which studies the stability of periodic orbits in nonlinear systems, to obtain the periodic stability information of a given orbit via a monodromy matrix (i.e., the state transition matrix over an entire period). Subsequently, we impose open-loop monodromy constraints to learn Floquet-parameterized linear predictors using a least-squares Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) formulation, thereby constraining the search space and aiding in Koopman learning. We then utilize these linear predictors to design a model predictive control (MPC) law for tracking periodic references. Finally, we evaluate the designed framework on a cartpole system under periodic forcing and compare it against a purely data-driven baseline.

</details>


### [100] [RoboMoCap: A Large-Scale Whole-Body Motion Capture Dataset with Egocentric Vision for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10994)
*Mayur Deshmukh,Aaron Tung,Junyi Li,Nikash Bhardwaj,Ademi Adeniji,Vincent Liu,Michael Cho,Jiafei Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboMoCap：一个面向以运动为中心的人形机器人学习的大规模多模态数据集，将以第一人称视角视觉与高精度光学全身动捕相结合，并覆盖多样化的移动与操作行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需协调移动与操作，从人类演示学习时需准确的全身运动与第一人称视觉，尤其是第一人称常不可见的下肢运动和足部放置；现有第一人称数据集常缺乏高保真光学动捕或对多样移动—操作行为的系统覆盖。

Method: RoboMoCap 包含 14 名参与者、超过 200 小时记录，配备 6 个头部相机、腕部视觉与惯性传感，以及 120 Hz 标记点光学全身动捕；系统覆盖变速行走、转向、受限导航、地形穿越、物体搬运及移动中操作等移动基元、动态全身运动和移动—操作任务，并提供时间对齐的多模态观测与度量全身运动表示。

Result: 在三个基准任务上系统评估现有方法：第一人称全身运动重建、足部接触与放置估计、人到人形机器人的运动重定向。

Conclusion: 通过将第一人称视觉与多样化移动和交互场景中的高保真人体运动配对，RoboMoCap 为第一人称运动理解及从人类演示进行人形机器人学习提供基础。

Abstract: Humanoid robots must coordinate locomotion and manipulation to navigate complex environments and perform everyday tasks. Learning such behaviours from human demonstrations requires accurate whole-body motion paired with egocentric vision, particularly for lower-body movements and foot placements that are frequently invisible from a first-person perspective. However, existing egocentric datasets often lack high-fidelity optical motion capture or systematic coverage of diverse loco-manipulation behaviours. To address this gap, we introduce RoboMoCap, a large-scale multimodal dataset combining egocentric vision with marker-based optical motion capture, specifically designed for locomotion-centric humanoid learning. RoboMoCap comprises over 200 hours of recordings from 14 participants, featuring six head-mounted cameras, wrist-mounted visual and inertial sensing, and optical full-body motion capture at 120 Hz. The dataset systematically covers diverse locomotion primitives, dynamic whole-body movements, and loco-manipulation tasks, including variable-speed walking, turning, constrained navigation, terrain traversal, object transportation, and manipulation during locomotion. It provides temporally aligned multimodal observations and metric whole-body motion representations, enabling the study of human movement beyond directly observable body regions. We systematically evaluate existing methods on three benchmark tasks: egocentric full-body motion reconstruction, foot contact and placement estimation, and human-to-humanoid motion retargeting. By pairing egocentric vision with high-fidelity human motion across diverse locomotion and interaction scenarios, RoboMoCap provides a foundation for egocentric motion understanding and humanoid learning from human demonstrations.

</details>


### [101] [BAT-WAM: Bridging Action and Touch in World-Action Models for Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10967)
*Dong Fang,Feixuan Huang,Yuanxin Zhong,Yuxiang Gao,Qi Song,Yunlong Wang,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BAT-WAM框架，将视觉、关节状态与触觉表征桥接起来，用于世界动作模型中的灵巧操作，并处理关节状态和全手触觉部分或完全缺失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM灵巧操作依赖手部构型与接触信息，但关节状态和触觉常缺失；RGB虽提供场景与手物几何，却不足以可靠恢复缺失模态，且手状态与触觉信息存在相关性。

Method: BAT-WAM为触觉和动作流分别学习码本与解码器，用RGB条件化的连续潜变量桥连接两流进行双向学习，并以视觉信息作为连续上下文。

Result: 在HTBench下，完全掩码时触觉重建RMSE相对图像条件HandTouch降低34.73%，部分掩码时降低38.84%；基于目标时刻RGB和历史动作的未来触觉预测平均RMSE相对OpenWAM with HandTouch降低85.56%；真实机器人实验优于相关WAM基线，并在未见物体和场景上展现明显泛化提升。

Conclusion: 联合建模视觉上下文、手部状态与触觉是多模态WAM的一种有效表征方法。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with world action models (WAMs) relies on hand configuration and contact information, yet joint-state and whole-hand tactile observations may be partially or entirely missing. RGB images provide scene context and hand-object geometry but are insufficient to reliably recover these missing modalities. We further observe a correlation between hand state and tactile information. Motivated by this observation, we introduce BAT-WAM, a framework that bridges visual, joint-state, and tactile representations for WAMs. BAT-WAM learns separate codebooks and decoders for the tactile and action streams. An RGB-conditioned continuous latent bridge connects the two streams for bidirectional learning, while visual information serves as continuous context. Under the HTBench evaluation protocol, BAT-WAM reduces the root mean squared error (RMSE) of tactile reconstruction by 34.73% under fully masked conditions and 38.84% under partially masked conditions, relative to an image-conditioned HandTouch checkpoint. For future tactile prediction conditioned on target-time RGB and historical actions, it reduces mean RMSE by 85.56% relative to OpenWAM with HandTouch. Real-robot experiments further show that BAT-WAM consistently outperforms related WAM baselines in dexterous manipulation. Generalization experiments on unseen objects and scenes also demonstrate substantial improvements over baseline models. These findings support joint modeling of visual context, hand state, and touch as a representation approach for multimodal WAMs.

</details>


### [102] [Explicitly capturing demonstrator multi-modality for distillation into diffusion policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10949)
*Shao Yuan Chew Chia,Rebecca H. Jiang,Abhinav Agarwal,Andreas Hofmann,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过将 vine sampling 引入行为克隆数据采集，解决扩散策略蒸馏中观测-动作配对歧义，使策略更好复现示教者的多模态条件动作分布并提升探索相关下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏扩散策略的输出多样性不仅影响成功率，也影响测试时搜索与策略改进等需要探索的下游应用；因此希望将多模态示教者蒸馏为能复现其条件动作分布的扩散策略。标准行为克隆用滑动窗口将唯一观测历史与动作配对，导致数据既可被多模态策略拟合，也可被高 Lipschitz 常数的单模态策略拟合；稀疏数据下扩散策略常记忆每个观测的一个动作。

Method: 作者改编 vine sampling 用于行为克隆数据采集：在示教者访问的部分状态上，从完全相同的观测查询示教者 k 次，从而显式获得同一观测下的多个动作。并在合成任务上研究每个观测的动作数 k 对学习策略的影响，随后在 2D truck unloading 任务上验证。

Result: 合成任务上，较大的 k 使学习到的策略更好地匹配示教者的条件分布，包括其多模态性和多样性；在 2D truck unloading 任务上，vine sampling 得到的策略更接近示教者，并显著提升 best-of-N 与 expert iteration 性能。

Conclusion: vine sampling 能消除滑动窗口行为克隆数据中的观测-动作配对歧义，有助于将多模态示教者蒸馏为具有多样输出并更好复现条件动作分布的扩散策略。

Abstract: The diversity of a distilled diffusion policy's outputs matters beyond how it impacts the policy's success rate. In particular, downstream applications like test-time search and policy improvement require exploration. Thus, we want to distill multi-modal demonstrators into diffusion policies which reproduce their conditional action distributions. Standard data collection for behavior cloning applies a sliding window to demonstration episodes, which pairs unique observation histories with actions. These datasets can be fit by a policy that is either multi-modal, or unimodal with high Lipschitz constant to capture a diversity of actions seen at nearby observation samples. Prior work finds that diffusion policies trained on sparse data learn the latter, memorizing one action per observation. We adapt vine sampling to behavior cloning data collection to resolve this ambiguity: at some states the demonstrator visits, query it $k$ times from exactly the same observation. On a synthetic task, we study how the number of actions per observation, $k$, affects the learned policy, and find that larger $k$ yields a policy that better matches the demonstrator's conditional distribution, including its multi-modality and diversity. On a 2D truck unloading task, vine sampling likewise leads to a policy closer to the demonstrator and substantially improves both best-of-$N$ and expert iteration performance.

</details>


### [103] [BRACE: Belief-guided Residual Adaptive Control for Safe Navigation under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10938)
*Jaeyoun Choi,Niklas Schmid,Oswin So,Augustinos Saravanos,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BRACE 是一种分层强化学习框架，用于未知、有界且空间变化的扰动下的到达-规避导航：它从模型预测误差推断扰动信念，并用该信念调节高层策略以自适应调整参考轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航中的扰动通常随空间变化且可能不连续，而跟踪控制器常假设有足够控制权限来抑制扰动并维持安全；该假设失效的情形仍缺乏研究。

Method: 提出 Belief-guided Residual Adaptive Control（BRACE）：从模型预测误差推断扰动信念，以其为条件训练高层策略来调整参考轨迹；策略由标称项与信念引导的残差项组成，并采用似然比优势重加权启发式联合训练。

Result: 在三组具有突变风场的四旋翼仿真环境和 Crazyflie 硬件风扇扰动实验中，BRACE 的到达-规避性能优于模型预测控制（MPC）和强化学习基线。

Conclusion: BRACE 通过信念引导的残差自适应控制，在未知、有界、空间变化的扰动下提升了到达-规避导航性能，并得到仿真与硬件验证。

Abstract: Disturbances encountered during autonomous navigation generally vary across space, often discontinuously, as obstacles redirect wind and changing terrain alters traction. Tracking controllers typically assume sufficient control authority to mitigate disturbances and maintain safety; the regime where this assumption fails remains underexplored. We present Belief-guided Residual Adaptive Control (BRACE), a hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework for reach-avoid navigation under unknown, bounded, spatially varying disturbances. BRACE infers a disturbance belief from model prediction errors and uses that to condition a high-level policy that adapts the reference trajectory. The policy combines a nominal and a belief-guided residual term, trained jointly with a likelihood-ratio advantage reweighting heuristic. We evaluate BRACE in three simulated quadcopter environments with abruptly switching wind fields, and validate it on Crazyflie hardware with fan-generated disturbances. Across these settings, BRACE improves reach-avoid performance over model predictive control and RL baselines.

</details>


### [104] [Beating the Pool, Not the Game: A White-Box Equilibrium Agent for Language-Based Economic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10932)
*Sudhanshu Srivastava,Faisal Aqlan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 sudsho：一个在 GLEE 竞赛中执行讨价还价、谈判与说服的规则型智能体，推理时零 LLM 调用，依靠经验池百分位设定需求目标并用均衡份额钳制约束报价与接受，最终获得 2056 评分、$0 推理成本，账号排名 26/71。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞赛以同一配置下历史对局经验池的收益百分位计分时，需要一种低成本、可验证且能利用池信息设定合理需求与接受阈值的策略，同时避免推理时 LLM 调用带来的成本与不确定性。

Method: 采用规则型确定性策略：用经验池百分位设定需求目标，以钳制后的均衡份额限定需求和接受条件；除说服混合外，每步动作均为可见状态的确定性函数。通过将冻结后的动作日志回放至策略来验证对齐，并用三个冻结对手池快照进行回放评估池感知钳制的作用。

Result: 在 117,280 局比赛中，智能体以 $0 推理成本达到 2056 总体评分；按最佳智能体（同策略副本）计，账号在 71 个排名账号中列第 26。对三个冻结对手池快照的回放显示，池感知钳制带来每局讨价还价评分平均 274 至 330 分的提升，且在由日志对手决策固定回放的条件下该效应仍成立。

Conclusion: 规则化、池感知的 sudsho 能在零推理成本下取得有竞争力的表现，并可通过日志回放验证策略对齐；其中池感知份额钳制是提升讨价还价评分的关键机制。

Abstract: We describe sudsho, a rule-based agent for the GLEE Competition that plays bargaining, negotiation, and persuasion with zero inference-time LLM calls. Games are scored as payoff percentiles against an empirical pool of prior play on the same configuration, so pool percentiles set our demand targets while clamped equilibrium shares bound demands and acceptance. Every move is a deterministic function of the visible state (persuasion mixing aside), so alignment is verified by replaying the post-freeze move log through the policy. Across 117,280 games the agent reached an overall rating of 2056 at $0 inference cost, and the account, ranked by its best agent (a same-policy replica), placed 26th of 71 ranked accounts. A replay against three frozen snapshots of the opponent pool credits the pool-aware clamp with 274 to 330 points of mean per-game bargaining rating, an effect that holds where logged opponent decisions fix the replay.

</details>


### [105] [Zero-Shot VLA Adaptation with Retrieved Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10894)
*Subin Kim,Anh-Quan Pham,Dinesh Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAGu 将检索增强生成（RAG）思想引入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过从域不匹配的真实演示数据中检索并适配轨迹，引导流匹配推理，从而显著提升跨域机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模参数化 VLA 模型在相机视角、背景、物体身份或机器人形态等轻微域偏移下性能显著下降，作者希望利用域不匹配的“in-the-wild”演示数据（如 DROID）来增强目标域中的 VLA 行为。

Method: RAGu 针对机器人 RAG 的特定挑战提出三项设计：1）用简单几何标定将源演示变换到目标设置，减少跨域差异；2）采用由粗到细的检索策略，从大量预变形候选轨迹中选择与基础 VLA 最兼容的轨迹；3）在流匹配推理过程中引导 VLA，而非重训练基础模型或扩展其有限上下文窗口。

Result: 在真实世界、仿真、仿真到真实迁移以及跨形态人形机器人操作中评估 RAGu；真实世界 VLA 成功率从 20% 提升到 80%，并在仿真中优于最相关的基线方法。

Conclusion: 从域不匹配的真实演示中检索并几何适配轨迹，可有效增强 VLA 的跨域鲁棒性和操作性能，为机器人检索增强推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Pre-training large parametric "Vision-Language-Action" (VLA) models on large demonstration datasets has recently yielded promising results for general-purpose manipulation. However, these models still struggle under even small domain shifts relative to the training data, such as changes in camera viewpoint, background, object identity, or robot morphology. Motivated by the success of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) in language models, we pursue retrieval to boost VLA performance.
