Specifically, we seek to leverage retrieval from domain-mismatched, "in-the-wild" demonstration datasets like DROID to guide a VLA's behavior in a new target domain. To achieve this, we introduce RAGu, which overcomes robotics-specific challenges for RAG: (1) It incorporates simple geometric calibration to transform source demonstrations into the target setup and reduce cross-domain differences. (2) It uses a coarse-to-fine retrieval strategy that selects, from a large pool of pre-warped candidates, the trajectory most compatible with the base VLA. (3) It guides the VLA's flow-matching inference procedure rather than seeking to retrain the base model or expand its limited context window. We evaluate RAGu across real-world, simulation, sim-to-real transfer, and cross-embodiment humanoid manipulation. We find it improves real-world VLA success rates from 20% to 80% and outperforms the closest-related baselines in sim. Project website: this http URL

</details>


### [106] [Sampling-based Model Predictive Control as Sequential Monte Carlo Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10877)
*Austin Garrett,Muyang Yan,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文将 predictive sampling、MPPI、交叉熵方法及其 biased/colored-noise/annealed 变体统一为同一个基于序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）的推理算法，并由提议分布、更新规则和重采样 ESS 阈值三个独立维度参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型模型预测控制（MPC）通常被视为一族彼此不同的算法；作者希望给出统一推断视角，并解决长时域下的粒子退化以及提议分布设计问题。

Method: 提出单一 SMC 推断算法，通过三个轴指定具体算法：提议分布、将代价加权粒子归约为名义计划的更新规则、触发重采样的有效样本量阈值。在 MuJoCo 非线性控制、障碍场开环导航、7-DoF 运动规划、非抓取 push-T 上评估，包含仿真与 Franka FR3 硬件实验。

Result: 重采样可缓解长时域粒子退化；在导航域 28-knot 时域下，相比不重采样基线将碰撞率降低 30%。可编程提议优于扰动式基线，push-T 成功率从仿真 1% 提升到 100%，硬件从 10% 提升到 90%。

Conclusion: SMC 为采样型 MPC 提供了统一框架，重采样与可编程提议是提升长时域规划和困难操作任务性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) is treated as a family of distinct algorithms: predictive sampling, MPPI, the cross-entropy method, and their biased, colored-noise, and annealed variants all sample control sequences and reweight them by cost. We show a single Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) inference algorithm implements them all, specified by three independent axes: the proposal, the update rule that reduces cost-weighted particles to a nominal plan, and the effective-sample-size threshold for resampling. We evaluate SMC-based planning on MuJoCo non-linear control, open-loop navigation through obstacle fields, 7-DoF motion planning, and non-prehensile push-T, in simulation and on a Franka FR3. Resampling combats particle degeneracy at long horizons. At a 28-knot horizon in our navigation domain it cut the collision rate by 30% over non-resampling baselines. Programmable proposals improve push-T success over perturbative baselines, improving from 1% to 100% in simulation and 10% to 90% on hardware.

</details>


### [107] [DreamVLA: From Generated Human Motions to Humanoid Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10872)
*Kevin Park,Izat Rasyad,Dvij Kalaria,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamVLA 利用生成的运动轨迹引导仿真中的强化学习，并通过参考条件 DAgger 蒸馏将专家纠错轨迹用于训练 VLA 策略，从而避免为新任务采集遥操作数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练 VLA 策略通常依赖大量机器人示范数据，人类遥操作成本高且繁琐，尤其对全身人形交互；仿真强化学习可扩展数据，但设计任务特定奖励困难。

Method: 提出 DreamVLA：用生成运动轨迹指导仿真 RL 生成可靠任务执行轨迹，再用于仿真中训练 VLA。核心是参考条件 DAgger 蒸馏：在学习者 rollout 失败后，从采样的学习者状态以相同参考后缀重启 RL 专家，聚合成功纠错轨迹，以缓解离线模仿留下的分布偏移。

Result: 评估了从平台拾取、打开抽屉、按下按钮、行走并从平台拾取等任务，并对 DAgger 迭代次数、残差 RL 和生成数据集规模进行了消融；摘要未给出定量结果。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了无需新任务特定遥操作数据、在仿真中利用生成轨迹与专家纠错来训练 VLA 的可行路径，有助于缓解人形全身交互中的数据瓶颈。

Abstract: Training vision-language-action (VLA) policies typically depends on substantial robot demonstration data, making human teleoperation a costly and cumbersome bottleneck, particularly for whole-body humanoid interaction. Reinforcement learning in simulation offers an alternative for scaling this data, but designing task-specific rewards remains challenging. To address this, we propose DreamVLA, which uses generated motion trajectories to guide reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation, converting them into reliable task execution trajectories. These trajectories train VLA models in simulation without new task-specific teleoperation data. Our central contribution is a reference-conditioned DAgger distillation procedure: after a failed learner rollout, we restart the RL expert from a sampled learner state with the same reference suffix and aggregate successful corrective trajectories, addressing the distribution shift left by offline imitation. We evaluate picking from a platform, opening a drawer, pressing a button, and walking and picking from a platform, with ablations of DAgger iterations, residual RL, and generated dataset scale. Project website: this http URL .

</details>


### [108] [MoE-DAgger: Transition-Aware Router-Entropy Gating for Selective Human Correction in Phase-Structured Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10825)
*Sungjun Heo,Sungjun Yang,Heedon Jeong,Junhyoung Ha,Jeong hwan Jeon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoE-DAgger利用因果路由历史区分正常相邻阶段切换与异常高熵，从而在DAgger中更精准地选择介入与纠正时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有序阶段的机器人操作中，仅用阶段监督软MoE的路由熵无法判断高熵是正常相邻阶段交接，还是对非相邻/更早阶段的支持，导致选择性数据采集和介入决策不充分。

Method: 提出MoE-DAgger，在不向执行策略提供阶段标签、进度变量或硬阶段命令的情况下，用因果路由历史设计采集规则：允许相邻阶段切换自行稳定；若高熵在非切换期持续、切换超时或路由持续偏向更早阶段，则请求纠正。

Result: 在匹配主动人工控制预算下，Green任务成功率达84.0%，Red达78.0%，分别比阶段无关的过滤熵高22和26个百分点，每任务50次 rollout 未观察到碰撞。每任务50条共享无介入轨迹中，选择15和25条轨迹时，其首次提议分别有83.3%和80.0%落在预定义失败窗口内的失败 rollout 上，优于时序动作块不一致的70.0%/72.0%和集成不一致的63.3%/60.0%。

Conclusion: Shadow replay与主动无否决消融表明，抑制正常相邻阶段交接处的高熵查询是改善介入预算分配的关键，MoE-DAgger能更有效地选择需纠正的 learner rollout。

Abstract: Selective data acquisition in Dataset Aggregation (DAgger) on robots requires deciding which learner rollouts to correct and when to intervene. In manipulation with ordered phases, router entropy from a phase-supervised soft mixture-of-experts (MoE) is insufficient: the same value can reflect either a normal adjacent-phase handoff or support for nonadjacent or earlier phases. We propose MoE-DAgger, which uses causal router history to distinguish these cases without providing the policy with a phase label, progress variable, or hard phase command during execution. The acquisition rule allows an adjacent transition to settle, but requests correction when high entropy persists outside such a transition, the transition times out, or routing persistently favors an earlier phase. Under matched active human-control budgets, MoE-DAgger achieves 84.0% task success on Green and 78.0% on Red, exceeding phase-unaware filtered entropy by 22 percentage points on Green and 26 points on Red, with no observed collisions in 50 rollouts per task. On 50 shared intervention-free traces per task, 83.3% and 80.0% of its first proposals fall on failed rollouts within predefined failure windows when 15 and 25 traces are selected, compared with 70.0% and 72.0% for temporal action-chunk disagreement and 63.3% and 60.0% for ensemble disagreement. Shadow replay and an active no-veto ablation indicate that suppressing queries at normal adjacent handoffs is a key contributor to the improved allocation.

</details>


### [109] [Selective Cooperative Perception via Blind Spot Reasoning and Agentic Dialogue with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10819)
*Chengyuan Ma,Shixiao Liang,Hangyu Li,Zheng Li,Hang Zhou,Keke Long,Bofeng Cao,Zhaohui Liang,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种由视觉语言模型驱动的智能体框架，用于盲区推理与对话式协同感知，通过仅在安全关键场景和关键遮挡对象上按需协同，提升多智能体交通协同感知的效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时协同感知（CP）资源消耗大，难以大规模部署；若能仅在安全关键场景及关键遮挡对象上选择性执行CP，可显著降低通信与计算开销，而复杂动态环境中的推理任务适合由VLM承担。

Method: 设计混合架构：融合传统目标检测以稳定感知、视觉语言推理以识别影响驾驶的关键遮挡、以及基于物理的几何模块以对齐真实可见性约束；引入Chain-of-Thought提示机制增强可解释性与意图感知决策；采集城市交通中网联车辆真实数据，并比较四种先进VLM的驾驶关键盲区识别与对话性能。

Result: 驾驶关键盲区检测准确率超过80%，对话驱动的感知共享成功率超过90%。

Conclusion: 该方法为传统CP流程提供可扩展、可解释且带宽高效的替代方案，并为未来网联交通系统中的智能体通信设计提供新见解。

Abstract: Real-time cooperative perception (CP) in multi-agent traffic environments is highly resource-intensive, which posing scalability challenges for large-scale deployment. Efficiency can be significantly improved by selectively performing CP only in safety-critical scenarios and for key occluding objects. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are well-suited for complex reasoning in dynamic and uncertain environments, making them a promising tool for this selective CP task. This study proposes a VLM-driven agentic framework for blind spot reasoning and dialogue-based CP. A mixed-model architecture is designed that integrates conventional object detection for stable perception, vision-language reasoning for identifying impactful occlusions, and a physics-based geometric module to align reasoning with real-world visibility constraints. The framework also incorporates a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting mechanism to enhance interpretability and intent-aware decision-making. We collect real-world data from connected vehicles operating in urban traffic and compare the driving-critical blind spot identification and dialogue performance of four state-of-the-art VLMs. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves over 80% accuracy in driving-critical blind spot detection and over 90% success in dialogue-driven perception sharing. This work provides a scalable, interpretable, and bandwidth-efficient alternative to conventional CP pipelines, and offers new insights into agentic communication design for future connected traffic systems.

</details>


### [110] [Passivity-Guaranteed Variable Impedance and Compliance Control via Online Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10778)
*Huseyin Tugcan Dinc,Ersin Daş,Nikolas Thelenberg,Christian Ott*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于二次规划的变阻抗参数优化框架，通过调制期望惯量补偿刚度变化注入的能量以保证无源性，并在Kinova Gen3机械臂上验证了无需能量罐或额外阻尼即可实现较少保守的刚度跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变阻抗控制在线调整阻抗参数可增强交互能力，但时变阻抗参数可能向系统注入能量并破坏无源性，尤其任务需要快速刚度变化时；现有方法通过增大阻尼或限制刚度曲线来解决，但常损害动态响应。

Method: 提出基于二次规划的变阻抗参数优化框架：通过调制期望惯量补偿刚度变化注入的能量并保持临界阻尼；针对对角阻抗矩阵，以惯量和刚度的变化率为优化变量，在凸二次规划中施加无源性约束；进一步扩展到顺应控制，将期望惯量与机器人构型相关的惯量矩阵耦合，并利用基于Cholesky分解的特征分解优化谱权重以保持无源性。

Result: 在Kinova Gen3 7自由度机械臂上进行带人类交互扰动的实验，验证了两种方法；与能量罐方法和非无源性方法相比，所提框架实现了较少保守的刚度跟踪，且不需要能量储库或额外阻尼开销。

Conclusion: 该框架可在保证无源性的同时改善变阻抗控制的动态响应，避免依赖能量罐或增大阻尼，并实现较少保守的刚度跟踪。

Abstract: Variable impedance control enhances the interaction capability of robot manipulators by adapting impedance parameters online. However, time-varying impedance parameters may inject energy into the system and violate passivity, particularly when tasks demand rapid stiffness changes. Existing methods address this by increasing damping or limiting the stiffness profile, which often degrades the dynamic response. In this work, we propose a quadratic programming-based parameter optimization framework that guarantees passivity by modulating the desired inertia to compensate for energy injected by stiffness variations, while preserving a critically damped system. For the case of diagonal impedance matrices, we optimize the rates of change of inertia and stiffness subject to a passivity constraint formulated as a convex quadratic program. We extend the framework to compliance control, where the desired inertia is coupled to the robot's configuration-dependent inertia matrix. We use an eigendecomposition based on Cholesky factorization to optimize the spectral weights while maintaining passivity. Experiments on the Kinova Gen3 7-degree-of-freedom manipulator validate both methods with human interaction perturbations. Comparisons with an energy tank approach and a nonpassivity-based method demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves less conservative stiffness tracking without requiring an energy reservoir or damping overhead.

</details>


### [111] [REINS: A Self-Evolving Agent Harness for Real-Time Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10748)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Haoran Tang,Shijun Liu,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出REINS运行时harness，通过将LLM规划输出与学习动力学模型、校准谓词验证及索引规则记忆耦合，实现实时且自演化的轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM轨迹规划方法要么让LLM留在决策环中而超出毫秒级控制预算，要么依赖离线规则或训练时监督，导致运行时规划器无法演化；中间混合方案也缺乏可靠harness所需的验证与巩固闭环。

Method: REINS建立三类LLM-规划器耦合：将LLM输出接地到学习的变量级动力学模型；用校准谓词和前向rollout进行验证；将验证输出巩固为索引规则记忆。熟悉场景通过O(log n)记忆检索解决而不重新调用LLM，新场景调用LLM并用验证输出丰富记忆，形成自演化闭环且不修改LLM权重。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界基准的战术决策层，REINS达到98%以上合规性、近50%碰撞减少、1.9-4.6 ms延迟；随着记忆成熟，LLM调用率单调下降。

Conclusion: REINS表明运行时harness可使LLM规划器在满足实时约束的同时自演化并转化为可部署规划器，且无需修改LLM权重。

Abstract: Agent harnesses -- runtime scaffolds that wrap an LLM's reasoning loop with tool dispatch, verification, and persistent memory -- have become the standard substrate for deploying LLMs as autonomous agents, as demonstrated by Claude Code and Codex in software engineering. In trajectory planning, however, this paradigm has not taken hold: existing LLM-based trajectory planning methods either keep the LLM in the decision loop, exceeding the millisecond-level control budget, or offload it to offline rules or training-time supervision -- leaving the runtime planner non-evolving. Intermediate hybrids still lack the verification-and-consolidation loop that makes harnesses reliable. We ask: what runtime harness would let an LLM planner meet real-time constraints while self-evolving into a deployable one? We propose REINS, a harness built on three LLM-planner couplings: LLM outputs are (i) grounded to a learned variable-level dynamics model, (ii) verified via calibrated predicates and forward rollout, and (iii) consolidated into an indexed rule memory that serves future scenes in O(log n) without re-invoking the LLM. The harness closes a self-evolution loop: familiar scenes resolve by memory lookup; novel scenes invoke the LLM, whose verified outputs enrich the memory. Without modifying LLM weights, REINS attains 98%+ compliance, nearly 50% collision reduction, and 1.9-4.6 ms latency on simulated and real-world benchmarks at the tactical decision layer, with LLM invocation rate monotonically declining as memory matures. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [112] [Burden, Bounty, or Bliss? Muscle-Aware Retargeting and Null-Space Exploration for Whole-Body Musculoskeletal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10682)
*Ruiyang Kong,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从人体运动到全身肌肉骨骼控制策略的运动到策略流水线，通过肌肉感知重定向与零空间探索解决参考轨迹难以肌肉驱动复现和700维冗余动作空间探索困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人体运动学习全身肌肉骨骼控制对人形机器人、辅助设备和康复系统很重要，但现有方法面临两个主要挑战：参考轨迹在运动学上合理却难以通过肌肉驱动动力学复现；700维高度冗余动作空间中的无结构探索容易破坏目标运动。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线：第一阶段肌肉感知重定向（MAR）在语义、解剖和接触等硬约束下对运动学轨迹进行有界自适应，并通过分析参考轨迹伪影对肌肉驱动跟踪的影响来确定约束边界；第二阶段零空间探索（NSE）估计从肌肉兴奋到广义力的单控制帧映射，并沿其零空间向价值引导流策略的动作中注入有界、解剖结构化的扰动。

Result: 在24段动作捕捉基准上，MAR与NSE结合可求解所有参考轨迹，以基线一半样本达到完整时间跨度，并将关节中心误差降低44%且无后期崩溃；据作者所知，这是首个将单目视频端到端转换为可控制700块肌肉的全身模仿策略的训练流水线。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用肌肉冗余进行有界重定向和结构化零空间探索，可有效提升高维肌肉骨骼全身模仿学习的可解性、样本效率与跟踪精度，并实现从单目视频到700肌肉全身控制策略的端到端学习。

Abstract: Learning whole-body musculoskeletal control from human motion is important for developing human-like robots, assistive devices, and rehabilitation systems. This task presents two main challenges, namely reference trajectories that are kinematically plausible but difficult to reproduce through muscle-driven dynamics, and a highly redundant 700-dimensional action space in which unstructured exploration can easily disrupt the target motion. In this paper, we propose a motion-to-policy pipeline that exploits muscle redundancy in two complementary stages. First, muscle-aware retargeting (MAR) performs bounded adaptation of kinematic trajectories subject to hard semantic, anatomical, and contact constraints. We motivate the constraint bounds by analyzing how reference-trajectory artifacts affect muscle-driven tracking. Second, null-space exploration (NSE) estimates a one-control-frame mapping from muscle excitations to generalized forces and injects bounded, anatomically structured perturbations along its null space into the actions of a value-guided flow policy. On a 24-clip motion-capture benchmark, MAR and NSE together solve every reference, reach the full horizon with half the samples of the baseline pipeline, and lower joint-center error by 44% without late collapse. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end pipeline that transforms monocular video into a trained whole-body imitation policy controlling 700 muscles.

</details>


### [113] [Task-Critical Part Alignment Improves Imitation of Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10664)
*Denis Hadjivelichkov,Sebastian Ægidius,Valerio Modugno,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文对DINOBot框架进行改进，通过RANSAC离群点剔除和任务相关交互区域语义对应优化，提升物体-物体交互模仿学习的对齐精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DINOBot在模仿学习物体-物体交互时，对齐阶段受深度噪声和错误特征匹配影响，且语义对应未充分聚焦任务关键交互区域，导致迁移到真实场景的鲁棒性和准确性不足。

Method: 在配准/对齐阶段引入RANSAC进行离群点剔除，以抑制噪声深度数据和虚假特征匹配；同时改进语义对应流程，聚焦任务相关的交互区域，使学习到的表示优先关注影响成功操作的关键物体特征。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务（抓取、插入、摇动、倾倒）中评估，相比基线整体任务成功率提升13.8%，并且对齐过程收敛更快。

Conclusion: 所提出的增强方法有效提升了机器人物体-物体交互模仿学习的对齐精度、鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: This paper presents enhancements to the DINOBot framework for imitation learning of object-object interactions. We introduce two key contributions aimed at improving the accuracy and efficiency of alignment in imitation-based robotic tasks. First, we integrate RANdom SAmple Consensus (RANSAC) for outlier rejection during the alignment phase, effectively mitigating the impact of noisy depth data and spurious feature matches. This improves robustness when transferring demonstrations to real-world applications. Second, we refine the semantic correspondence process by focusing on task-relevant interaction regions, ensuring that the learned representation prioritizes critical object features that influence successful manipulation. We evaluated our approach in a range of real-world manipulation tasks, including picking, insertion, shaking, and pouring, using a robotic system. The experimental results demonstrate an overall improvement of 13.8% in task success rates compared to the baseline, along with faster convergence in the alignment process. These results highlight the effectiveness of our enhancements in improving precision and efficiency for object-object interaction imitation in robotics.

</details>


### [114] [Trajectory Inversion for a Rudderless Dual Vectored-Thrust Flying Wing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10650)
*Weicheng Di,Qiqi Liu,Jinwu Xiang,Daochun Li,Zhan Tu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无舵面双矢量推力飞翼飞行器，提出一种联合优化路径几何与时间分配的运动轨迹生成方法，以解决推力矢量导致平移与俯仰控制强耦合下的动态可行轨迹生成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无气动操纵面的飞翼构型依赖矢量推力实现控制，导致平移与俯仰控制耦合，传统轨迹生成难以保证动力学兼容性，因而需要面向该构型的可行轨迹规划方法。

Method: 将轨迹生成建模为路径几何与时间参数的联合优化问题，并通过轨迹反演获得满足耦合运动方程的状态与控制历史；优化中权衡曲率诱导加速度与气动支撑，以降低推力矢量需求并保留典型机动特征。

Result: 在闭环仿真中跟踪了代表性的筋斗与滚转机动序列，验证了所生成轨迹的动态可行性与跟踪效果。

Conclusion: 该方法为无舵面矢量推力飞行器自主特技机动的规划提供了基础，表明联合优化路径与时间可在耦合约束下生成可跟踪的机动轨迹。

Abstract: A rudderless dual vectored-thrust flying wing combines vertical takeoff and landing with efficient for- ward flight in a compact tailless configuration. Without aerodynamic control surfaces, vectored thrust couples translation and pitch control. Therefore, generating dy- namically compatible trajectories remains an open chal- lenge. To address this challenge, this study proposes a trajectory generation method that jointly optimizes path geometry and timing. Trajectory inversion yields state and control histories that satisfy the coupled equations of motion. The optimization balances curvature-induced acceleration against aerodynamic support, lowering thrust- vectoring demand while retaining the defining maneuver features. Representative looping and rolling sequences are tracked in closed-loop simulations. These results provide a basis for planning autonomous aerobatic maneuvers with rudderless vectored-thrust aircraft.

</details>


### [115] [Which Taxels, Not How Many: Tactile Sensor Placement for Dexterous Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10647)
*Taeyun Kim,Jinsoo Shin,Taehwa Kim,Taeyoon Kim,Jinyoung Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文用统一受控实验检验触觉传感器配置与输入表示的默认设计，发现少量基于接触统计选择的触觉单元即可媲美大规模均匀布置，且输入张量布局不影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作策略的性能依赖训练前的传感器选择、放置和编码表示，但触觉传感中把触觉单元均匀铺满手部已成为未经检验的默认做法；作者希望系统评估这些设计选择。

Method: 在固定教师策略、训练流程和候选触觉单元的前提下，仅改变传感器配置与表示；在三种机器人手上评估掌内旋转和抓取任务，比较均匀布置、基于接触统计的小规模选择、稀有接触位点及不同输入张量布局（含卷积编码器）。

Result: 基于接触统计选出的小规模触觉单元集合可达到远大于它的均匀集合的性能；很少被接触的位点等价于无触觉输入；使用全部候选触觉单元时，其在输入张量中的布局不影响性能，即使采用卷积编码器亦然；因此均匀布置会高估任务所需触觉单元数量。

Conclusion: 作者据此提炼触觉策略设计经验，并提出触觉策略研究应报告的最小设计选择集合，强调应依据任务接触统计选择传感器而非默认均匀铺满。

Abstract: Policies for dexterous manipulation depend on design choices made before training: which sensors enter the observation, where they are placed, and how their readings are arranged for the encoder. These choices are rarely examined. For tactile sensing, simulation studies place taxels uniformly over the hand, and this placement has become a default without being tested. We test this default under a single controlled protocol. The protocol holds the teacher, the training procedure, and the candidate taxels fixed and varies only the sensor configuration and the representation. We evaluate it on in-palm rotation and grasping with three robot hands. A small set of taxels selected from contact statistics matches a far larger uniform set. Taxels at rarely contacted sites are equivalent to no tactile input. With all candidate taxels, their layout in the input tensor does not affect performance, even for a convolutional encoder. Uniform placement therefore overestimates the number of taxels a task requires. We distill these results into lessons for designing tactile policies and into a minimal set of design choices that a tactile policy study should report.

</details>


### [116] [Model-Based Imitation Learning For Monocular Vision-Based Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10635)
*Basant Sharma,Prajyot Jadhav,Saqi Hussain Kalan,Arun Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型的模仿学习方法，将学习到的碰撞模型嵌入轨迹优化，并用人类示范学习风险权衡和优化器参数，实现单目受限视野机器人在杂乱环境中的可靠导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目受限视野相机缺乏深度测量和空间感知，使碰撞检查困难。现有模型规划器泛化较好但计算昂贵且需人工调参；端到端模仿学习灵活但仅隐式编码安全，在新场景中碰撞率高。

Method: 学习一个碰撞模型，将历史图像序列和候选控制序列映射为最坏情况障碍间隙的分布，并在轨迹优化中定义为碰撞风险代价；利用人类示范学习场景相关的碰撞风险与目标进度等任务成本的平衡，同时学习优化器算法参数（如warm start）以加速求解。

Result: 在杂乱环境中，该方法在不同本体的机器人上实现高度可靠导航，并相比近期端到端和模型基基线显著降低碰撞率。

Conclusion: 基于模型的模仿学习可作为模型规划与端到端学习之间的折中，在单目受限视野导航中兼顾泛化性、安全性和灵活性，并减少碰撞。

Abstract: Reliable navigation with a single monocular limited field-of-view camera is challenging because the lack of depth measurements and limited spatial awareness complicate collision checking. Existing approaches to tackle these challenges fall into two categories. Model-based planners can incorporate explicit collision-avoidance guidance which leads to better generalization but are often computationally expensive and require manual tuning of the algorithmic parameters. In contrast, end-to-end approaches trained by imitation learning can be flexible but typically encode safety only implicitly and thus can suffer from high collision rates in novel scenarios. In this paper, we present a middle ground through model-based imitation learning. The model-based part is a learned collision model that can map a sequence of past images and a candidate control sequence to a distribution of worst-case obstacle clearance. This predictive distribution in turn is used to define a collision-risk cost in a trajectory optimization formulation. The imitation component uses human demonstrations to learn scene-dependent balancing of collision risk with other task costs such as goal progress. We also use it to learn the algorithmic parameters (e.g., a warm start) of our optimizer to accelerate the solution process. We show that our approach can demonstrate highly reliable navigation in cluttered environments on robots with different embodiments. We also demonstrate a substantial reduction in collision rate over recent end-to-end and model-based baselines.

</details>


### [117] [Online Geometric Consistency Assessment of LiDAR Measurements via Recursive Bayesian Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10566)
*Mingqiao Han,Qigeng Duan,Xuchen Liu,Jinqiang Cui,Xi Chen,Ben M. Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于递归贝叶斯比较的LiDAR配准异常在线评估方法，通过Normalized Drift Evidence（NDE）在观测同化前检测配准误差，并维护历史体素几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR定位与建图生成的配准点云会因配准误差引入重复表面和结构变形；无独立参考时只能依赖历史几何进行在线评估，但需容忍采样变化，并保证历史仅在评估后才更新。

Method: 将几何评估与历史维护耦合为递归贝叶斯比较：用Normal-Inverse-Wishart模型表示历史体素几何及其不确定性，用弱各向同性参考模型适应当前采样；比较同一当前点在两模型下的边际预测支持得到NDE。评估先于同化：正NDE触发告警并保留先前历史状态，否则当前观测更新历史。

Result: 在4类平台、14条真实序列约73分钟内，告警识别全部9条损坏序列，5条一致序列无误报；4个有事件参考的场景中首次告警在参考时间0.9 s内且无前置告警；跨后端评估响应一致；消融与受控扰动验证了不确定性建模、递归历史、位移和几何支持的作用；1 Hz下平均每更新4.2--6.1 ms；10次UAV试验中告警触发里程计隔离、降落、重定位和任务继续。

Conclusion: NDE可在无独立参考的在线条件下有效检测LiDAR配准退化，并通过评估先于同化的递归历史机制实现低误报、低延迟和跨后端一致性，支持实际导航系统的故障响应与任务恢复。

Abstract: LiDAR-based localization and mapping systems produce registered point clouds that serve as geometric measurements for downstream estimation and navigation. Registration errors introduce duplicated surfaces and structural deformation into these measurements. In the absence of an independent reference, online assessment relies on geometry accumulated from earlier measurements. Such assessment requires tolerance to sampling variation and an accumulation process in which observations enter the history only after evaluation. We therefore formulate a recursive Bayesian comparison that couples geometric assessment with history maintenance. A Normal-Inverse-Wishart model represents historical voxel geometry and its uncertainty, while a weak isotropic reference adapts to current sampling. Comparing the marginal predictive support of the same current points under both models yields Normalized Drift Evidence (NDE). Assessment precedes assimilation: positive NDE triggers an alarm and preserves the preceding historical state; otherwise, the current observations update the history. Across 14 real-world sequences totaling approximately 73 min from four platform types, alarms identified all nine corrupted sequences and were absent from all five consistent sequences. In four representative scenarios with event references, first alarms occurred within 0.9 s of the reference times, with no preceding alarms. Cross-backend evaluation demonstrates consistent responses to reconstructed geometry. Ablation and controlled perturbation results establish the roles of uncertainty modeling, recursive history, displacement, and geometric support. Mean processing time is 4.2--6.1 ms per update at 1 Hz. In ten UAV trials, alarms initiated odometry isolation, landing, relocalization, and mission continuation.

</details>


### [118] [HSGNav: Hypothesis Scene Graphs for Multi-Scale Lifelong Navigation under Object Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10547)
*Kaiyao Liao,Qingfeng Li,Chen Chen,Qing Sun,Ningbo Gu,Xiaoze Wu,Haochen Zhao,Ying Li,Jianwei Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出假设场景图（HSG）与HSGNav，将持久3D家具实体与可修正的表面物体假设分离，以支持真实家庭中多尺度、终身目标导航，并引入FAROS-Bench评估物体中途移动和多尺度寻找。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实家庭中人物会移动物体，辅助机器人需反复定位从大型家具到微小桌面物品的目标；将不同尺度检测统一实例化为3D物体对小型表面物品易失效，且缺乏显式物体状态验证会导致场景变化后保留过期条目。

Method: 核心设计是将持久3D家具实体与可修正的表面物体假设分离，保留视觉证据而无需独立物体几何，形式化为HSG并支持局部修正而不重建稳定实体拓扑。基于HSG的HSGNav按目标尺度选择实体或假设证据进行检索，通过探索获取额外表面观测，并通过验证确认目标存在或使被矛盾假设记录失效。作者还提出FAROS-Bench，用于表面物体中途重定位和多尺度寻找目标。

Result: 实验表明HSGNav在GOAT-Bench上具有竞争力，并在FAROS-Bench上显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 将持久实体与可修正表面物体假设分离能有效支持多尺度终身导航与记忆失效处理；现有终身基准缺乏此类评估，FAROS-Bench补足了中途重定位和多尺度搜索场景。

Abstract: In real homes, assistive robots must repeatedly locate targets from large furniture to tiny tabletop items, as people move objects between household surfaces. Uniformly instantiating detections at different scales as 3D objects can be brittle for small on-surface items; without explicit object-state validation, stale entries may persist after scene changes. Our key insight is to separate persistent 3D furniture entities from revisable surface-object hypotheses, retaining their visual evidence without requiring independent object geometry. We formalize this design as the Hypothesis Scene Graph (HSG), which preserves small-object evidence and supports local revision without rebuilding the stable entity topology. Building on HSG, HSGNav uses this separation for multi-scale lifelong navigation: retrieval selects entity or hypothesis evidence according to target scale, exploration acquires additional surface observations, and verification confirms target presence or invalidates contradicted hypothesis records. Because existing lifelong benchmarks such as GOAT-Bench rarely require mid-episode memory invalidation or multi-scale search, we introduce FAROS-Bench (Find And Re-find Objects across Scales) for mid-episode relocation of surface objects and multi-scale find goals. Experiments show that HSGNav performs competitively on GOAT-Bench while substantially outperforming prior methods on FAROS-Bench.

</details>


### [119] [ReSkill: Selective Self-Improvement for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10510)
*Zheng Sun,Zhihao Li,Zhuo Li,Wenrui Liu,Xin Wang,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReSkill 是一个选择性自我改进框架，面向组合式操作任务，通过定位能力缺口、局部修订并经全任务验证后积累可复用技能，从而提升执行成功率；智能体仅在开发阶段参与，部署策略运行时无需调用智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合式操作任务的自我改进需要决定复用什么、改进哪里以及保留哪些修改；直接大范围修改效率低且风险高，因此需要将修订集中在能力缺口和反复失败环节。

Method: ReSkill 让编码智能体根据指令和技能库构建以对象为中心的任务图，并利用节点级执行记录定位失败。任务级修订阶段组织、交接条件和恢复路径；技能级增强反复失败的感知、运动、操作和验证能力。候选修订需经过全任务评估和配对比较后才采纳，使局部修改逐步积累为经验证的技能和可独立执行的策略。

Result: 在同一开发集上，十个 RoboDojo 任务的平均成功率从初始版本的 30.0% 提升到最终采纳程序的 86.2%；保留程序在记录的鲁棒性和模型选择评估中达到 83.6%。在八个真实世界任务中，ReSkill 在重建仿真试验中成功 72/80，在真实机器人试验中成功 62/80（77.5%）。修订中位数仅改变三个函数，约占程序的 0.5%；后续新增任务中 39% 的任务特定函数直接复用已有实现。

Conclusion: 定位局部缺陷、验证候选修改并积累可复用技能，能够有效改进组合式操作任务的执行表现。

Abstract: Self-improvement on compositional manipulation tasks requires deciding what to reuse, where to improve, and which changes to retain. We present ReSkill, a selective self-improvement framework that concentrates revision on capability gaps. A coding agent constructs an object-centric task graph from an instruction and a skill library, then uses node-level execution records to locate failures. At the task level, it revises stage organization, hand-off conditions, and recovery routes; at the skill level, it strengthens perception, motion, manipulation, and verification capabilities that repeatedly fail. Candidate revisions undergo full-task evaluation and paired comparisons before adoption, allowing local changes to accumulate into validated skills and an independently executable policy. The agent participates only during development; the deployed policy calls the retained skills without invoking the agent at run time. On the same development set, average success across ten RoboDojo tasks rises from 30.0% for the initial versions to 86.2% for the final adopted programs; retained programs achieve 83.6% in recorded robustness and model-selection evaluations. Across eight real-world tasks, ReSkill succeeds in 72 of 80 rebuilt-simulation trials and 62 of 80 real-robot trials (77.5%). The median revision changes only three functions, approximately 0.5% of a program, while 39% of task-specific functions in later-added tasks directly reuse existing implementations. These results show how locating local deficiencies, validating candidate changes, and accumulating reusable skills can improve execution of compositional manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [120] [EmbodiedIntentBench: Benchmarking Intent-Driven Runtime Adaptation for Mobile Robotic Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10498)
*Rajat Bhattacharjya,Changwoon Lee,Hyeonjong Ju,Sing-Yao Wu,Hamidreza Alikhani,Zhuoyi Yang,Ian Harris,Nalini Venkatasubramanian,Nikil Dutt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 EmbodiedIntentBench，一个面向移动机器人任务中意图驱动运行时自适应的基准，用结构化任务契约和因果、资源核算的 UAV 仿真执行来评估感知/计算决策是否真正满足任务目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人感知系统需适应操作员需求、有限资源、连接变化以及由物理运动决定的观测机会；因此运行时策略评估不能只看孤立感知精度或执行效率，而应判断其决策是否满足任务目标。

Method: EmbodiedIntentBench 将任务意图表示为覆盖、时间证据、报告、紧迫性和资源等结构化契约，并将模拟 UAV 工作负载与因果、资源核算的执行耦合；计算决策会影响任务时间、资源使用以及哪些观测能贡献证据。作者在多阶段任务及变化紧迫性、连接性和电池条件下评估固定与自适应感知策略，并进行消融和 Jetson AGX Orin CPU/GPU 回放。

Result: 在 Depot 多阶段研究中，可见目标观测上全库目标召回率 87.5% 的配置仅完成 16 个任务中的 1 个，而较低召回率配置完成 14 个；消融中忽略执行导致的观测损失会提高总体任务成功率并反转两种运行时策略的排序；Jetson AGX Orin CPU 与 GPU 配置回放会改变本地与远程策略的相对成功率；在部分 Jetson GPU 配置回放中，意图驱动自适应在任务成功率不变的情况下将建模任务能耗最多降低 28%。

Conclusion: EmbodiedIntentBench 提供了一个可扩展框架，用于在物理与资源约束下依据任务完成情况评估运行时决策，揭示感知精度之外的执行耦合、观测损失和硬件配置对策略有效性的重要影响。

Abstract: Mobile robotic sensing systems must adapt to operator requirements, limited resources, changing connectivity, and observation opportunities shaped by physical motion. Evaluating runtime strategies therefore requires assessing whether their decisions fulfill mission objectives, beyond isolated perception accuracy or execution efficiency. We present EmbodiedIntentBench, a benchmark for intent-driven runtime adaptation in mobile robotic missions. It represents mission intent through structured contracts for coverage, temporal evidence, reporting, urgency, and resources, and couples simulated unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) workloads with causal, resource-accounted execution. Computation decisions affect mission time, resource use, and which observations can contribute evidence. We evaluate fixed and adaptive perception strategies in multi-phase missions and under changing urgency, connectivity, and battery conditions. In the multi-phase Depot study, a configuration with 87.5% full-bank target recall across visible target observations completes only 1 of 16 missions, whereas a lower-recall configuration completes 14. In an ablation, ignoring execution-induced observation loss increases aggregate mission success and reverses the ordering of two runtime strategies. Replays using Jetson AGX Orin CPU and GPU profiles change the relative success of local and remote strategies. In selected Jetson GPU-profile replays, intent-driven adaptation reduces modeled task energy by up to 28% with unchanged mission success. EmbodiedIntentBench provides an extensible framework for evaluating runtime decisions against mission fulfillment under physical and resource constraints.

</details>


### [121] [Tactile Sensors Are an Effective Substitute for Wrist Cameras in Contact-Rich or Occluded Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10490)
*Aijia Yang,Arsh Tangri,Sergey Zakharov,Krishna Murthy Jatavallabhula*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将两个低成本指尖触觉阵列集成到视触觉ACT策略中，在训练和部署均不使用腕部图像的情况下验证触觉对接触密集型操作的替代作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部相机可提供近距离视觉反馈，但接触密集型任务中的关键接触常被遮挡，甚至腕部相机也无法观测；指尖触觉传感器可直接、可靠地测量接触。

Method: 构建视触觉ACT策略，融合场景视觉、自我中心视觉与两个低成本触觉阵列；在训练和部署中去除腕部图像；在包含放置、插入、遮挡取物和持续接触工具使用的6类真实任务上评估，并在3个接触密集任务中固定示范、策略架构和优化设置，独立改变腕部相机与触觉可用性。

Result: 在3个接触密集任务上，加入腕部相机相较无腕部图像的纯视觉策略提升成功率18.3–28.3个百分点；加入触觉传感提升31.7–60.0个百分点，且在所有3个任务上观察成功率高于带腕部相机的纯视觉策略，其中2个任务在Holm校正检验下具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 在所评估的接触密集型场景中，指尖触觉传感可作为腕部相机的有效替代方案，帮助策略恢复甚至超越腕部相机带来的性能优势。

Abstract: Learned manipulation policies often rely on wrist-mounted cameras for close-range visual feedback that complements external views of the workspace. However, contact-rich manipulation requires establishing and maintaining contacts that can remain hidden even from wrist cameras. Tactile sensors mounted on fingers can reliably and directly measure contact, without needing visual access. In this work, we show that this feedback enables policies to recover the performance advantage of wrist cameras using scene and egocentric vision together with touch. Our approach incorporates two low-cost tactile arrays into a visuotactile ACT policy, without wrist images during training or deployment. We evaluate six real-world task families spanning placement, insertion, occluded retrieval, and sustained-contact tool use. On three contact-rich tasks, we independently vary wrist-camera and tactile availability while keeping the demonstrations, policy architecture, and optimization settings fixed. Adding wrist cameras improves success by 18.3-28.3 percentage points over vision without wrist images. Adding tactile sensing instead improves success by 31.7-60.0 percentage points and yields higher observed success than vision-only policies with wrist cameras on all three tasks, with significant advantages on two under Holm-corrected tests. These results support finger-mounted tactile sensing as an effective alternative to wrist cameras in the evaluated contact-rich settings.

</details>


### [122] [Grip, Trace, Write: Force-Aware Imitation and Reinforcement Learning for High-DoF Robotic Handwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10451)
*Rongqian Chen,Yiding Chen,Jijie Zhou,Tom Z. Jiahao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出力感知仿真环境与学习框架Grip, Trace, Write，将自由持笔书写分解为握持与书写两分支并保持闭环耦合，结合行为克隆初始化与强化学习提升高自由度机器人书写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人书写多将笔刚性固定，把书写简化为轨迹跟踪，忽略了自由持笔时握持稳定性、笔尖运动与接触力之间的耦合。

Method: 构建高自由度力感知仿真环境；采用双分支actor分离笔的保持与臂腕书写并保留闭环耦合；先学习握持控制器，用逆运动学演示经行为克隆初始化书写，再以墨水、力、时间和保持奖励进行强化学习改进。

Result: 相比行为克隆和逆运动学，策略在完成率、精度、力调节和效率上均有提升，并可无需微调泛化到未见过的英文和中文内容。

Conclusion: 结果表明力感知书写可作为灵巧工具使用的测试平台。

Abstract: Most robotic handwriting rigidly mounts the pen and reduces writing to trajectory tracking. A freely held pen instead couples grasp stability, tip motion, and contact force. We present Grip, Trace, Write, a force-aware simulation environment and learning framework for high-DoF handwriting. A two-branch actor separates pen retention from arm-wrist writing while preserving their closed-loop coupling. Training learns a grasp controller, initializes writing from inverse-kinematics demonstrations by behavior cloning, and improves writing through reinforcement learning with rewards on ink, force, time, and retention. Across diverse tasks, the policy improves completion, precision, force regulation, and efficiency over behavior cloning and inverse kinematics, and generalizes without fine-tuning to unseen English and Chinese content. These results establish force-aware handwriting as a testbed for dexterous tool use.

</details>


### [123] [Spatial Representation Distillation and Knowledge Routing for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10443)
*Jungin Park,Chaoran Zhu,Changjae Oh,Kwanghoon Sohn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARK-VLA 提出“保留-专业化-路由”框架，在不依赖推理时外部3D输入或专家模型的情况下，将3D空间理解蒸馏到VLA的视觉分支，并用轻量知识路由器筛选动作相关视觉token，以0.5B语言骨干和约53M可训练参数在多个操作基准和真实场景取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维物理结构理解对视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）至关重要，但现有方法要么推理时需要显式几何输入，要么采用单一视觉通路，可能损害预训练模型继承的视觉-语义知识。

Method: 方法包含两部分：一是空间表示蒸馏，将冻结3D专家的3D理解迁移到LoRA适配的视觉分支，通过对齐注意力响应实现，同时保留原始视觉通路；二是轻量知识路由器，利用由动作损失梯度导出的路由监督，选择性将动作相关视觉token送入语言模型。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN及真实世界操作任务上表现强劲；仅使用0.5B语言骨干和约53M可训练参数。

Conclusion: SPARK-VLA为3D感知VLA学习提供了一种有效且参数高效的方法，无需推理时外部3D输入或专家模型，并兼顾空间适应与预训练视觉-语义知识保留。

Abstract: Understanding three-dimensional physical structure has become essential for vision-language-action models (VLAs). However, existing approaches either rely on explicit geometric inputs at inference time or adopt a single visual pathway, potentially compromising the visual-semantic knowledge inherited from pre-trained models. We present SPARK-VLA, Spatial Representation distillation and Knowledge routing for VLAs, a preserve-specialize-route framework for spatially adapting VLAs without requiring external 3D inputs or experts at inference. SPARK-VLA consists of two key components: (1) spatial representation distillation, which transfers 3D understanding from a frozen 3D expert to a LoRA-adapted visual branch by aligning attention responses while preserving the original visual pathway, and (2) a lightweight knowledge router, which selectively forwards action-relevant visual tokens to the language model using routing supervision derived from action-loss gradients. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN, and real-world manipulation, SPARK-VLA achieves strong performance with only a 0.5B language backbone and approximately 53M trainable parameters, providing an effective and parameter-efficient approach to 3D-aware VLA learning. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [124] [Multi-Objective Adaptation After Deployment: A Position on World Models for Remote Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10435)
*Jamie Santos,Kagan Tumer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文主张用世界模型支持远程非结构化环境中自主机器人团队在部署后的多目标策略重评估与适应，并给出研究优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队部署后可能遇到环境、硬件和团队组成变化，使原策略失效；任务目标变化可从现有Pareto集中选择方案，但部署条件变化会重排Pareto集，使原被支配策略变得有竞争力或需新策略。在新情境中评估策略且尽量降低硬件风险仍是开放问题。

Method: 作者提出研究世界模型：在当前条件更新估计下预测候选策略结果，从而无需物理执行每个候选即可重评估现有策略；并提出三项研究重点：基于模型的策略重评估、预测性能与实际性能的验证、将共享信息纳入世界模型以支持团队协同适应。

Result: 摘要未报告实验结果，属于立场/研究议程型工作；核心结果是论证世界模型在该领域虽基本未经测试但值得研究，并明确后续研究优先级。

Conclusion: 世界模型有望用于部署后多目标适应，但前提是预测性能变化能反映部署系统实际表现；需重点验证预测与现实一致性，并利用共享信息实现协调的团队适应。

Abstract: Autonomous robot teams deployed in remote, unstructured environments may face changes in the environment, hardware, and team composition that degrade previously effective policies. Shifting mission objectives can be handled by selecting a different solution from an existing Pareto set, but shifting deployment conditions can reorder the set itself, making previously dominated policies competitive or new policies necessary. However, evaluating these policies in new contexts while minimizing risk to the hardware remains an open problem. We argue that, although largely untested in this domain, world models merit investigation for post-deployment multi-objective adaptation. By predicting policy outcomes under updated estimates of current conditions, world models could enable teams to re-evaluate existing policies without physically executing each candidate, provided predicted performance changes reflect those realized on deployed systems. We identify research priorities for model-based policy re-evaluation, validation of predicted against realized performance, and incorporating shared information into world models to support coordinated team adaptation.

</details>


### [125] [Deploying Physically and Epistemically Compliant Agents by Distilling Belief-Space Task and Motion Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10430)
*Phillip J.K. Christoffersen,Rachel Ma,Patrick Barragán,Dylan Hadfield-Menell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将信念空间规划与策略蒸馏结合：离线规划生成轨迹数据集，再蒸馏为直接基于原始观测行动的策略，以在不确定性下遵循指定的信念依赖规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习理论上可通过奖励设计让智能体遵循规则，但实践中难以完美优化奖励，导致行为无效或不合规；信念空间规划器虽能按开发者规范产生行为，却需要手工编写在线规划器和信念状态估计器，工程复杂度高。

Method: 作者离线规划生成样本轨迹并蒸馏为策略，并针对基于历史的策略提出两项改进：Dual Averaging（结合每时间步预测损失与任务变化上的平均损失）和 ρ-Optimal Reinforcement（当策略自身成功 rollout 接近最优时进行反馈强化）。作者还给出静态搜索在无噪声观测模型下完备的充分条件，并证明连续信念静态搜索中最大似然观测规划蕴含该条件。

Result: 作者构建了需要获取信息才能成功的 Scavenger 域，并在视觉信息和自然语言命令两种版本中评估。蒸馏策略成功率超过 90%，而 RL 至多 5%；对未见且故意模糊命令的迁移成功率超过 60%，对大于训练规模的楼层平面图迁移成功率超过 75%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，离线信念空间规划加策略蒸馏能避免 RL 奖励优化失败和在线规划器工程负担，使智能体在不确定性下更可靠地遵循规范，并具备一定泛化能力。

Abstract: In this work, we train agents to act under uncertainty according to specified, belief-dependent rules. Deep reinforcement learning (RL) can in theory train agents to follow rules if encoded into well-designed rewards, but in practice, RL fails to perfectly optimize these rewards, leading to ineffective or noncompliant agent behavior. Belief-space planners by construction produce behavior in line with developer specification, but require hand-programming online planners and belief-state estimators, adding significant engineering complexity at scale. We combine the two: we plan offline to generate a dataset of sample trajectories and then distill the plans into a policy that acts on raw observations. We propose two changes to planner distillation that address problems specific to history-based policies: Dual Averaging, a loss that combines per-timestep predictive loss and average loss on task changes, and $\rho$-Optimal Reinforcement, a feedback mechanism that reinforces the policy's own successful rollouts when these are near-optimal. We provide a sufficient condition under which static search problems with noiseless observation models is complete, and show that maximum-likelihood-observation planning implies this condition in static search with continuous beliefs. We create a challenging new domain, Scavenger, where an agent needs to gain information in order to succeed. We evaluate our distilled policy in two versions of Scavenger, one where information is provided visually and one where it is provided as a command in natural language. In either case, the learned policy attains a success rate above 90% in Scavenger, compared with at most 5% for RL. We also find that distilled planners transfer with greater than 60% success to following unseen and intentionally vague commands and greater than 75% success to following floorplans larger than those seen in training.

</details>


### [126] [SimDuoReal: Object-Centric Bimanual Policy for Emergent Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10410)
*Qiyang Yan,Yulin Liu,Akash Sharma,Haozhi Qi,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以物体为中心的共享奖励学习框架，使复杂双臂灵巧操作行为能够涌现，并蒸馏为可执行多样任务的单一策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧操作需要手臂与手部协调，但手动指定协调策略困难且往往任务特定；作者探索复杂双臂灵巧性是否可从简单的物体中心学习目标中涌现。

Method: 在共享的物体中心奖励下训练强化学习策略，通过改变训练环境而非为每种行为单独调奖励；策略自发发现预抓取操作、抓取与重抓、手内调整和交接等行为。随后在过渡状态上精炼专家，并使用DAgger和强化学习将其蒸馏为单一策略，以组合这些行为完成多样双臂任务。

Result: 在真实硬件上，同一策略在四个任务、未见物体且无任务特定微调的情况下，80次试验成功61次（76.3%）；在模拟条码扫描任务中达到85.0%成功率，而无交接的两个单臂策略为60.4%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该物体中心目标为学习通用双臂灵巧性提供了简单且可扩展的接口，将负担从手工设计机器人协调转向指定物体应发生什么。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous manipulation requires coordinated arm and hand control, yet specifying such coordination manually is challenging and often task-specific. In this work, we explore whether complex bimanual dexterity can emerge from a simple object-centric learning objective. We train reinforcement learning policies under a shared object-centric reward, varying their training environments rather than tuning separate rewards for each behavior. We find that the policies discover pre-grasp manipulation, grasping and regrasping, in-hand adjustment and handover to reach object-pose goals without hand-designed motion sequences. We then refine the experts on transition states and use DAgger and reinforcement learning to distill them into a single policy that composes these behaviors for diverse bimanual tasks. On real hardware, the same policy succeeds in 61 of 80 trials (76.3\%) across four tasks with unseen objects and no task-specific fine-tuning. In simulated barcode scanning, our policy achieves 85.0\% success, compared with 60.4\% for two single-arm policies without handover. These results suggest that this objective provides a simple and scalable interface for learning general bimanual dexterity, shifting the burden from manually designing robot coordination to specifying what should happen to the object.

</details>


### [127] [Detecting and Explaining Robot Policy Failures from Sparse Internal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10400)
*Riad Ahmed,Sujosh Nag,Moniruzzaman Akash,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIND 通过稀疏自编码器分解视觉运动策略的内部激活，将特征与机器人位置、运动和环境关联，并利用成功/失败执行中的特征模式来检测和解释失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略虽能完成多种操作任务，但失败原因难以理解；仅观察机器人行为无法揭示策略内部出了什么问题。

Method: MIND 使用稀疏自编码器将策略内部激活拆解为可单独研究的特征，并通过关联机器人位置、运动与周围环境，以及引导特征激活来赋予其语义；随后从成功和失败执行中学习特征走向失败时的行为模式，在执行中标记偏离并用贡献最大的有意义特征描述问题。

Result: 仿真和实体机器人实验表明，这些内部特征的变化有助于检测和解释失败。

Conclusion: MIND 将同一组特征同时用于失败检测与诊断，从而把失败信号与策略内部可理解的变化联系起来。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies can handle many robot manipulation tasks, but understanding their failures remains difficult. Unfamiliar conditions and small action errors can lead to failure, yet watching the robot alone tells us little about what went wrong inside the policy. We introduce MIND (Mechanistic Interpretability for Neural Diagnosis), a method that looks inside a trained policy to detect failures during execution and identify the internal concepts involved. MIND uses a sparse autoencoder to break down the policy's internal activations into features that can be studied individually. We give meaning to these features by linking their activity to the robot's position, movement, and surroundings, and by steering their activations to examine how they affect the robot's actions. From successful and failed executions, we learn how the features behave on the way to a failure. During execution, MIND flags deviations from these patterns and uses the meaningful features contributing most to the change to describe the problem. Experiments in simulation and on a physical robot show that changes in these internal features help detect and explain failures. By using the same features for detection and diagnosis, MIND connects failure signals to understandable changes inside the policy.

</details>


### [128] [CPNav: Calibrated Vision-Language Navigation for Object-Goal Search in Unseen Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10366)
*Qizhao Chen,Yuanhong Zeng,Shoh Nishino,Anushri Dixit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CPNav 将碰撞感知规划与基于共形预测校准的 VLM 动作评分结合，用于无地图目标物体导航，并在仿真与真实机器人上提升成功率与路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中让机器人找到目标物体仍是具身智能的核心难题；现有 VLM 方法虽具备语义推理能力，但动作决策常未校准且过度自信。

Method: CPNav 从 RGB-D 输入构建可导航地图并生成无碰撞运动基元，查询两个 VLM 分别进行目标检测与动作评分；利用带回溯的树状探索过程获得 oracle 动作，离线通过共形预测校准 VLM 分数，提供用户指定风险水平下的有限样本保证。在线阶段用校准分数指导动作选择，并用树状探索历史记录备选动作以支持回溯；该 CP 层与模型无关，可封装任意 VLM 策略。

Result: 在 AI Habitat 仿真中，相较未校准基线，CPNav 在 HM3D v0.2 上 SR 与 SPL 相对提升 6.2% 和 11.4%，在 MP3D 上提升 12.4% 和 8.8%；在修改版 Hiwonder MentorPi 真实机器人部署中，SR 与 SPL 分别提升 24.9% 和 15.2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将统计校准引入 VLM 动作评分并结合碰撞感知规划与可回溯探索，可有效提升无地图目标物体导航的可靠性与实际部署性能。

Abstract: Directing a robot to locate a target object in unseen environments remains a central challenge in embodied AI. Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based methods provide rich semantic reasoning but often produce uncalibrated and overconfident action decisions. We introduce CPNav, a navigation framework that combines collision-aware planning with statistically calibrated VLM action scoring for map-free object-goal navigation. From RGB-D input, CPNav constructs a navigability map, generates collision-free motion primitives, and queries two VLMs for goal detection and action scoring. The resulting scores are calibrated offline via conformal prediction (CP) using oracle actions obtained through a tree-structured exploration procedure with backtracking, providing finite-sample guarantees at a user-specified risk level. The CP layer is model-agnostic and can wrap any VLM policy. Online, calibrated VLM scores guide action selection, while a tree-structured exploration history records alternative actions and allows backtracking. We evaluate CPNav in both simulation and real-world hardware settings. In AI Habitat simulation, compared with the uncalibrated baseline method, CPNav achieves relative SR and SPL improvements of 6.2% and 11.4% on HM3D v0.2, and 12.4% and 8.8% on MP3D, respectively. In real-world deployment on a modified Hiwonder MentorPi robot, the corresponding improvements in SR and SPL are 24.9% and 15.2%, respectively. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [129] [LSRP*: Scalable and Anytime Planning for Multi-Agent Path Finding with Asynchronous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10363)
*Dachuan Liu,Shuai Zhou,Shizhe Zhao,Zhongqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出随时规划算法 LSRP*，用于解决松散同步多智能体路径规划中 LSRP 解质量差的问题，并在初始解后持续优化直至预算耗尽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MAPF 算法多假设所有智能体动作同步开始且每个动作耗时相同，这限制实际应用；LSRP 虽能处理松散同步并支持多智能体，但存在无界次优性，常产生较差解。

Method: 作者设计 LSRP*，一种 anytime 规划器，在获得初始解后继续改进解质量直到运行时间预算耗尽；同时分析其性质，并在多种地图上与多个基线对比，规模达 1000 个智能体。

Result: 实验表明，LSRP* 比 LSRP 可多处理最多 25% 的智能体，并可将 LSRP 找到的解成本最多降低 40%。

Conclusion: LSRP* 通过 anytime 优化机制缓解了 LSRP 解质量差的问题，在松散同步 MAPF 场景下提升了可扩展性和解质量。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) seeks collision-free paths for multiple agents from their respective starting locations to their respective goal locations while minimizing path costs. Although many MAPF algorithms were developed, most of them rely on a common assumption on synchronized actions, where the actions of all agents start at the same time and always take a time unit. This assumption may limit the use of MAPF planners in practice. To get rid of this assumption, recently, an algorithm called Loosely Synchronized Rule-based Planning (LSRP) was proposed, which can find sub-optimal solutions for many agents. However, LSRP often finds poor quality solutions due to its unbounded sub-optimality. This paper develops a new anytime planner called LSRP* that can keep improving solution quality after the initial solution is obtained until the runtime budget depletes. We analyze the properties of LSRP* and test it against several baselines with up to 1000 agents on various maps. LSRP* can handle up to 25% more agents than LSRP and can reduce up to 40% of the solution cost found by LSRP.

</details>


### [130] [GUARD: Anomaly-Informed Q-Steering for Generative Robot Policies in Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10343)
*Aryaman Gupta,Xiaoyue Wu,Rana Soltani Zarrin,Mark Van der Merwe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GUARD 提出一种基于异常感知的 Q 引导框架，在不修改预训练生成式机器人策略的情况下，利用训练分布学习到的稠密异常分数与稀疏任务奖励共同引导去噪过程，从而提升闭环执行鲁棒性并实现 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散和流匹配的生成式机器人策略虽能建模复杂多模态行为，但在闭环执行中易受协变量偏移影响；现有方法依赖额外示范、策略微调或测试时引导，往往需要人工干预、工程化奖励或其他任务特定信号。

Method: GUARD 将稀疏任务奖励与从策略训练数据中学习的稠密异常分数结合，并通过学习到的 Q 函数传播二者；利用其梯度引导去噪过程，使动作偏向提高任务回报，同时避免轨迹进入训练支持不足的区域，且不直接修改预训练策略。

Result: 在四个 Adroit 灵巧操作任务上，GUARD 在标称和扰动动力学下分别达到 96.0% 和 80.0% 的平均成功率，比最强基线高 21.0 和 20.0 个百分点，并保持最低异常分数；在手中螺丝刀旋转和重定向任务上实现直接 sim-to-real 迁移，成功率分别提升 40 和 25 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明异常信号不仅可用于失败监测，还可作为稀疏任务奖励的补充监督，用于实现鲁棒的策略引导。

Abstract: Generative robot policies based on diffusion and flow matching can model complex, multimodal behaviors, but remain susceptible to covariate shift during closed-loop execution. Existing approaches mitigate such failures through additional demonstrations, policy fine-tuning, or test-time guidance, but often require human intervention, engineered rewards, or other task-specific signals. We introduce GUARD, an anomaly-informed Q-steering framework that leverages the policy's training distribution to construct a dense, failure-anticipatory steering signal. GUARD pairs sparse task rewards with dense anomaly scores learned from policy training data and propagates both through a learned Q-function. Its gradients then steer the denoising process toward actions that improve task return while preventing trajectories from entering poorly supported regions, without directly modifying the pretrained policy. Across four Adroit dexterous manipulation tasks, GUARD achieves average success rates of $96.0\%$ and $80.0\%$ under nominal and perturbed dynamics, outperforming the strongest baseline by $21.0$ and $20.0$ percentage points while maintaining the lowest anomaly score. GUARD further demonstrates direct sim-to-real transfer on in-hand screwdriver rotation and reorientation tasks, achieving $40$ and $25$ percentage-point gains in success rate. These results demonstrate that anomaly signals can serve not only for failure monitoring but also to complement sparse task rewards as effective supervision for robust policy steering.

</details>


### [131] [Mobility Information Capacity of Multi-Robot Systems: Safety-Aware Maneuver Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10298)
*Weijie Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出移动性信息容量（MIC）框架，用互信息量化多机器人系统在噪声观测下可观测的机动自由度，并给出线性高斯反馈动力学下的容量基准、约束映射与有限码本构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数量、工作空间占用率和任务吞吐量无法刻画多机器人舰队能多大幅度改变运动，也无法说明这些运动变化在噪声轨迹观测下能被多可靠地识别；需要一种联合考虑机动自由度与可观测性的度量。

Method: 将移动性信息定义为联合机动序列与其含噪轨迹观测之间的互信息，并在机动资源约束下最大化该互信息以定义MIC。针对带反馈动力学的线性高斯机动-观测模型，刻画有限时域信息基准并推导长时域平稳谱极限，纳入异构移动增益、有色不确定性和时间耦合机动代价。将工作空间边界、机器人间分离和执行器机会约束映射为耦合机动协方差限制，给出协方差优化可简化为移动模式间资源分配的条件。通过个体风险筛选和噪声白化轨迹分离构造有限联合机动码本，并建立序列恢复误差、可达信息与容量基准之间的界。

Result: 在空中机器人仿真中评估了谱收敛、安全感知资源分配、有限码本识别和舰队规模缩放；理论部分给出了有限时域基准、长时域谱极限、约束下的协方差优化条件以及有限码本的性能界。

Conclusion: MIC框架补充了占用率和任务吞吐量等指标，可量化移动系统所支持的最大可观测机动自由度，从而刻画多机器人在共享工作空间中协调运动的容量与可辨识性。

Abstract: Multiple robots operating in shared workspaces must adjust their speed, direction, and trajectories to coordinate with one another and respond to changing tasks and disturbances. The available maneuver freedom depends on the dynamics, uncertainty, and safety margins, even for a fixed fleet size and workspace. However, robot count, workspace occupancy, and task throughput do not quantify how much the fleet can vary its motion or how reliably these variations can be observed. We develop a mobility information capacity (MIC) framework that jointly characterizes these two aspects. Mobility information is defined as the mutual information between a joint maneuver sequence and its noisy trajectory observation, and MIC maximizes this quantity under maneuver-resource constraints. For a linear Gaussian maneuver-to-observation model with feedback dynamics, we characterize the finite-horizon information benchmark and derive its stationary long-horizon spectral limit, accounting for heterogeneous mobility gains, colored uncertainty, and temporally coupled maneuver costs. We further map workspace-boundary, inter-robot separation, and actuator chance constraints into coupled maneuver-covariance restrictions and derive conditions under which covariance optimization reduces to resource allocation across mobility modes. Finite joint maneuver codebooks are then constructed through individual risk screening and noise-whitened trajectory separation, with bounds linking sequence recovery error and achieved information to the capacity benchmarks. An aerial-robot simulation study evaluates spectral convergence, safety-aware allocation, finite-codebook identification, and fleet-size scaling. The resulting framework complements occupancy and task-throughput measures by quantifying the maximum observable maneuver freedom supported by a mobility system.

</details>


### [132] [Who Taught the Robot That? Removing One User's Corrections from a Robot Policy That Learns Online](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10280)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人部署后多人轮流纠正时，能否“忘记”某个人取决于纠正方式：接管控制时按原顺序重训剩余会话可近似目标机器人，删除数据后单次重训不行；仅动作微调时无方法能恢复该人从未参与时的机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习的机器人需要支持用户事后撤回其数据与影响，目标是得到“该人从未存在过”的机器人，因此要研究不同纠正方式如何影响可移除性。

Method: 在两个仿真任务和脚本用户中精确计算移除目标，比较接管控制与动作微调两类纠正，并评估删除数据重训、按原顺序重训剩余会话、继续训练等方法。

Result: 接管控制下，有序训练日志按原顺序重训剩余会话可接近目标，误差仅受训练噪声限制；删除数据并单次重训会因丢失顺序而失败，即使他人纠正不依赖被移除者。动作微调下，他人纠正会携带被移除者的影响，测试方法均无法恢复目标；继续训练可去掉大部分习惯，但训练缓冲中数据维持的影响仍在。日志能识别明显有害用户，但按影响力排序不如直接计数纠正。

Conclusion: 可遗忘性由纠正接口和训练顺序决定；应保留有序训练日志并按序重训，仅删数据或继续训练不足；微调式纠正会使个人影响难以完全移除。

Abstract: A robot that keeps learning after deployment is corrected by several people in turn, and one of them may later ask to be removed. We show that whether the robot can forget that person depends on how people correct it. The goal of removal is the robot that would exist if the person had never been there, which we compute exactly in two simulated tasks with scripted users. When users correct the robot by taking over control, an ordered log of training updates is enough: retraining on the remaining sessions in their original order gives a robot as close to the goal as training noise allows. Deleting the person's data and retraining in one pass does not, even when the other users' corrections do not depend on the removed person, because it loses the order of training. When users only nudge the robot's action, the other users' corrections carry the removed person's influence, and no method we tested recovers the goal robot. Continuing to train without the person removes most of their habits, but not what their data in the training buffer keep alive. The log can identify one clearly harmful user, but not rank users by influence better than counting their corrections.

</details>


### [133] [Look Only Where You Plan for Real-Time End-to-End Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10264)
*Haohua Que,Tianle Zhu,Qianyi Wu,Zhipeng Bao,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOWP是一种“只在规划处看”的紧凑驾驶规划器：用两组各16个可学习查询经已知标定读取相机特征，不构建稠密BEV，也不做对象或地图监督，并在多个基准上取得有竞争力的规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强规划器中稠密鸟瞰图（BEV）构建约占60%参数，计算与监督成本高；作者希望去除稠密BEV及对象/地图监督，仅提取规划所需信息。

Method: 两组各16个可学习查询通过已知相机标定读取图像：一组在投影的3D锚点处采样图像特征，另一组对带视射线标签的特征进行注意力；不构建稠密BEV，不监督对象或地图。

Result: nuScenes上平均L2由0.302降至0.296 m，参数为稠密对照的53%，同GPU速度为其1.7倍，锚点读取器在转弯上优于全局读取器；NAVSIM上无需重训，用自身轨迹与词表打分达85.9 PDMS；Bench2Drive闭环控制配置在218条路线上得分65.5，存在刹车导致停驶的失败，且释放规则的总体收益不确定；实测延迟与硬件迁移估计需区分，后者不能建立同硬件排名。

Conclusion: LOWP表明无需稠密BEV和对象/地图监督也能以紧凑表示实现有竞争力规划，但闭环停驶恢复策略及跨硬件延迟比较仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Dense bird's-eye-view (BEV) construction accounts for 60% of the parameters in a strong recent driving planner. This paper presents LOWP, a planner that looks only where it plans: two sets of sixteen learnable queries read the cameras through the known calibration, one by sampling image features at projected 3D anchors and one by attending to features labeled with their viewing rays, so no dense BEV is built and no object or map is supervised. Evaluations on open-loop nuScenes, pseudo-simulated NAVSIM and closed-loop Bench2Drive show competitive planning with a compact representation. On nuScenes, average L2 changes from 0.302 to 0.296 m with 53% of the dense counterpart's parameters and 1.7 times its speed on the same GPU; the anchor-based reader performs better on turns than the global reader. On NAVSIM, scoring the planner's own trajectories alongside a vocabulary gives 85.9 PDMS without retraining. The closed-loop control configuration scores 65.5 on 218 routes, with a brake-induced standstill failure whose release rule has an inconclusive aggregate benefit. We distinguish measured latency from hardware-transferred estimates, which do not establish a common-hardware ranking.

</details>


### [134] [OfficeTwin: Realizing 3D Reconstruction of Redundant Scenes through Partial Viewpoint Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10241)
*Quanyun Wu,Kyle Gao,Daisy Li,Kshitij Goyal,Dayou Mao,David A. Clausi,Jonathan Li,Yuhao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OfficeTwin 将机器人采集的不完整单目观测转换为可用于仿真的数字孪生：通过施加度量与结构约束，并将重复工位配准到共享规范坐标系，恢复被遮挡几何并输出水密、带度量的实体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式三维重建速度快且对弱纹理/重复表面鲁棒，但原始点云缺乏物体身份、物理属性和水密体积，难以用于机器人学习；机器人视角受限导致物体仅部分可见，办公室重复且弱纹理使不同工位难以区分，现有图像到三维生成方法在欠观测下易幻觉几何并混淆重复实例。

Method: 提出 OfficeTwin 机器人原生框架：先施加度量与结构约束，再利用办公室结构冗余，将重复 cubicles 配准到共享 canonical frame，通过互补观测恢复单视角中被遮挡的几何，避免生成式幻觉。

Result: 在真实办公室扫描中，OfficeTwin 将每个 cubicle 恢复为独立水密、带度量的实体，优于无法生成仿真可用资产的前馈重建与生成式基线。

Conclusion: 利用机器人原生采集与办公室结构先验/重复性配准，可在不完整观测下可靠构建仿真就绪的数字孪生。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction turns an uncalibrated monocular stream into a dense point cloud in a single inference pass, orders of magnitude faster than classical structure-from-motion (e.g., COLMAP), and reliable on the weakly textured, repetitive surfaces where keypoint matching fails outright. However, raw point clouds lack the object identity, physical properties, and watertight volume required for robot learning. This gap widens under robot-native capture. A camera mounted on a robot constrained to a few degrees of freedom sees each object only partially, and the repetitive, weakly textured structure of an office makes distinct workstations nearly indistinguishable, leaving redundant and partial observations that downstream reconstruction cannot resolve. Existing image-to-3D generation methods struggle with these conditions, hallucinating geometry under incomplete views and confusing repeated instances. We present OfficeTwin, a robot-native framework that converts these incomplete observations into simulation-ready digital twins. OfficeTwin first imposes metric and structural constraints, then exploits the office's structural redundancy by registering repeated cubicles into a shared canonical frame, where complementary observations recover geometry occluded in any single view without hallucination. On a real office scan, OfficeTwin recovers every cubicle as an individually watertight, metric solid, outperforming feed-forward reconstruction and generative baselines that fail to produce simulation-ready assets.

</details>


### [135] [DASH-SLAM: Calibration-Free Metric Monocular SLAM via Depth Scale Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10239)
*Otavio Ribas,Jacopo Panerati,Pierre-Yves Lajoie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DASH-SLAM 将预训练 Depth Anything 3 的度量深度预测集成到共享关键帧位姿图中，无需任务特定微调，通过共享锚点图像深度比估计并累积尺度校正，实现单目 SLAM 的一致尺度与跨重建数据关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何基础模型可从单目图像估计稠密几何和相机参数，但可靠 SLAM 还需要连续重建间的一致尺度与准确数据关联；现有方法在尺度一致性和感知退化环境下跟踪鲁棒性不足。

Method: DASH-SLAM 从预训练 Depth Anything 3 获取度量深度，构建共享关键帧位姿图；在共享锚点图像处由深度比导出尺度校正，并跨重建累积，使用阻尼因子控制其影响；校正用于重缩放里程计平移、回环约束和地图几何，且无需任务特定微调。

Result: 在 TUM、Replica 和 7-Scenes 上优于现有最先进方法；采用刚性与相似变换对齐分别评估度量尺度保真度和至尺度精度。在 Unified Autonomy Stack 现场数据集中隧道、雾和冰湖等四个感知退化挑战序列上，优于基础模型 SLAM 最先进方法，并在经典 SLAM 失败的场景中保持跟踪。

Conclusion: DASH-SLAM 表明无需微调的基础模型度量深度结合共享关键帧尺度校正，可提升单目 SLAM 的尺度一致性、精度与感知退化环境鲁棒性。

Abstract: Geometric foundation models estimate dense geometry and camera parameters from monocular images, but reliable SLAM also requires consistent scale and accurate data association across successive reconstructions. We present DASH-SLAM, a monocular SLAM system that integrates metric depth predictions from pretrained Depth Anything 3 into a shared-keyframe pose graph without task-specific fine-tuning. DASH-SLAM derives scale corrections from depth ratios at shared anchor images and accumulates them across reconstructions, with a damping factor controlling their influence. These corrections rescale odometry translations, loop constraints, and map geometry. Experiments on TUM, Replica, and 7-Scenes show that DASH-SLAM outperforms state-of-the-art methods. We evaluate performance using both rigid and similarity alignment to distinguish metric-scale fidelity from accuracy up to scale. We further evaluate DASH-SLAM on four challenging sequences in perceptually-degraded environments from the Unified Autonomy Stack field dataset, covering tunnels, fog, and a frozen lake. On these sequences, DASH-SLAM outperforms the foundation-model SLAM state-of-the-art and maintains tracking where classical SLAM methods fail.

</details>


### [136] [Agibot: An Open-Source Modular Swerve-Drive Robot Platform for Agricultural Robotics Research and Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10235)
*Moeen Ul Islam,Bishal Adhikari,Kori Edwards,Lorien Harvey,J. Alex Thomasson,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了开源、模块化、低成本农业机器人平台Agibot，用于农业自主性研究与实验，并支持机器人教育。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业劳动力短缺和自动化需求增长，但农业机器人研究受限于缺乏低成本、灵活且易获取的研究平台。

Method: Agibot集成四轮独立转向驱动、可更换计算单元、分布式嵌入式控制、多模态传感和可配置操作机构，构建统一的“感知-规划-执行-验证”框架，支持感知、控制、导航、操作和自主任务验证；通过不同光照与土壤条件下的行作物导航和操作任务进行评估。

Result: 实验实现了可靠的车道跟踪、行间自主导航、目标识别、播种、植物操作及任务成功的闭环验证；控制器调参和集成试验验证了平台的研究能力，模块化架构支持自主性组件的独立测试、修改和验证。

Conclusion: Agibot以低成本、模块化、开源可获取、可配置操作和端到端自主性，为农业机器人研究、技术开发和劳动力培训提供了实用平台。

Abstract: Agricultural production faces persistent labor shortages and increasing demand for automation, yet progress in agricultural robotics remains constrained by the limited availability of affordable, flexible, and accessible research platforms. This paper presents Agibot, an open-source, modular, low-cost agricultural robotics platform designed for autonomy research and experimentation, with potential applications in hands-on robotics education. The platform integrates four-wheel independent swerve drive, swappable computing, distributed embedded control, multimodal sensing, and configurable manipulation within a unified sense-plan-act-verify framework supporting perception, control, navigation, manipulation, and autonomous task verification. The platform was evaluated through representative row-crop navigation and manipulation tasks under varying lighting and soil conditions. Experiments demonstrated reliable lane following, autonomous row-to-row navigation, target identification, seed dispensing, plant manipulation, and closed-loop verification of task success. Its modular architecture supports independent testing, modification, and validation of autonomy components for rapid prototyping and experimentation. These proof-of-concept tasks, controller-tuning studies, and integrated trials demonstrate the platform's research capabilities, while its accessible and reconfigurable design supports its use for hands-on robotics education. By combining affordability, modularity, open-source accessibility, configurable manipulation, and end-to-end sense-plan-act-verify autonomy, Agibot provides a practical platform for agricultural robotics research, technology development, and workforce training.

</details>


### [137] [Capturability-Aware Footstep Planning in Cluttered Environments: A Recursively Feasible Sequential MPC on Axis-Aligned Safe Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10169)
*Athanasios Kyriakopoulos,Shubham S. Kumbhar,Kunal S. Narkhede,Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Ioannis Poulakakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种感知可捕获性的序列MPC框架，用于LIP模型的人形机器人脚步规划，在避障同时保证运动稳定性，并在受阻时自适应切换原地踏步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人脚步规划需同时保证避障与运动稳定性（可捕获性），并需应对规划时可通过、执行时却被阻塞等受限场景。

Method: 基于LIP模型构建序列MPC；用相交矩形序列构造安全走廊，将避障与可捕获性约束结合，并为规划层提供递归可行性保证；当障碍阻塞前进时自动切换为原地踏步。

Result: 性能在很大程度上与预测时域无关，可在较低计算代价下获得高成功率；在高保真仿真中使用人形机器人Digit验证。

Conclusion: 该方法为杂乱环境中可捕获的人形机器人脚步规划提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: A fundamental aspect of humanoid footstep planning is to jointly guarantee obstacle avoidance and locomotion stability. This paper presents a capturability-aware sequential Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework for Linear Inverted Pendulum (LIP)-based humanoid footstep planning in the presence of obstacles. The proposed method combines obstacle avoidance with capturability constraints through safe corridors constructed as sequences of intersecting rectangles, yielding a sequential MPC framework with recursive feasibility guarantees for the LIP-based planning layer. The planner autonomously adapts to constrained scenarios, switching to in-place walking when obstructions---such as doors assumed to be open during planning but unexpectedly found to be closed during execution---block forward motion. Performance is largely independent of the prediction horizon, allowing high success rates while keeping the computational cost low. The approach is validated in high-fidelity simulations with the humanoid robot Digit, demonstrating a practical solution for capturable humanoid footstep planning in cluttered environments.

</details>


### [138] [BATCH: Behavior Alignment for Cross-Embodiment Learning from Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10148)
*Zeyi Liu,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BATCH 是一个跨具身学习框架，通过自监督时序表示与循环一致性对齐人类和机器人行为，并在自回归视频-动作扩散模型中利用对齐关系提升机器人策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示数据多样且可扩展，但由于具身形态、视角、动作空间和时序结构差异，直接用于机器人学习仍很困难；核心动机是显式对齐人类与机器人行为以促进有效迁移。

Method: BATCH 使用循环一致性目标学习自监督时序表示，在无需人工标注的情况下建立跨具身对应关系；随后在自回归视频-动作扩散模型中引入两个互补训练目标：行为对齐协同训练和人类到机器人风格迁移。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务上，与未对齐数据训练和先前跨具身基线相比，BATCH 提升了对未见任务配置的泛化能力，并且风格迁移目标带来额外增益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式行为对齐能够更有效地将人类演示迁移到机器人策略，提升跨具身学习的泛化性能。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide a scalable source of diverse manipulation data, but leveraging them for robot learning remains challenging due to differences in embodiment, viewpoint, action spaces, and temporal structure. We introduce BATCH, a cross-embodiment learning framework that explicitly aligns human and robot behaviors. BATCH learns self-supervised temporal representations with a cycle-consistency objective to establish cross-embodiment correspondences without manual annotation. We further introduce two complementary training objectives that leverage these correspondences within an autoregressive videoaction diffusion model: behavior-aligned co-training and human-to-robot style transfer. On real-world manipulation tasks, BATCH improves generalization to unseen task configurations compared with training on unaligned data and prior cross-embodiment baselines, with additional gains from the style-transfer objective. Our results demonstrate that explicit behavior alignment enables more effective transfer from human demonstrations to robot policies.

</details>


### [139] [A Unified Inertial Encoder for Human and Humanoid Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10137)
*Yutian Chen,Shibo Zhao,Rocky Duan,Karen Liu,Carmelo Sferrazza,Guanya Shi,Yuheng Qiu,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一惯性编码器，通过运动学IMU合成构建人与类人机器人共享惯性接口，联合训练速度估计与运动-文本对齐，实现无需仿真、真实轨迹或微调的零样本类人机器人状态估计与语言 grounding 运动理解迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从机器人录制数据学习类人状态估计和语言 grounding 运动理解受限于机器人运动 repertoire，训练覆盖不足；人类运动数据集覆盖更广，但迁移需克服人体与类人机器人在几何结构、传感和物理扰动上的差异。

Method: 设计统一惯性编码器，利用运动学IMU合成在对应身体部位表示人类与类人机器人运动，形成共享惯性输入空间；在该空间内联合训练人类运动和运动学重定向类人运动，结合速度估计与运动-文本对齐，并采用连续时间编码及传感器与运动随机化。

Result: 模型零样本迁移至实体 Unitree G1，无需物理仿真、真实机器人训练轨迹或微调；在五个数据集上取得最低速度误差，实体 G1 上为 0.099 m/s；运动描述检索可扩展到仅在人类训练中出现的重定向 G1 运动类别。

Conclusion: 人类运动知识可支持类人机器人惯性里程计与语言 grounding 运动理解的泛化，实现跨具身零样本迁移。

Abstract: Learning humanoid state estimation and language-grounded motion understanding from robot recordings ties training coverage to the robot's motion repertoire. Human motion datasets offer broader coverage, yet transferring this knowledge requires overcoming differences in body geometry, sensing, and physical disturbances. To enable this transfer, we propose a unified inertial encoder whose shared inertial interface uses kinematic IMU synthesis to represent human and humanoid motion at corresponding body sites. Within this common input space, joint training on human and kinematically retargeted humanoid motion combines velocity estimation and motion--text alignment with continuous-time encoding and sensor and motion randomization. The resulting model transfers zero-shot to a physical Unitree G1 without physics simulation, real-robot training trajectories, or fine-tuning, achieving the lowest velocity error among evaluated methods across five datasets, including 0.099m/s on real G1. Its motion-description retrieval also extends to retargeted G1 motion categories observed only in human training, demonstrating how human motion knowledge can support the generalization of humanoid inertial odometry and language-grounded motion understanding.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Semantic Classes: Online Physical Parameter Estimation via Embedding-Conditioned Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10107)
*Alexander Borca-Tasciuc,Prayag Sharma,Franck Djeumou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种实时物理属性估计器，用交互驱动的混合高斯后验替代语义类别索引，直接从连续视觉嵌入推断接触前的摩擦、刚度等物理参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人若能在接触前从视觉推断与交互相关的物理属性，可改善非结构化环境中的规划与控制；但外观常存在歧义，视觉相似区域可能对应不同摩擦、刚度或下沉响应，点估计或固定语义类别会丢失多模态物理状态。

Method: 将逆建模等交互获得的物理参数高斯估计作为混合分量，构建以连续视觉嵌入为条件的潜在物理参数混合高斯后验；用嵌入相似度检索并加权相关历史交互；交互历史稀疏时引入条件生成先验进行支持感知融合；学习嵌入细化以提升检索相关性且不离散化表示。

Result: 实验表明该估计器可恢复多模态物理信念、拒绝无关交互历史、避免空间地图离散化，并能以控制器频率运行；在高保真自动驾驶赛车仿真中，面对未知低/高摩擦区域，后验使控制器随交互证据累积在多圈中自适应，提升性能并保持摩擦感知行为。

Conclusion: 以交互驱动混合高斯后验结合连续视觉嵌入，可实时保留接触前物理属性的多模态不确定性，并有效服务于规划与控制。

Abstract: Enabling robots to infer interaction-relevant physical properties from vision before contact would improve planning and control in unstructured environments. Vision provides useful pre-contact information, but appearance alone is often ambiguous: visually similar regions can induce different friction, stiffness, or sinkage responses upon contact. Existing approaches often collapse this ambiguity to a point estimate or aggregate experience through fixed semantic classes, limiting their ability to preserve distinct physical regimes. Instead, we propose a real-time estimator that replaces semantic class indexing with an interaction-grounded Gaussian-mixture posterior over latent physical parameters, conditioned directly on continuous visual embeddings. Our approach maintains interaction-derived Gaussian parameter estimates, typically from inverse modeling, as mixture components while using embedding similarity to retrieve and weight relevant past interactions for each new query region. A support-aware fusion mechanism incorporates a conditional generative prior model when interaction history is sparse, while a learned embedding refinement improves retrieval relevance without discretizing the representation. Our experiments show that the estimator recovers multimodal physical beliefs, rejects unrelated interaction history, avoids spatial map discretization, and evaluates at controller rates. In a high-fidelity autonomous racing simulation with unknown low- and high-friction surface regions, the posterior enables the controller to adapt over repeated laps as interaction evidence accumulates, improving performance while maintaining friction-aware behavior.

</details>


### [141] [UniLatent: Cross-Embodiment Transfer via Latent Observation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10061)
*Yufei Ding,Yi Du,Weiduo Yuan,Hao Jiang,Dylan Goetting,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Daniel Seita,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLatent 是一个跨具身迁移框架，通过在共享潜空间中对齐源/目标机器人观测，减少具身差异带来的观测鸿沟，使源域策略无需像素级图像翻译即可直接部署到目标机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据采集成本高且通常绑定特定具身，不同几何、外观和运动学导致示范难以复用，核心问题是跨具身观测差异。

Method: 在仿真中渲染成对的源-目标机器人观测，将源视觉编码器对齐到冻结的目标编码器；在编码后的源示范上训练策略，并直接运行于目标观测，无需像素级图像翻译。

Result: 在三个仿真任务和两个目标机械臂上实现最高 93% 零样本成功率，平均超过最强像素翻译基线 30 个点以上；真实 xArm-to-Franka 迁移中，每任务仅用 10 条目标示范微调即达到 27/30 次成功 rollout。

Conclusion: 潜空间对齐能有效缩小具身导致的观测差距，实现高成功率、低目标数据需求的跨具身策略迁移。

Abstract: Robot datasets are costly to collect and are usually tied to a specific embodiment, making it difficult to reuse demonstrations across robots with different geometry, appearance, and kinematics. We introduce UniLatent, a cross-embodiment transfer framework that reduces the embodiment-induced observation gap in a shared latent space. UniLatent renders paired source-target robot observations in simulation and aligns a source visual encoder to a frozen target encoder. A policy trained on encoded source demonstrations can then run directly on target observations, with no pixel-level image translation. Across three simulation tasks and two target robot arms, UniLatent reaches up to 93% zero-shot success, exceeding the strongest pixel-translation baseline by over 30 points on average. In real-world xArm-to-Franka transfer, fine-tuning with only 10 target demonstrations per task yields 27/30 successful rollouts. Code, videos and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [142] [Efficient Learning of Differentiable MPC Policies on the GPU via Smoothed ADMM and First-Order Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09937)
*Jianghan Zhang,Roland Schwan,Gabriel Bravo-Palacios,John Zhang,Thomas Lew,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SmoothedTurboMPC：一种结合 SQP 与对数障碍平滑 ADMM 内求解器的 GPU 并行可微 MPC 求解器，并与 SHAC 一阶强化学习结合，实现高效 MPC 策略训练与实车验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微 MPC 能端到端优化带约束的策略，但学习 MPC 策略存在两个瓶颈：主动集切换处灵敏度不光滑，以及每次策略评估都需优化求解导致计算昂贵。

Method: 设计 SmoothedTurboMPC，将序列二次规划与对数障碍平滑的 ADMM 内层求解结合，提供平滑梯度、收敛时精确灵敏度和 GPU 高效执行；训练端采用 Short-Horizon Actor-Critic 的一阶强化学习，复用 rollout 中的 MPC 解而非反复重新评估。

Result: 与先进求解器相比，端到端训练吞吐最高提升 4.4×；在车辆竞速任务中，回报高于 MPC PPO 且加速 46×，仅 7 分钟训练 330 万条转移；结合域随机化训练更可靠的 MPC 竞速策略，并在全尺寸赛车上验证。

Conclusion: 该工作通过平滑可微 MPC 求解与高效一阶 RL 训练相结合，显著提升 MPC 策略学习效率与实车可用性，并集成到开源 TurboMPC 求解器中。

Abstract: Differentiable model predictive control (MPC) enables structured, constraint-aware policies to be optimized end-to-end. However, learning MPC policies faces two bottlenecks: (1) nonsmooth sensitivities at active-set transitions and (2) expensive policy evaluation, since each requires an optimization solve.
