We first introduce SmoothedTurboMPC, a GPU-parallel differentiable MPC solver combining sequential quadratic programming (SQP) with a log-barrier-smoothed alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) inner solver. Its design enables smooth gradients, exact sensitivities at convergence, and efficient execution on GPUs. Second, to efficiently train MPC policies, we pair our solver with first-order reinforcement learning (FORL) using Short-Horizon Actor-Critic (SHAC), which reuses rollout MPC solutions instead of repeatedly re-evaluating them. Comparisons with state of the art solvers show that the approach achieves up to $4.4\times$ higher end-to-end training throughput. On a vehicle racing task, our method achieves higher returns than MPC PPO with a $46\times$ speedup, training on 3.3 million transitions in only 7 minutes. Finally, we use the framework to train a more reliable MPC racing policy via RL with domain randomization, and validate it on a full-scale race car. We integrate our advances into the open-source TurboMPC solver.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [$Σ$-LeWM: Learning World Models via S-Information for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12732)
*Xinrui Zu,Michael Przystupa,Zhao Yang,Shujian Yu,Kevin Sebastian Luck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在 LeWorldModel 中引入后向动力学预测器和逆向动力学动作预测器两个额外潜变量损失，可进一步约束潜嵌入结构，从而提升世界模型的控制性能与规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型已用于机器人操作与运动等基于模型的控制，但无解码器的世界模型仅依赖潜空间前向动力学预测器和熵正则项，潜表示结构可能不足；作者希望增强潜空间结构以提升实时真实机器人控制适用性。

Method: 在 LeWorldModel 架构上额外加入两个潜变量损失：后向动力学预测器和逆向动力学动作预测器，与仅使用潜空间前向动力学预测器和稳定化熵项的方案相比，进一步约束潜嵌入。

Result: 在标准世界模型基准和两个模拟机器人操作任务上，所提方法在控制性能和规划效率上均取得进一步提升。

Conclusion: 额外潜变量动力学损失能为世界模型潜嵌入引入有效结构，改善性能与规划效率，从而增强此类方法用于真实机器人实时基于模型控制的潜力。

Abstract: World models have increasingly been applied as a model-based control method for robotics tasks such as manipulation and locomotion. While early world model architectures relied on decoders to prevent mode collapse and learn a predictable latent space, recent works on world models, such as Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture or LeWorldModel, increasingly aim to learn embeddings without a grounding decoder, utilising only a latent forward dynamics predictor and a stabilising entropy term. In this paper, we set out to demonstrate that the further introduction of two additional latent losses to LeWorldModel, a backward dynamics predictor and an inverse dynamics action predictor, enforces further structure on the latent embedding, leading to further gains in both performance and planning efficiency. These are critical properties for improving the applicability of such methods to real-time model-based control of real-world robots. We demonstrate these gains on standard world model benchmarks and two simulated robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [144] [Simulation-aided Reinforcement Learning with Control Variates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12643)
*Omer Hemo,Ido Sefi,Mirco Mutti,Aviv Tamar,Kfir Y. Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于控制变量的保守仿真辅助强化学习方法，用仿真数据仅降低方差而不直接改变学习目标，并在策略评估、actor-critic策略改进及离线RL中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习依赖昂贵且有噪声的真实环境数据；仿真器虽可提供大量数据，但直接混合仿真与真实经验会因仿真不准确而引入偏差，甚至使解偏离最优策略。

Method: 将仿真数据作为控制变量用于保守的仿真辅助学习：在策略评估中仅利用仿真数据降低方差，而不直接塑造学习目标；随后结合actor-critic框架用于策略改进，并扩展到离线RL场景。

Result: 在多个MuJoCo基准任务上，该方法相较标准仿真混合方法取得改进，表明控制变量是仿真辅助强化学习的有效工具。

Conclusion: 控制变量式仿真辅助学习能够在不被仿真偏差主导学习目标的前提下利用仿真数据，为真实数据昂贵场景下的强化学习提供有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms often rely on data collected from a target environment, which can be expensive and noisy. Although simulators can generate large amounts of additional data, standard simulation mixing approaches, which directly combine simulated and real experience, can degrade performance due to simulator inaccuracies, especially when they bias the solution away from the optimal policy. We propose a conservative simulation-aided learning method based on control variates, where simulated data is used solely to reduce variance in the learning process rather than directly shaping the learning target. We develop our approach for Policy-Evaluation, which is a fundamental building block of RL algorithms. Then we combine it within the actor-critic framework towards policy improvement, as well as extend it towards offline RL scenarios. Experiments on multiple MuJoCo benchmark tasks demonstrate improvements over standard simulation mixing approaches, highlighting control variates as an effective tool for simulation-aided reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [145] [LawKAN: Group-Law Layers for Composition -- When Imposing a Known Law Pays Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12567)
*Anna Mishchenko,Nikolai Gavrilin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将已知非交换群复合律嵌入自包含层节点、仅学习局部增量与零初始化偏差修正的网络层；在SU(2)组合和真实机械臂上以更少参数达到或优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习模型会重复学习已知的复合变换规则并造成误差累积；经典估计器虽硬编码规则，却忽略硬件偏差。

Method: 在层节点中放置精确的非交换群律，边学习局部增量，零初始化的修正项 psi 学习群律的未知偏差；并给出预训练准则以预测精确可表示性。

Result: 在固定角度 SU(2) 组合中，54 参数稠密边模型为最准确的稠密模型，参数比最强固定矩阵基线少 27 倍，距 Bayes 下界仅 0.05 度；在真实机械臂上，孤立 306 参数 psi 在 N>=60 时达到 1.12–1.14 mm，优于 Gauss-Newton 的 1.29–1.33 mm，并可跨三个厂商和两种协议迁移。

Conclusion: 当数据符合 imposed group law 时，该配方可匹配经典拟合；当所施加的群律不符合任务时性能会下降，表明结构先验必须与任务一致。

Abstract: Many tasks compose transformations by a known rule: chained rotations in robotics and navigation. Learned models relearn it, compounding error; classical estimators hardcode it and miss hardware deviations. We impose the known structure and learn only the remainder: an exact non-commutative group law sits in the node of a self-contained layer, its edges learn local increments, and a zero-initialized correction psi learns unknown deviations of the law. A pre-training criterion predicts exact representability. On fixed-angle SU(2) composition, a 54-parameter dense edge is the most accurate dense model, at 27x fewer parameters than the strongest fixed-matrix baseline, and lies 0.05 deg above the Bayes floor. On real robot arms, an isolated 306-parameter psi reaches 1.12-1.14 mm versus 1.29-1.33 mm for Gauss-Newton at N >= 60, and the recipe transfers across three vendors and two protocols. The recipe matches classical fits where the data obey the law and loses where the imposed law is not the task's.

</details>


### [146] [RAIL: Representation-Aligned Imitation Learning for Student-Compatible Teacher Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12251)
*Meraj Mammadov,Pedro Zuidberg Dos Martires,Johannes Andreas Stork*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAIL通过在教师训练阶段用对比学习构建师生共享的潜在表征，并在该共享空间中训练教师策略，从而显著缩小特权信息导致的模仿差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从原始图像或车载传感器等高维观测中进行强化学习面临高维观测和稀疏奖励挑战。常见做法是先训练可访问特权状态的教师策略，再蒸馏为仅用原始输入的学生策略；但教师依赖学生不可得信息时会产生不可消除的模仿差距。现有方法多通过奖励塑形或教师训练后的学生额外强化学习来缓解该不匹配。

Method: 提出Representation-Aligned Imitation Learning（RAIL）。该方法在教师训练过程中即处理师生观测不匹配：利用对比学习学习教师与学生观测共享的潜在表征，并直接在该共享空间中训练教师策略。由于教师策略被约束在共享空间上运行，其学到的行为更可由学生观测复现。

Result: 在多个环境中，RAIL无需修改奖励或进行事后学生微调即可优于强基线，并超过直接从原始观测进行强化学习的方法；所学表征还支持通过仅训练教师实现对新任务的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: RAIL表明在教师训练阶段进行表征对齐可大幅降低模仿差距并保持任务性能，是一种无需奖励修改或后置学生微调的有效特权蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) from raw sensory inputs such as images or onboard sensors can be challenging due to high-dimensional observations and sparse rewards. A common strategy is to train a teacher policy with access to privileged state information and then distill it into a student policy that acts from raw inputs alone. However, when the teacher relies on information unavailable to the student, exact imitation may be impossible, creating an irreducible imitation gap. Existing approaches typically mitigate this mismatch through careful reward shaping or additional reinforcement learning on the student policy after the teacher has been trained. Instead, we introduce Representation-Aligned Imitation Learning (RAIL), which addresses the mismatch during teacher training itself. RAIL learns a latent representation shared across teacher and student observations using contrastive learning, and trains the teacher policy directly in this space. Because the teacher policy is constrained to operate on this shared space, it learns behaviors that are reproducible from student observations. This substantially reduces the imitation gap while preserving task performance. Across multiple environments, RAIL outperforms strong baselines without reward modification or post-hoc student fine-tuning, and surpasses direct reinforcement learning from raw observations. The learned representations further enable zero-shot transfer to new tasks through teacher-only training. Project site: this https URL .

</details>


### [147] [How Many Candidates Does a One-Step World Model Need? A Random-Codebook Account of Nearest-of-m Training on Frozen DINO Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11887)
*Yong Hwi Song,Keon Myung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在冻结的DINOv2/v3 patch token上检验一步随机世界模型的nearest-of-m（IMLE）目标：m=16在模式不确定性下覆盖优于50步flow matching（约1/30延迟），并用随机码本失真I(m)统一解释记忆化、模式概率扁平化和高维连续结果欠分散；Push-T自rollout匹配降低长时误差约三分之一，但规划成功率未提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步随机世界模型可避免确定性特征预测的模式平均，也避免扩散/flow每步数十次网络评估。近期工作猜想小m已足够，且应以严格proper energy score替代硬最小值；作者用可枚举未来的基准实证检验这两个主张。

Method: 在可枚举未来基准上，用冻结DINOv2/v3 patch tokens训练一步世界模型；比较nearest-of-m（m=16）、50步flow matching、一步flow生成器和匹配参数量的energy-score生成器；将nearest-of-m解释为m个生成样本构成随机码本的期望失真，并预注册三项预测；在随机Push-T变体上做自rollout逐步匹配、消融并评估规划。

Result: m=16 nearest-of-m在模式类不确定性下覆盖枚举未来优于50步flow matching（约1/30延迟）、一步flow与energy-score生成器，rollout保持在token流形上而flow会漂移；验证三项预测：非重复上下文中对已实现结果记忆化且受I(m)而非漏检概率控制，energy-score提高m也不逃脱；模式概率随m扁平化并与已知最优差0.01内；连续高维结果欠分散并限制真实机器人视频。自rollout逐步匹配将长时误差降约1/3，消融区分流形与覆盖收益；规划成功率未因风险估计准确而提升。

Conclusion: Nearest-of-m是高效的一步随机世界模型目标，小m在模式覆盖和流形保持上有效，但受码本失真导致的记忆化、模式扁平化和高维欠分散限制；准确风险估计不足以保证规划收益，真实机器人视频与规划仍是边界。

Abstract: One-step stochastic world models avoid the mode averaging of deterministic feature predictors without the tens of network evaluations per step that diffusion and flow matching require. The simplest such objective is nearest-of-m matching (conditional implicit maximum likelihood estimation, IMLE): draw m candidate futures and pull only the one closest to the observed outcome. Recent work conjectures that a small m suffices for feature-space world models and that a strictly proper energy score should replace the hard minimum. We test both claims with one-step world models on frozen DINOv2/v3 patch tokens, using benchmarks whose futures can be enumerated. Under mode-like uncertainty, nearest-of-m with m=16 covers the enumerated futures better than 50-step flow matching (at about 1/30 of its latency), one-step flow generators and an energy-score generator at matched parameter counts, and its rollouts stay on the token manifold where flow rollouts drift. One quantity governs the choice of m: the nearest-of-m objective is the expected distortion of a random codebook of m draws from the generator. This view yields three predictions, recorded before the corresponding runs: (i) memorization of the realized outcome in contexts that never repeat, whose strength is governed by the codebook's miss distortion I(m) rather than its miss probability, and which an energy-score generator does not escape by raising m; (ii) a flattening of mode probabilities with m, matching a known optimum within 0.01; and (iii) under-dispersion on continuous high-dimensional outcomes, which bounds the method on real robot video. Per-step matching on self-rollouts cuts long-horizon error by a third on a stochastic Push-T variant; an ablation separates its manifold and coverage benefits. Planning success does not improve despite accurate risk estimates, a negative result we report with its diagnosis.

</details>


### [148] [DeltaForge: A Post-hoc Mixture-of-Experts VLA for Multi-Domain Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11856)
*Bingzhang Wang,Mo Poorsartep,Yasaman Esfandiari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeltaForge 是首个面向驾驶的后验混合专家（post-hoc MoE）VLA 模型，可将多个独立按域微调的同源模型仅凭权重和小规模代理数据合并为一个多域模型，并以接近单模型的参数与计算成本达到接近各域专家的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按域独立微调驾驶 VLA 会产生大量专用专家，集中合并成本高；权重合并随异构域增多而精度下降，稠密 MoE 路由又过于昂贵，因此需要一种高效的后验多域整合方法。

Method: 利用同源基座模型在不同域微调后权重更新共享大量结构的特点，对所有更新联合做 SVD，将每个更新分解为共享部分和极小的域特定部分；路由器只需处理域特定小分量。随后在代理数据集上用软标签训练两个协作的 clip 级盲路由器，分别路由 VLM 推理专家和 flow-matching 动力学专家。新增域时只需将其权重更新投影到共享基上，无需端到端重训。

Result: 在覆盖城市、越野和高速道路，以及轿车、卡车和全地形车的六个多样域上，DeltaForge 接近各域专用专家精度，并超过所有合并、路由、集中式和联邦式基线，参数与计算成本接近单模型；还能泛化到未显式训练的域组合。

Conclusion: DeltaForge 提供了一种高效可扩展的多域驾驶 VLA 后验专家整合方案，在保持接近专用专家性能的同时显著降低合并与部署成本，并支持低成本扩展新域和未见域组合泛化。

Abstract: Fleets of driving vision-language-action (VLA) models are developed one domain at a time: each operator finetunes a shared base model on private data, producing specialized experts that are costly to consolidate into a single model. Weight merging loses accuracy as heterogeneous domains accumulate, and dense mixture-of-experts routing is prohibitively expensive to combine them. We present DeltaForge, to our knowledge the first post-hoc mixture-of-experts VLA for driving, which consolidates N independently finetuned per-domain models into a single multi-domain model from their weights and a small proxy set alone. When different operators finetune the same base model, their weight updates end up sharing a lot of structure. We exploit this by decomposing all the updates together using singular value decomposition (SVD), splitting each one into a shared part (the same across all domains) and a tiny domain-specific part. The router then only needs to work with those tiny domain-specific pieces, not the full expert weights, which is what keeps the approach efficient. Then two cooperating clip-level blind routers, one over the VLM reasoning experts and one over the flow-matching dynamics experts, are trained from soft labels on the proxy dataset. Across six diverse domains spanning urban, off-road, and highway driving on cars, trucks, and all-terrain vehicles (ATVs), DeltaForge approaches the accuracy of the per-domain experts and surpasses every merging, routing, centralized, and federated baseline at close to single-model parameter and compute cost. Adding a new domain requires only projecting its weight update onto the shared basis with no end-to-end retraining needed. The resulting model also generalizes to domain combinations it was never explicitly trained on.

</details>


### [149] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Internal Computation for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10822)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应内部计算（AIC），让迭代策略根据状态学习何时停止细化潜表示并行动，从而在保持或提升奖励的同时减少平均计算量与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代神经策略通常对每个决策投入相同计算，但不同状态从额外处理中获益不同；因此需要状态依赖的计算分配，而非固定停止调度。

Method: AIC将动作选择与停止决策联合训练；用期望深度惩罚对计算量定价，并以中间价值预测作为辅助监督，使策略学习何时停止细化潜表示并执行动作。

Result: 在最多8步细化下，AIC在12个基准任务上平均执行3.3步；在4个控制与机器人任务上相较固定8步策略降低31–44%的单决策延迟。其平均奖励在7个任务上超过最佳固定深度策略、在4个任务上落后。消融显示：在PickCube和AntMaze上停止机制贡献大部分奖励增益且执行更多细化步；在RareEvent上价值监督贡献大部分增益。

Conclusion: 状态依赖的自适应停止可显著减少迭代策略的计算与延迟，并在多数任务上达到或超过固定深度策略；停止机制与辅助价值监督的贡献因任务而异。

Abstract: Iterative neural policies usually spend the same computation on every decision, although the benefit of additional processing varies across states. We introduce Adaptive Internal Computation (AIC), an iterative policy that learns when to stop refining its latent representation and act. In AIC, we train action selection and stopping jointly, an expected-depth penalty values computation and intermediate value predictions provide auxiliary supervision. We show that when only some states benefit from further refinement, an ideal state-dependent allocator outperforms every state-independent stopping schedule at the same expected depth. With at most eight refinement steps per decision, AIC executes 3.3 steps on average across twelve benchmark tasks and lowers per-decision latency by 31--44% relative to a fixed eight-step policy on four control and robotics tasks. Its mean reward exceeds the best fixed-depth policy on seven tasks and trails it on four. Ablating adaptive stopping and value supervision separately shows that stopping accounts for most of the reward gain on PickCube and AntMaze, where it also executes more refinement steps and that supervision accounts for most of it on RareEvent.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Efficient VLA Inference via Retrieval-Based Speculation and Hardware Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11098)
*Zhengyang Cheng,He Zhu,Zeyu Guo,Zihan Liu,Anbang Wu,Yiming Gan,Jingwen Leng,Yu Feng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Zora 是一个面向自回归 VLA 模型推理的软硬件协同加速框架，通过 RAG 草稿、运动学验证与专用硬件降低边缘端推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型需在资源受限机器人平台上低延迟执行复杂指令，但自回归动作生成延迟高；现有投机解码依赖昂贵草稿模型，在边缘设备上效率低。

Method: 提出基于检索增强生成（RAG）的轻量草稿模型，利用机器人动力学避免额外神经草稿模型；采用运动学引导的动作接受策略和 KV-cache 共享注意力降低批量验证开销；并设计专用 Zora core 硬件支持，缓解 RAG 草稿带来的内存不规则访问。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准的四个 VLA 模型上评估，Zora 保持可比精度，纯软件优化相较 GPU 最高加速 3.4 倍，硬件支持最高加速 7.6 倍。

Conclusion: Zora 通过软硬件协同设计有效加速自回归 VLA 推理，在边缘机器人场景中实现高加速比并保持精度。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable robots to understand and execute complex instructions, but their autoregressive (AR) action generation introduces significant latency on resource-constrained robotic platforms. Speculative decoding can accelerate AR-based inference by parallelizing action generation and verification; however, existing approaches rely on costly draft models, which are inefficient on edge platforms. In this paper, we propose Zora, a hardware-software co-design framework for accelerating AR-based VLA inference. Specifically, Zora introduces a lightweight retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based draft model that exploits robotic dynamics without additional neural draft models. We further reduce batch verification overhead through a kinematics-guided action-acceptance strategy and a KV-cache-sharing attention mechanism. To address the retrieval overhead introduced by RAG-based drafting, we co-design dedicated hardware support, Zora core, to address the memory irregularity in RAG-based drafting. We evaluate Zora on four VLA models with the LIBERO benchmark. Zora retains comparable accuracy while achieving up to 3.4$\times$ speedup with software-only optimization and up to 7.6$\times$ speedup with hardware support over GPUs.

</details>


### [151] [The World Model Planning Accelerator (WMPA): Hardware-Algorithm Co-Design for Embedded AI in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10316)
*Prerana Ramkumar,Suhas Suresh Bharadwaj,Fazal Hameed*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文从信息论与布尔电路复杂度推导潜状态转移的算术操作和CMOS动态能量下界，揭示DreamerV3式RSSM在GPU上的能效差距与热瓶颈，并提出面向嵌入式世界模型规划的WMPA加速器架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式AI正从感知转向预测，机器人需在严格功耗和热预算下片上执行世界模型推演；但每次潜空间预测步的根本能量需求及真实硬件与理论下界的差距尚未被刻画。

Method: 推导潜状态转移所需算术操作数与CMOS动态能量的理论下界；分析DreamerV3式RSSM的rollout操作，指出其受内存带宽约束；开展3D瞬态热仿真；据此提出WMPA，结合近内存计算、实时熵估计、可变精度rollout单元和写时复制潜变量暂存区。

Result: GPU执行rollout的能耗至少为CMOS能量下界的5.9×10^10倍；持续背靠背规划可使毫米级硅片在理想铜散热器后仍升温至556 K；WMPA以40倍降功耗为目标，仿真中同一硅片温度约为306 K，接近环境温度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从理论能量下界到实际瓶颈的链条，表明嵌入式世界模型规划面临显著能效与热限制，并指出近内存计算与可变精度等架构协同设计有望实现热约束机器人上的实用规划。

Abstract: Embedded AI is moving from perception to prediction. Robots now use world models$\unicode{x2014}$learned simulators that predict the outcome of candidate actions in a compressed latent space before any is executed. Every predicted step must be computed on board within a tight power and thermal budget, yet the energy these steps fundamentally require, and how far real hardware sits from it, have not been characterized. We derive lower bounds on the arithmetic operations and the CMOS dynamic energy of a latent state transition from information theory and Boolean circuit complexity. Profiling a DreamerV3-style Recurrent State Space Model (RSSM) shows that every rollout operation is memory-bandwidth bound and that a GPU consumes at least $5.9\times10^{10}$ times the CMOS energy floor. Transient 3D thermal simulations show that this inefficiency is also a thermal one, since sustained back-to-back planning heats a millimeter-scale silicon tile to 556 K even behind an ideal copper heat spreader. Guided by this chain from bound to bottleneck, we propose the World Model Planning Accelerator (WMPA), a chip architecture for embedded world model planning that combines near-memory computation, real-time entropy estimation, variable-precision rollout units, and copy-on-write latent scratchpads. With a design target of 40x lower power, the same tile stays near ambient at 306 K in simulation, pointing toward practical planning on thermally constrained robots.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Rethinking Radar 3D Perception: Representation Learning from Raw Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12334)
*Miao Zhang,Mohamed Abdelsamad,Bin Yang,Sherif Abdulatif,Benedikt Loesch,Marco Altmann,Abhinav Valada,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文挑战“原始雷达频谱过于杂乱、不适合直接表征学习”的假设，提出用自监督掩码建模直接从密集原始雷达频谱学习结构表征，并在3D感知任务上超过现有雷达检测器和点云SSL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖手工降维、稀疏点云或紧凑Doppler描述子，但这类降维会引入刚性信号假设，丢弃弱而有用的结构并保留伪影；一旦信息被移除，监督学习也无法恢复。作者认为核心限制不是原始表征，而是缺少能区分结构化信号与噪声的学习目标。

Method: 作者将“结构”定义为在局部破坏下仍可重建的雷达频谱成分，将“噪声”定义为不可重建成分，并据此提出Leakage-aware Spectral MAE：对原始雷达立方体进行掩码自编码，采用受频谱泄漏导致的跨bin能量扩散启发的破坏策略；同时将Doppler视为x-y-D表示中的主要空间维度，把高度编码为特征，从而有效学习密集4D频谱。

Result: 不做手工降维时，该方法在雷达3D感知中持续优于最先进的雷达检测器和基于点云的自监督学习对应方法；将Doppler作为主要空间维度并编码高度，显著优于朴素通道堆叠。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在配合合适预训练目标时，原始雷达频谱可作为3D感知的有效表征，无需依赖手工降维或预定义信号-噪声分离。

Abstract: Raw radar spectra are widely viewed as too cluttered and noisy for direct representation learning, motivating handcrafted reductions, sparse point clouds, or compact Doppler descriptors in radar perception. We challenge this assumption and argue that the main limitation lies not in the raw representation itself, but in the absence of a learning objective that separates structured signal from noise. Handcrafted reductions partially address this problem but impose rigid assumptions about what constitutes signal, discarding weak yet informative structure while retaining artifacts that fit the reduction model. Once removed, such information cannot be recovered by supervised learning. We propose an alternative: structure is the component of a radar spectrum that remains reconstructable under localized corruption, while noise is the component that does not. This perspective motivates self-supervised masked modeling for dense radar spectra without predefined signal-noise separation. We instantiate it with Leakage-aware Spectral MAE, a masked autoencoder for raw radar cubes using a corruption strategy inspired by cross-bin energy spreading caused by spectral leakage. We also demonstrate the importance of Doppler organization: treating Doppler as a primary spatial dimension in an x-y-D representation, with height encoded as features, substantially outperforms naive channel stacking and enables effective learning from dense 4D spectra. Without handcrafted reductions, our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art radar-based detectors and point-cloud-based self-supervised learning (SSL) counterparts. These results establish raw radar spectra as an effective representation for 3D perception when paired with an appropriate pretraining objective.

</details>


### [153] [SCALE: Small-Model-First Concept Abstraction with LLM-Guided Experience Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12120)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCALE框架：以轻量模型为主、按需触发LLM反思，将经验抽象为可复用概念并内化为分层神经表示，从而提升自主智能体的知识获取与复用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体需持续从经验中获取可复用知识，不能对每个决策都依赖高成本LLM推理；现有基于经验的方法难以将累积观察与推理转化为轻量模型可独立应用的紧凑知识。

Method: SCALE采用小模型优先框架，融合选择性LLM推理、经验驱动概念抽象与知识内化。轻量模型负责常规预测；当出现预测错误或跨回合证据积累时，选择性触发LLM反思。LLM分析相关历史经验，生成正负反事实样例并提出可复用概念假设；经独立验证的概念逐步纳入分层神经表示。已掌握经验从活跃记忆中排除，但原始记录保留在完整档案中。

Result: 在十个随机种子实验中，历史召回将留出集准确率从87.11%提升至98.28%；分层概念知识将困难案例准确率从55.33%提升至66.47%；抽象归档将活跃经验记录从6000条降至1528条。

Conclusion: SCALE表明，选择性LLM推理与经验概念化、知识内化相结合，可在降低LLM依赖和活跃记忆规模的同时提升轻量模型的预测准确率与困难案例表现，为自主智能体的可持续经验学习提供有效路径。

Abstract: Autonomous agents must continually acquire reusable knowledge from experience without relying on costly large language model (LLM) reasoning for every decision. However, existing experience-based approaches often struggle to transform accumulated observations and reasoning into compact knowledge that lightweight models can independently apply. This paper proposes SCALE, a small-model-first framework that integrates selective LLM reasoning, experience-driven concept abstraction, and knowledge internalization. A lightweight model handles routine predictions, while prediction errors and accumulated cross-episode evidence selectively trigger LLM reflection. The LLM analyzes relevant historical experiences, generates positive and negative counterfactual examples, and proposes reusable concept hypotheses. Independently validated concepts are progressively incorporated into hierarchical neural representations, enabling lightweight models to exploit increasingly abstract knowledge. Meanwhile, mastered experiences are excluded from active memory while their original records remain available in a complete archive. Experiments across ten random seeds show that historical recall improves held-out accuracy from 87.11% to 98.28%, hierarchical concept knowledge increases difficult-case accuracy from 55.33% to 66.47%, and abstract archiving reduces active experience records from 6,000 to 1,528.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Certified Zonotopic Reachability Analysis for Trajectory Tracking on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12767)
*Yanliang Huang,Peng Xie,Zhen Zhang,Zhuoqi Zeng,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于李群误差动力学与zonotope传播的连续时间轨迹跟踪误差管（tube）方法，用于在存在未知有界扰动时严格界定自主车辆的跟踪误差，从而在执行前认证规划轨迹的安全性；相比单一不变椭球或区间方法更紧，并在仿真与真实四旋翼飞行中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主车辆常贴近障碍物跟踪规划轨迹，未知扰动会导致跟踪误差；现有基于集合的方法用整条轨迹的单一不变椭球或区间界定误差，较为保守。航空/航天车辆的跟踪误差在矩阵李群上演化且动力学非线性，需要更紧且可证明的误差界。

Method: 针对group-affine系统，在参考输入下误差动力学在李代数坐标中线性；控制输入与扰动通过依赖误差的逆右雅可比进入。作者用zonotope传播误差动力学的线性部分，并以闭式形式包络逆右雅可比对扰动的影响，得到连续时间可达跟踪误差的严格外包。对于部分误差分量无输入导致的非匹配非线性项，采用混合坐标使其匹配，并由控制器抵消所有不含扰动的非线性项。

Result: 仿真中的平面飞行器与全驱动多旋翼，以及真实四旋翼飞行实验表明，所提tube比椭球方法更紧，且包含所有实测姿态误差；在更大扰动和相同反馈增益下，椭球方法无法给出有效椭球，而所提tube仍保持有效。

Conclusion: 该方法为李群上自主车辆的扰动下轨迹跟踪提供更紧、可证明且连续时间有效的安全认证误差界，并能处理无输入误差分量引起的非匹配非线性项，优于传统椭球界。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles track planned trajectories close to obstacles. Unknown disturbances make them deviate from these trajectories. With a bound on the tracking error that disturbances can cause, a planned trajectory can be certified as safe before execution. Existing set-based methods bound the tracking error by one invariant ellipsoid for the whole trajectory or by intervals, which are both conservative. For aerial and space vehicles, the tracking error evolves on a matrix Lie group with nonlinear dynamics. However, for group-affine systems, the error dynamics are linear in Lie algebra coordinates when the vehicle receives the inputs of the reference. The control input and the disturbance enter through the inverse right Jacobian, a matrix that depends on the error. We propagate the linear part of the error dynamics with zonotopes and enclose the effect of the inverse right Jacobian on the disturbance in closed form. The resulting tubes are tight and provably contain all reachable tracking errors in continuous time. When some error components have no input, the inverse right Jacobian leaves unmatched nonlinear terms in the closed loop. None of the tested methods certifies such a closed loop. Hybrid coordinates make these terms matched, so that our controller cancels every nonlinear term that does not contain the disturbance. Our tubes give tighter bounds than the ellipsoid method in simulations of a planar aircraft and a fully actuated multirotor and in real flights of a quadrotor, where the tube contains every measured attitude error. Under larger disturbances and the same feedback gain, the ellipsoid method yields no valid ellipsoid, whereas our tubes remain valid.

</details>


### [155] [Generalizable Output Feedback Optimal Control via Transformers Without Explicit State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11815)
*Maitham F. AL-Sunni,Turki Bin Mohaya,John M. Dolan,Peter Seiler*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证：一个Transformer策略可统一控制一族部分可观测、含噪声的MIMO LTI系统；通过模仿最优LQG控制，并在运行时仅依赖短窗口噪声测量，无需系统矩阵或状态估计器，即可在已见系统上达到约0.4%、未见系统经轻量微调后约1.0%的中位最优成本差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优控制通常为每个被控对象单独设计控制器。作者追问：能否用一个学习到的策略服务于整个部分可观测、含噪声线性系统族，从而避免逐系统建模与控制器设计。

Method: 在维度、代价矩阵和噪声水平均变化的异构MIMO LTI系统族上，训练Transformer模仿最优LQG控制。采用系统级标准化、零填充、掩码以及代价与噪声协方差编码形成共享表示，使一组权重可控制整个系统族。运行时仅需一段短窗口的噪声测量即可输出控制动作，不需要对象矩阵和状态估计器。理论上刻画了以动态状态空间模型表示的最优LQG策略与作用于有限测量窗口的静态Transformer之间的关系，并将任意因果策略的闭环超额代价表示为其动作偏离最优动作的能量。

Result: 在28个仿真系统上评估：训练策略对已见系统达到最优成本，中位差距为0.4%；对未见系统，经过轻量微调后中位差距为1.0%。

Conclusion: 一个共享Transformer策略能够有效覆盖异构部分可观测噪声线性系统族，并在无需显式系统模型和状态估计器的情况下接近最优LQG成本；理论上给出了静态窗口策略与最优动态LQG策略及闭环超额代价的联系。

Abstract: Classical optimal control is used to design a separate controller for each plant. This paper asks a different question. Can one learned policy serve a whole family of partially observed, noisy linear systems? We show that one transformer can. The policy is trained to imitate optimal LQG control on a heterogeneous family of Linear Time-Invariant (LTI) systems with Multiple Inputs and Multiple Outputs (MIMO), whose dimensions, cost matrices, and noise levels all vary. A shared representation with system-wise standardization, zero-padding, masking, and an encoding of the cost and noise covariances lets one set of weights control every member of the family. At run time, a short window of noisy measurements is all the policy needs to produce a control action, with no plant matrices and no state estimator. We characterize the relation between the optimal LQG policy (represented as a dynamic state-space model) and a static transformer acting on a finite window of measurements. We then express the closed-loop excess cost of any causal policy as the energy of its deviation from the optimal action. Finally, we evaluate the approach on 28 simulated systems. The trained policy reaches the optimal cost to within a median gap of $0.4\%$ for seen systems. For unseen systems, a light fine-tuning step brings the median gap to $1.0\%$.

</details>


### [156] [Concurrent Learning-Based Inertial Parameter Estimation and Control of Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11555)
*Sandesh Thapa,He Bai*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于积分并发学习（ICL）的几何控制器，用于质心与几何中心不重合的四旋翼无人机轨迹跟踪与惯性参数在线估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机载IMU、相机、电池等载荷未必位于几何中心，飞行中受外界扰动还可能移动，导致质心偏移和惯性矩阵非对角，因此需要在线估计质量、质心偏移和完整惯性矩阵。

Method: 设计基于积分并发学习的几何控制器，在线估计质量、质心偏移和完整惯性矩阵，不依赖加速度计或数值微分，并利用在线估计的质心偏移修正期望轨迹进行跟踪控制。

Result: 在有限激励条件下证明估计参数收敛到真值，并通过仿真示例验证算法有效性。

Conclusion: 所提ICL几何控制方法能够同时实现四旋翼无人机轨迹跟踪与惯性参数在线估计，并在仿真中表现有效。

Abstract: In this letter, we consider trajectory tracking control and inertial parameter estimation of a quadrotor UAV whose center of mass (CoM) does not coincide with its geometric center. Onboard payloads like an IMU, a camera, or a battery are not necessarily placed at its geometric center. Moreover, during the flight these sensors might move because of external disturbances and cause a CoM offset. Thus, these events and equipment introduce an offset and cause the inertia matrix to be non-diagonal. We develop an integral concurrent learning (ICL)-based geometric controller that estimates the mass, the CoM offset, and the full inertia matrix online, without an accelerometer or numerical derivatives, and tracks a given trajectory corrected by the offset estimated online. We prove that the estimated parameters converge to their true values under a finite excitation condition. We validate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm using a simulation example.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Cable construction effects on the lateral forces and wakes of helically stranded cables under oblique inflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11286)
*Tielin Ma,Junlin Zhang,Jingcheng Fu,Chongwen Jiang,Chuan Ye*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究通过大涡模拟与谱本征正交分解，揭示斜向来流下绞合电缆截面对称性和股槽深度主导气动力与尾流模态，并表明平均侧向力与侧向力脉动可解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中拖曳系统中的螺旋绞合电缆常承受斜向来流，但现有模型多将其简化为光滑圆柱，忽略三维跨流耦合与表面织构，导致气动载荷预测可能失真。

Method: 对光滑圆柱及四种绞合电缆在22.5°至90°四个倾角下开展静止大涡模拟，Re约1.0×10^4，并用谱本征正交分解分析尾流；同时进行力源分解以区分表面压力与黏性贡献。

Result: 表面压力贡献平均法向力的94.9%–98.3%，并主导平均侧向力。高对称准圆截面保持周向压力平衡并在中高倾角出现展向同步Karman脱落和低秩主导模态；深槽低对称三瓣截面导致非对称分离、侧向回流区偏转及宽带能量分布。准圆(1×19)电缆平均侧向力小但周期脉动强，深槽(3×7)电缆脉动弱但平均侧向力显著。

Conclusion: 绞合电缆气动特性具有构造依赖性，平均侧向力与侧向力脉动并不一致，需在电缆选型与气动载荷建模中考虑截面对称性和股槽深度。

Abstract: Helically stranded cables in aerial towing systems experience oblique inflow, but are often modeled as smooth cylinders, neglecting three-dimensional cross-stream coupling and surface texture. We perform large-eddy simulations and spectral proper orthogonal decomposition for a smooth cylinder and four stranded cables at four inclinations from $22.5^\circ$ to $90^\circ$, all stationary, with $Re \approx 1.0 \times 10^4$. Aerodynamic differences are governed mainly by cross-sectional symmetry and strand-scale groove depth. Force-source decomposition shows that surface pressure contributes 94.9%--98.3% of the mean normal force and dominates the mean lateral force. High-symmetry, quasi-circular envelopes maintain balanced circumferential pressure, whereas deep-grooved, low-symmetry tri-lobed geometries produce asymmetric separation and a persistent lateral pressure imbalance. In the wake, high-symmetry cables sustain spanwise-synchronized Karman shedding at moderate-to-high inclinations, concentrating fluctuation energy in a dominant low-rank mode. Deep grooves disrupt shedding coherence, deflect the recirculation zone laterally, and spread energy across broadband scales. Mean lateral force and lateral-force fluctuations are therefore decoupled: the quasi-circular $(1 \times 19)$ cable has minimal mean lateral force but strong periodic fluctuations, while the deep-grooved $(3 \times 7)$ cable has weak fluctuations but appreciable mean lateral force. These results support construction-dependent cable selection and aerodynamic load modeling.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Co-Creating Neurorehabilitation Technology Alongside Lived Experience Experts: A Participatory Design Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12202)
*Irene L. Y. Beck,Salvatore L. Cucinella,Johannes B. J. Bussmann,Natacha Léon,José López-Sánchez,Francesca Lunardini,Katherine L. Poggensee,Cristina Vázquez-González,Joost de Winter,Laura Marchal-Crespo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出并评估了一种在神经康复技术早期设计阶段引入患者生活体验专家的参与式设计工作坊，证明其能有效将设计讨论转向以生活体验为核心的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生活体验专家通常仅在原型完成后才参与测试，早期参与不足限制了神经康复技术设计的相关性和有效性，因此需要一种可操作的早期参与式设计方法并评估其影响。

Method: 在第6届国际神经康复大会上开展参与式设计工作坊，5名卒中生活体验专家与18名参会者（含照护者）分为5组，依据Double Diamond模型的Discover、Define、Develop阶段，使用Journey Maps和数据收集笔记本围绕生活体验专家提出的目标共同创建设计方案；对Journey Maps进行主题分析，并用大语言模型对小组录音转录进行结构化提取。

Result: 生活体验输入使讨论从基于学科的假设转向以生活体验为基础的重点，包括恐惧、信心、感官不适、疲劳、美学和独立性；狭窄目标（如打开泡罩包装）产生任务特异性方案，较宽泛目标产生设备概念（如骑行感官反馈和带刺激的烹饪夹板）；参会者与生活体验专家满意度均较高，且工作坊工具与流程已共享以促进采用。

Conclusion: 该工作坊为在神经康复技术开发早期引入生活体验专家提供了实用基础；未来迭代应更好捕捉心理与社会维度、增加反思时间，并通过研究者与生活体验社区的持续跨学科参与纳入全部相关利益相关者。

Abstract: Involving patients' lived experience (LE) early in neurorehabilitation technology design can improve the relevance and effectiveness of solutions, yet LE experts are often involved only when finished prototypes are tested. We present and evaluate a participatory design (PD) workshop for early LE involvement, examine how LE contributions influence design processes and outcomes, and provide recommendations for similar approaches. We conducted a PD workshop at the 6th International Conference on NeuroRehabilitation. Five people who have had a stroke (LE experts) and 18 workshop attendees (including the LE experts' caregivers) were organized into five groups. Following the Discover, Define, and Develop phases of the Double Diamond model, each group used Journey Maps and Data Collection Notebooks to co-create a solution for an objective specified by an LE expert. Journey Maps were thematically analyzed, and group audio transcripts underwent structured extraction using a large language model. LE input shifted discussions from discipline-based assumptions toward priorities grounded in lived experience, including fear, confidence, sensory discomfort, fatigue, aesthetics, and independence. Narrow objectives, such as opening a blister pack, produced task-specific solutions, whereas broader objectives generated device concepts, including sensory feedback for cycling and a stimulation-equipped cooking splint. Satisfaction was high among attendees and LE experts. All workshop tools and protocols are shared to support adoption. The workshop offers a practical foundation for involving LE experts early in neurorehabilitation technology development. Future iterations should better capture psychological and social dimensions, allow more reflection, and include the full range of relevant stakeholders through sustained transdisciplinary engagement between researchers and the LE community.

</details>


### [159] [ROS2MR: Connecting ROS 2 Motion Planning to Mixed-Reality Trajectory Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11830)
*Santiago Berrezueta-Guzman,Habiba-Loai Khalil,Andrei Koshelev,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出ROS2MR，将YAML序列化的ROS 2关节轨迹（含MoveIt运动规划）转换为MR-Robotics LAB的JSON回放格式，实现无需实时ROS连接的MR轨迹检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MR可用于在真实环境中检查机器人规划运动，但机器人软件输出的关节配置与时间信息需转换为MR应用可回放的格式，且需兼容现有平台与不同头显。

Method: ROS2MR对输入进行校验，按名称映射机械臂关节，以30 Hz名义频率重采样：有速度时用三次Hermite插值，否则线性插值；二值夹爪命令单独用零阶保持采样。Python导出助手可合并多段运动规划与夹爪状态，并检查分段边界位置一致性；应用还适配Apple Vision Pro与Meta Quest 3。

Result: 技术评估表明转换器对密集、稀疏和不规则时间输入均保持归一化时长与端点构型。提供速度时位置RMSE约2.91×10^-5度；不提供速度时稀疏输入增至0.85度，且位置接近不能保证速度曲线准确。抓放序列保持开-闭-开夹爪顺序，但非网格对齐切换被移到输出采样网格。

Conclusion: 该文件式转换通路使ROS生成的轨迹可在MR中检查，无需实时ROS连接。

Abstract: Mixed reality (MR) allows users to inspect planned robot motion within their physical surroundings, but displaying trajectories from robotics software requires converting their joint configurations and timing into a format the MR application can play back. This paper presents ROS2MR, an extension of the MR-Robotics LAB platform that converts YAML-serialized ROS 2 joint trajectories, including MoveIt motion plans, into the platform's existing JSON playback format. The converter validates the input, maps arm joints by name, and resamples the trajectory at a nominal rate of 30 Hz, using cubic Hermite interpolation when joint velocities are supplied and linear interpolation otherwise, while binary gripper commands are sampled separately using a zero-order hold. A Python export helper combines sequences of motion plans and gripper states into a single trajectory and checks positional agreement at segment boundaries. The MR application was also adapted for Apple Vision Pro in addition to Meta Quest 3. A technical evaluation showed that the converter preserved the normalized duration and endpoint configurations for dense, sparse, and irregularly timed inputs. With supplied velocities, the position RMSE was approximately 2.91x10^-5 degrees for all sampling patterns; without them, sparse input increased it to 0.85 degrees, and close positional agreement did not guarantee accurate velocity profiles. A pick-and-place sequence retained its open--closed--open gripper order, although off-grid transitions were shifted to the output sampling grid. The resulting file-based pathway enables inspection of ROS-generated trajectories in MR without a live ROS connection.

</details>


### [160] [Industry case studies of adapting TTS for smooth interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09914)
*Paige Tuttösi,Eléonore Ferrier-Barbut,Anais Scipioni,Olivia Yung,Ha Eun Shim,Ajla Basic,Yue Wang,H. Henny Yeung*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该文回顾自适应TTS研究，指出生产级自适应TTS系统稀缺，并报告在养老院和机场两个真实HRI场景中应用简单TTS适配（音高、时长、强度）的定性结果，显示有前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经ASR/TTS常面向平均用户和理想场景，面对特殊听力需求或非标准说话方式时性能可能下降；同时工业级、生产级自适应TTS实现很少。

Method: 回顾近期TTS研究，基于既有研究对TTS系统实施适配修改，并在两个真实HRI场景开展定性案例研究：养老院中的老年人护理环境和噪声导致理解问题的机场。

Result: 定性结果表明，对机器人语音的音高、时长和强度进行相对简单的修改具有 promising 方向，并为更大规模、更正式的TTS适配改善HRI研究提供框架。

Conclusion: 自适应TTS的生产部署仍不足；简单的韵律参数适配在真实HRI中显示出潜力，值得通过更大规模正式研究验证。

Abstract: Modern, neural-based ASR and TTS systems for speech communication are now very sophisticated, but they are frequently designed for average users in more idealized scenarios, which can underperform when interacting with users having specialized listening needs, or who speak in non-standard ways. Here we review recent TTS research, and note that industrial, production-level implementations of adaptive TTS systems are scarce. We describe how we implemented changes to TTS systems based on prior research, and then report qualitative results from two case studies, where adapted TTS techniques are applied to real-world HRI settings: First, in an older adult care home, and second, in an airport, where noise levels create comprehension problems. Results suggest promising directions using relatively simple modifications to pitch, duration, and intensity of the robot's speech, and offer a framework for larger, more formal studies of how TTS adaptations can improve HRI.

</details>


### [161] [Engagement Estimation in Human-Robot and Human-Human Interactions across Domain, Age and Culture: MultiMediate'26 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09913)
*Marius Funk,Daksitha Withanage Don,Michal Balazia,Victor Oei,Shogo Okada,François Brémond,Andreas Bulling,Elisabeth André,Philipp Müller*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MultiMediate'26 挑战将参与度估计从成人对话扩展到儿童-儿童和儿童-机器人互动，介绍 NoXi、NoXi-J、MPIIGI、PInSoRo 数据集、多模态特征与跨域基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类互动中的参与度对辅助式和情境感知 AI 系统至关重要。以往挑战主要估计成人跨文化对话中的连续参与度，但参与度可采用不同度量，且儿童与成人表现不同，因此需要扩展参与度类型、年龄范围和互动类型。

Method: MultiMediate'26 纳入 PInSoRo 的儿童-儿童和儿童-机器人英语游戏会话，并以分类方式标注任务参与度和社会参与度；论文介绍挑战目标与四个数据集，展示提取的多模态特征，提出跨所有领域的参与度估计基线模型，并讨论挑战提交与相关方法。

Result: 摘要未报告具体性能数值；主要成果是构建了覆盖多领域、多年龄和多互动类型的参与度估计挑战，并提供基线模型及公开的基线代码、排行榜、数据集和提取特征。

Conclusion: 该工作通过扩展数据集与参与度标注类型，推动参与度估计在年龄、互动类型和度量方式上的泛化研究，并提供透明开放的挑战环境。

Abstract: Understanding engagement in human interaction is essential for modelling assistive and context-aware AI systems. Previously, the MultiMediate challenge has focused on estimating momentary continuous engagement values in adult conversation across cultures, achieving high levels of generalisability across domains. However, engagement can be measured in different metrics and manifest differently between children and adults. MultiMediate'26 therefore expands the evaluated domain set to include the PInSoRo dataset of Child-Child and Child-Robot play sessions in English. These sessions were annotated categorically with task and social engagement, diversifying engagement types, age ranges and interaction types. In this paper, we describe the MultiMediate'26 challenge and its goals, introduce the NoXi, NoXi-J, MPIIGI, and PInSoRo datasets, showcase extracted multi-modal features, present our baseline model for engagement estimation across all domains, and discuss challenge submissions and approaches. In an effort to provide a transparent and welcoming challenge environment, baseline code, leaderboard, datasets, and extracted features are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [162] [How to start a conversation proactively?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09906)
*Mohit Nathani,Aaditya Gupta,Eshwar Dhande,Chayan Sarkar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该文提出面向社交机器人主动发起对话的情境变量模式，并构建上下文对齐的开场白数据集CCO，通过微调小语言模型提升主动对话开场白的语境适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动发起对话是社交机器人的关键能力，但如何在特定情境中决定何时、如何开口仍缺乏原则化的上下文定义；现有LLM多为回合反应式，即使有上下文信息也常生成泛化或单调的开场白，在具身社交机器人中不当或时机不佳的发起会损害用户参与度和融洽关系。

Method: 作者提出一个联合刻画主动对话发起相关情境的上下文变量模式，用于结构化感知、情境和交互线索以供语言模型利用；针对训练数据缺失，构建覆盖多样日常场景的上下文对齐对话开场白数据集CCO，并基于该数据集微调小语言模型以生成主动对话发起内容。

Result: 通过涉及零样本LLM、现成SLM和微调模型的对比评估，研究表明显式上下文结构化与针对性数据策展能显著提升对话开场白的质量和上下文适当性。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了上下文建模对主动社交交互的重要性，并为对话机器人提供了可操作的构建模块。

Abstract: Proactively initiating a conversation is a fundamental social competence and a critical capability for social robots. However, determining how and when to start a conversation in a given situation remains an open challenge, primarily due to the lack of a principled definition of conversational context. While recent large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive conversational fluency, they are predominantly turn-reactive and often produce generic or monotonous openers when tasked with initiating interaction, even when contextual information is available. Such limitations are particularly problematic for embodied social robots, where inappropriate or poorly timed initiations can negatively affect user engagement and rapport. In this work, we propose a schema of contextual variables that jointly characterize situations relevant for proactive conversation initiation. The schema is designed to structure perceptual, situational, and interactional cues such that they can be effectively exploited by language models. Furthermore, motivated by the absence of suitable training data, we introduce a curated dataset of contextually-aligned conversation openers (CCO) covering diverse everyday scenarios. Using this dataset, we fine-tune small language models (SLMs) to generate proactive conversation initiations. Through a comparative evaluation involving zero-shot LLMs, off-the-shelf SLMs, and our fine-tuned models, we demonstrate that explicit contextual structuring and targeted data curation substantially improve the quality and contextual appropriateness of conversation openers. Our results highlight the importance of context modeling for proactive social interaction and provide actionable building blocks for conversational robots.

</details>


### [163] [Building Rapport with Self-Disclosure in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09905)
*Haritha Anupindi,Chayan Sarkar,Tapas Nayak,Marynel Vazquez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出对话系统RADIA，利用RAG检索模拟自传体记忆，使机器人能在面对面人机交互中进行第一人称自我披露，并通过真人用户实验证明其能显著增强用户感知的融洽关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人日益进入日常环境，建立融洽关系对持续且有意义的人机交互至关重要。自我披露在人际沟通中是建立融洽关系的成熟机制，但其在人机交互中的作用尚不清楚；现有基于大语言模型的对话机器人多作为被动信息提供者，缺乏社会互惠性和关系深度。

Method: 提出RADIA对话系统，基于检索增强生成（RAG）检索并引用模拟自传体记忆，将第一人称自我披露自然融入持续对话。通过面对面用户研究，将进行自我披露的机器人与不进行自我披露的基线机器人进行对比。

Result: 结果表明，机器人自我披露显著影响对话动态，并提升用户对机器人的感知融洽关系。

Conclusion: 自我披露是促进长期且具有社会参与性的人机交互的有价值社会行为。

Abstract: As social robots become increasingly present in everyday environments, establishing rapport with users is critical for enabling sustained and meaningful human-robot interaction. In human-human communication, self-disclosure is a well-established mechanism for building rapport; however, its role and impact in human-robot interaction (HRI) are not yet well understood. Although recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have improved the linguistic competence of conversational robots, such systems are typically deployed as reactive information providers, offering limited social reciprocity and relational depth. In this article, we investigate the role of robot self-disclosure in fostering rapport during face-to-face human-robot interaction. We propose a dialogue system, called RADIA that leverages retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to enable a robot to retrieve and reference simulated autobiographical memories, allowing first-person self-disclosure to be incorporated naturally into ongoing conversations. We evaluate the proposed approach through an in-person user study, comparing interactions with a self-disclosing robot against a baseline non-self-disclosing robot. Our results show that robot self-disclosure significantly influences conversational dynamics and enhances users' perceived rapport with the robot, suggesting that self-disclosure is a valuable social behavior for long-term and socially engaging human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [164] [KR A1: Design and Preliminary Evaluation of a Humanoid-Robot Interaction Prototype Integrating Personalized Voice Synthesis, Contextual Dialogue, and Emotion-Aware Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09900)
*Mahek Aanthu,Om Aanthu,Aaryan AK Aanthu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: KR A1 是一个面向潜在心理健康应用的人形机器人交互原型，集成个性化语音合成、上下文对话与情绪感知响应；本文仅初步评估了语音克隆模块，整体系统尚未进行临床或正式效能验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将个性化语音、上下文对话和情绪感知结合的人形机器人交互系统用于潜在心理健康支持，并验证关键技术组件的可行性。

Method: 系统采用本地部署的 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct（经 Ollama）进行自然语言理解与回复生成，OpenVoice v2 实现零样本说话人自适应语音合成，情绪处理采用关键词启发式与 LLM 推理两级流水线，并结合 InsightFace 人脸识别和基于 FAISS 与 sentence-transformer 嵌入的向量记忆检索实现个性化交互。实验评估仅针对语音克隆组件，使用 MFCC、音高、频谱质心、频谱对比度、频谱滚降、过零率、共振峰和说话人嵌入余弦相似度等 8 项声学相似度指标，并与同说话人/跨说话人基线进行比较。

Result: 语音克隆在小型非正式开发样本上与参考录音进行了声学相似度比较，但摘要未报告具体定量结果，也未进行统计显著性检验；情绪识别准确率、端到端响应延迟以及治疗或临床效果均未正式评估。所有测试均由开发团队非正式自测，无患者或外部人类参与者。

Conclusion: KR A1 目前属于技术验证型原型，初步展示了多模块集成的可行性，但现有证据仅限于语音克隆的初步相似度评估，不足以支持其心理健康干预有效性或临床可用性结论；后续需开展严格的留出基线、统计检验、端到端性能和临床场景评估。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and preliminary evaluation of KR A1, a humanoid-robot interaction prototype that integrates personalized voice synthesis, contextual dialogue processing, and emotion-aware response generation for potential mental-healthcare applications. The system architecture combines a locally hosted large language model (Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, served via Ollama) for natural-language understanding and response generation, OpenVoice v2 for zero-shot speaker-adaptive speech synthesis, and a two-tier emotion-processing pipeline comprising a keyword heuristic and LLM-based inference. Face recognition (InsightFace) and vector memory retrieval (FAISS with sentence-transformer embeddings) enable user-personalized interaction. The experimental evaluation reported here is limited to the voice-cloning component: cloned voices were evaluated against reference recordings using eight acoustic similarity measures - mel-frequency cepstral coefficients, pitch, spectral centroid, spectral contrast, spectral rolloff, zero-crossing rate, formants, and speaker-embedding cosine similarity - supplemented by a same-speaker versus cross-speaker baseline comparison. All tests were conducted on a small, informal development sample; no statistical significance testing against a held-out baseline was performed. The emotion-recognition accuracy, end-to-end response latency, and therapeutic or clinical impact of the integrated system have not yet been formally evaluated and are identified as directions for future work. All testing was informal and self-conducted by the development team; no patients or external human participants were involved, and the system has not been evaluated or deployed in a clinical setting.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Maximizing Detection Probability in UAV Search Missions using Random-Key Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12317)
*Antonio Dantas,Elcio Shiguemori,Antonio Chaves*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该文提出一种面向搜救无人机路径规划的可配置数据驱动智能路由架构，结合闭环概率更新与Random-Key Optimizers，将问题建模为动态概率型开放车辆路径问题，以自适应平衡发现概率最大化与资源最小化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机搜救路径规划面临随机性与实时性约束，需要在不确定环境中兼顾搜索概率和资源消耗，并应对先验信息噪声或偏差。

Method: 构建可配置的数据驱动智能路由架构，将闭环概率更新与Random-Key Optimizers结合；把问题数学建模为开放车辆路径问题（OVRP）的动态概率变体，使求解器可在概率最大化与资源最小化两个目标间自适应切换。

Result: 框架在噪声先验下可优雅退化，但在有偏先验下退化明显：系统性偏差地图的表现约为无先验飞行的一半；计算实验表明该方案可扩展，并在解性能上与单路径场景具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为不稳定环境下搜救智能规划的强决策指南，但需重视先验地图偏差对性能的显著影响。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the stochastic and real-time constraints of Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) path planning in Search and Rescue (SAR) missions. We introduce a configurable data-driven and intelligent routing architecture that combines a closed-loop probability update with the Random-Key Optimizers (RKO). Our problem is mathematically modeled as a dynamic probabilistic variant of the Open Vehicle Routing Problem (OVRP), enabling the solver to switch adaptively between two objectives, thereby balancing probability maximization and resource minimization. The framework degrades elegantly under noisy priors but not under biased ones: a systematically biased map performs about half as well as flying with no prior at all. Computational tests show that the proposed scheme is scalable and competitive with single-path scenarios in terms of solution performance and serves as a strong decision guide for intelligent planning in SAR operations in unstable environments

</details>
